FILE: partitions.csv
--------------------------------------------------------------
# Name,       Type, SubType, Offset,  Size,   Flags
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# NVS Partition: Stores Wi-Fi credentials, system settings, and secure AES encryption keys (Non-Volatile Storage)
nvs,       data, nvs,     ,        0x4000, 

# OTA Data Partition: Acts as the "traffic cop" recording which firmware slot (A or B) is currently active
boot_meta,    data, ota,     ,        0x2000, 

# PHY Init Partition: Stores hardware radio calibration data for Wi-Fi and Bluetooth performance
phy_init,     data, phy,     ,        0x1000, 

# Firmware Slot A (4MB): Primary code storage slot where your application runs or receives updates
firmware_a,   app,  ota_0,   ,        4M,     

# Firmware Slot B (4MB): Secondary code storage slot used for safe background OTA network updates
firmware_b,   app,  ota_1,   ,        4M,     

# File Storage Partition (4MB): Dedicated internal storage (using SPIFFS/LittleFS) for files, configs, and logs
my_files,     data, spiffs,  ,        4M,

FILE: main/CMakeLists.txt
--------------------------------------------------------------

file(GLOB_RECURSE SOURCES
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/*.c"
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/*.cpp"
)

# 2. Extract unique directories from all discovered source files
set(INCLUDE_DIRS "")
foreach(source_file ${SOURCES})
    get_filename_component(dir_path ${source_file} DIRECTORY)
    list(APPEND INCLUDE_DIRS ${dir_path})
endforeach()
list(REMOVE_DUPLICATES INCLUDE_DIRS)

# Layer roots. These let files use stable prefixes ("adapters/...", "wifi/...",
# "general/...") regardless of the directory a source file currently lives in.
list(APPEND INCLUDE_DIRS
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/general"
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/esp_hardware")

# 3. Register the IDF component
idf_component_register(
    SRCS ${SOURCES}
    INCLUDE_DIRS ${INCLUDE_DIRS}
    REQUIRES 
      #  arduino-audio-tools
        esp_wifi
        fatfs
        nvs_flash
        esp_event
        esp_driver_i2s
        mbedtls#encryption
        app_update# this is for firmwire switch
        esp_http_server # for wifi privision
        esp_wifi_manager
        esp_bus  
        opus
        proto
)

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/app_context.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#include "app_context.hpp"

namespace app {

AppContext::AppContext()
    : services_{
          logger, clock, random, crypto, device, storage, files, link, tasks,
          firmware, audio_in, audio_encoder, media_player, indicator, buttons,
          speech, playlist, *this} {}

std::unique_ptr<core::ITransport> AppContext::create(const char* host, const char* port,
                                                     uint32_t conv_id) {
    return std::make_unique<adapters::KcpUdpTransport>(host, port, conv_id);
}

void AppContext::startMedia() {
    if (media) {
        return;
    }

    media = std::make_unique<core::app::MediaController>(
        media_player, indicator, buttons, speech, playlist, logger);

    core::app::MediaConfig config;
    if (!media->start(config)) {
        logger.logf(core::LogLevel::Error, "Media controller failed to start");
    }
}

} // namespace app

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/app_context.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

// Composition root: owns every concrete adapter and the media controller.
// This is a plain class created in main.cpp and shared as core::IAppServices;
// there is no global singleton accessor.

#include <memory>

#include "core/app/media_controller.hpp"
#include "interface.hpp"

#include "esp_hardware/adapters/audio/EspI2sAudioSource.hpp"
#include "esp_hardware/adapters/audio/EspMediaPlayer.hpp"
#include "esp_hardware/adapters/audio/OpusEncoder.hpp"
#include "esp_hardware/adapters/crypto/EspCrypto.hpp"
#include "esp_hardware/adapters/device/EspDeviceInfo.hpp"
#include "esp_hardware/adapters/input/EspButtonInput.hpp"
#include "esp_hardware/adapters/input/EspSpeechInput.hpp"
#include "esp_hardware/adapters/link/EspWifiLink.hpp"
#include "esp_hardware/adapters/ota/EspFirmwareUpdater.hpp"
#include "esp_hardware/adapters/platform/EspPlatform.hpp"
#include "esp_hardware/adapters/platform/EspTaskRunner.hpp"
#include "esp_hardware/adapters/storage/EspFileStore.hpp"
#include "esp_hardware/adapters/storage/EspNvsStorage.hpp"
#include "esp_hardware/adapters/storage/EspSdPlaylist.hpp"
#include "esp_hardware/adapters/transport/KcpUdpTransport.hpp"
#include "esp_hardware/adapters/ui/EspIndicator.hpp"

namespace app {

class AppContext : public core::ITransportFactory {
public:
    AppContext();

    // Port bundle handed to core/app. No singleton: the owner passes this.
    core::IAppServices& services() { return services_; }

    void startMedia();

    // core::ITransportFactory
    std::unique_ptr<core::ITransport> create(const char* host, const char* port,
                                             uint32_t conv_id) override;

    core::AudioConfig audio_config{};

    adapters::EspClock           clock;
    adapters::EspRandom          random;
    adapters::EspLogger          logger;
    adapters::EspCrypto          crypto;
    adapters::EspDeviceInfo      device;
    adapters::EspNvsStorage      storage{"storage"};
    adapters::EspFileStore       files;
    adapters::EspWifiLink        link;
    adapters::EspTaskRunner      tasks;
    adapters::EspFirmwareUpdater firmware;

    adapters::EspI2sAudioSource audio_in{audio_config};
    adapters::OpusEncoder       audio_encoder{audio_config};

    adapters::EspMediaPlayer media_player;
    adapters::EspIndicator   indicator;
    adapters::EspButtonInput buttons;
    adapters::EspSpeechInput speech;
    adapters::EspSdPlaylist  playlist;

    std::unique_ptr<core::app::MediaController> media;

private:
    core::IAppServices services_;
};

} // namespace app

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/core/app/audio_service.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
// AudioService: capture -> encode -> send, composed from injected ports.

#include "core/app/audio_service.hpp"

#include <atomic>
#include <cstdint>
#include <mutex>

#include "core/app/net_manager.hpp"
#include "core/audio/AudioPipeline.hpp"
#include "interface.hpp"

#include "audio_protocol.hpp"      // protocol::IoT::AudioPacket / encodeAudio
#include "turn_on_audio.hpp"       // protocol::IoT::client::encodeRecordAck

namespace AudioService {

namespace {

constexpr uint32_t kLoopDelayMs   = 10;
constexpr uint32_t kTaskStack     = 39152;
constexpr int      kTaskPriority  = 5;
constexpr int      kTaskCore      = 1;
constexpr int      kStopTimeoutMs = 500;

struct AudioState {
    // Guards task-handle publication vs. the task clearing it on another core.
    std::mutex mutex;
    std::atomic<core::ITaskRunner::TaskId> task{nullptr};
    std::atomic<bool> running{false};
    std::atomic<uint32_t> request_id{0};   // RecordControl.requestId
};

AudioState& state() {
    static AudioState s;
    return s;
}

core::IAppServices*& services() {
    static core::IAppServices* s = nullptr;
    return s;
}

void send_ack(core::IAppServices& svc, protocol::IoT::RecordCommand cmd,
              uint32_t request_id, bool ok, const char* error = "") {
    Network::send(protocol::IoT::client::encodeRecordAck(
        cmd, svc.clock.nowUnixMs(), request_id, ok, error));
}

void clear_task_handle() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(state().mutex);
    state().task.store(nullptr);
}

void audio_task(void* /*arg*/) {
    core::IAppServices& svc = *services();
    const uint32_t request_id = state().request_id.load();

    svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Info, "Audio task created & initializing");

    if (!svc.audio_in.open()) {
        svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Error, "Audio input open failed");
        send_ack(svc, protocol::IoT::RecordCommand::START, request_id, false,
                 "audio input open failed");
        clear_task_handle();
        svc.tasks.exitCurrentTask();
        return;
    }

    if (!svc.audio_encoder.init()) {
        svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Error, "Audio encoder init failed");
        svc.audio_in.close();   // release the driver + PSRAM DMA buffer
        send_ack(svc, protocol::IoT::RecordCommand::START, request_id, false,
                 "opus encoder init failed");
        clear_task_handle();
        svc.tasks.exitCurrentTask();
        return;
    }

    // Pipeline is really running: only now report START success to the server.
    send_ack(svc, protocol::IoT::RecordCommand::START, request_id, true);

    core::AudioPipeline pipeline(
        svc.audio_in, svc.audio_encoder, svc.clock, svc.log,
        [&svc](const core::Bytes& compressed, uint32_t timestamp_ms) {
            protocol::IoT::AudioPacket packet;
            packet.timestampMs = static_cast<int64_t>(timestamp_ms);
            packet.durationMs = svc.audio_encoder.frameDurationMs();
            packet.payload = compressed;
            packet.codec = 1;   // 1 = Opus
            packet.sampleRate = svc.audio_encoder.sampleRate();
            packet.channels = svc.audio_encoder.channelCount();
            Network::send(protocol::IoT::client::encodeAudio(packet));
        });

    while (state().running.load()) {
        // The I2S read inside step() blocks ~1 frame and yields, so only delay
        // on failure; otherwise the loop would fall behind the 20 ms frame rate.
        if (!pipeline.step()) {
            svc.tasks.delayMs(kLoopDelayMs);
        }
    }

    svc.audio_in.close();
    svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Info, "Exiting audio loop: releasing drivers");
    clear_task_handle();
    svc.tasks.exitCurrentTask();
}

} // namespace

void start(core::IAppServices& svc, uint32_t request_id) {
    services() = &svc;
    auto& s = state();

    // Hold the lock across publish+store: a fast-exiting task cannot erase the
    // handle before start() stores it (cross-core startup race).
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(s.mutex);
    if (s.task.load() != nullptr) {
        svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Warn, "Audio already running; acking START");
        send_ack(svc, protocol::IoT::RecordCommand::START, request_id, true);
        return;
    }

    s.request_id.store(request_id);
    s.running.store(true);
    s.task.store(svc.tasks.start("audio_proc_task", kTaskStack, kTaskPriority, kTaskCore,
                                 audio_task, nullptr));

    if (s.task.load() == nullptr) {
        svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Error, "Failed to create audio task!");
        s.running.store(false);
        send_ack(svc, protocol::IoT::RecordCommand::START, request_id, false,
                 "task create failed");
        return;
    }
    // START ack is sent by audio_task once open()/init() actually succeeded.
}

void stop(uint32_t request_id) {
    if (services() == nullptr) {
        return;
    }
    core::IAppServices& svc = *services();
    auto& s = state();

    if (s.task.load() == nullptr) {
        svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Warn, "Audio already stopped; acking STOP");
        send_ack(svc, protocol::IoT::RecordCommand::STOP, request_id, true);
        return;
    }

    svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Info, "Stopping audio task...");
    s.running.store(false);

    int waited = 0;
    while (s.task.load() != nullptr && waited < kStopTimeoutMs) {
        svc.tasks.delayMs(kLoopDelayMs);
        waited += kLoopDelayMs;
    }

    if (s.task.load() != nullptr) {
        // Never vTaskDelete a task that may hold NetState::mutex or heap locks.
        svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Error,
                     "Audio task did not exit within %d ms; leaving it to finish",
                     kStopTimeoutMs);
        return;
    }

    svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Info, "Audio task stopped");
    send_ack(svc, protocol::IoT::RecordCommand::STOP, request_id, true);
}

bool is_running() {
    auto& s = state();
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(s.mutex);
    return s.task.load() != nullptr;
}

} // namespace AudioService

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

namespace core { struct IAppServices; }

namespace AudioService {

    // Starts the capture/encode task. The START ack is sent only after the
    // hardware/codec are actually open (ok=false on failure).
    void start(core::IAppServices& services, uint32_t request_id = 0);

    // Stops the task and releases the audio hardware, then acks the request.
    void stop(uint32_t request_id = 0);

    bool is_running();

} // namespace AudioService

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/core/app/media_controller.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#include "core/app/media_controller.hpp"

namespace core::app {

MediaController::MediaController(core::IMediaPlayer& player,
                                 core::IIndicator& indicator,
                                 core::IButtonInput& buttons,
                                 core::ISpeechInput& speech,
                                 core::IPlaylist& playlist,
                                 core::ILogger& logger)
    : player_(player),
      indicator_(indicator),
      buttons_(buttons),
      speech_(speech),
      playlist_(playlist),
      logger_(logger) {}

bool MediaController::start(const MediaConfig& config) {
    config_ = config;

    if (!player_.init()) {
        logger_.logf(core::LogLevel::Error, "Media player init failed");
        return false;
    }

    tracks_ = playlist_.scan(config_.music_dir, config_.music_ext);
    logger_.logf(core::LogLevel::Info, "Media: %u track(s) found",
                 static_cast<unsigned>(tracks_.size()));

    indicator_.setMode(core::IndicatorMode::Idle);

    if (!buttons_.start(&MediaController::buttonTrampoline, this)) {
        logger_.logf(core::LogLevel::Warn, "Button input unavailable");
    }
    if (!speech_.start(&MediaController::speechTrampoline, this)) {
        logger_.logf(core::LogLevel::Warn, "Speech input unavailable");
    }
    return true;
}

void MediaController::buttonTrampoline(core::ButtonId id, core::ButtonEvent event, void* ctx) {
    if (auto* self = static_cast<MediaController*>(ctx)) {
        self->onButton(id, event);
    }
}

void MediaController::speechTrampoline(core::SpeechEvent event, int command, void* ctx) {
    if (auto* self = static_cast<MediaController*>(ctx)) {
        self->onSpeech(event, command);
    }
}

void MediaController::onButton(core::ButtonId id, core::ButtonEvent event) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    perform(actionFor(id, event));
}

core::app::MediaAction MediaController::actionFor(core::ButtonId id,
                                                  core::ButtonEvent event) const {
    for (const auto& binding : config_.bindings) {
        if (binding.id == id) {
            return (event == core::ButtonEvent::ShortPress) ? binding.short_action
                                                            : binding.long_action;
        }
    }
    return MediaAction::None;
}

void MediaController::perform(MediaAction action) {
    switch (action) {
        case MediaAction::VolumeUp:
            changeVolume(+static_cast<int>(config_.volume_step));
            break;
        case MediaAction::VolumeDown:
            changeVolume(-static_cast<int>(config_.volume_step));
            break;
        case MediaAction::TogglePlayPause:
            resume_after_voice_ = false;
            togglePlayPause();
            break;
        case MediaAction::Stop:
            resume_after_voice_ = false;
            player_.stop();
            indicator_.setMode(core::IndicatorMode::Idle);
            break;
        case MediaAction::NextTrack:
            resume_after_voice_ = false;
            if (!tracks_.empty()) {
                index_ = (index_ + 1) % tracks_.size();
                playCurrent();
            }
            break;
        case MediaAction::PreviousTrack:
            resume_after_voice_ = false;
            if (!tracks_.empty()) {
                index_ = (index_ + tracks_.size() - 1) % tracks_.size();
                playCurrent();
            }
            break;
        case MediaAction::None:
            break;
    }
}

void MediaController::togglePlayPause() {
    switch (player_.state()) {
        case core::MediaState::Running:
            player_.pause();
            indicator_.setMode(core::IndicatorMode::Idle);
            break;
        case core::MediaState::Paused:
            player_.resume();
            indicator_.setMode(core::IndicatorMode::Playing);
            break;
        case core::MediaState::Stopped:
        case core::MediaState::None:
            playCurrent();
            break;
        case core::MediaState::Finished:
            playCurrent();   // replay the finished track instead of sticking on Idle
            break;
        case core::MediaState::Error:
            break;
    }
}

void MediaController::playCurrent() {
    if (tracks_.empty()) {
        logger_.logf(core::LogLevel::Warn, "Media: no tracks to play");
        return;
    }

    const std::string url = config_.url_prefix + tracks_[index_];
    if (!player_.play(url)) {
        logger_.logf(core::LogLevel::Error, "Media: failed to play %s", url.c_str());
        return;
    }

    indicator_.setMode(core::IndicatorMode::Playing);
    logger_.logf(core::LogLevel::Info, "Media: playing %s", url.c_str());
}

void MediaController::changeVolume(int delta) {
    int next = static_cast<int>(player_.volume()) + delta;
    if (next < 0) {
        next = 0;
    }
    if (next > static_cast<int>(config_.max_volume)) {
        next = config_.max_volume;
    }
    player_.setVolume(static_cast<uint8_t>(next));
}

void MediaController::onSpeech(core::SpeechEvent event, int command) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);
    switch (event) {
        case core::SpeechEvent::Awake:
            if (player_.state() == core::MediaState::Running) {
                player_.pause();
                resume_after_voice_ = true;
            }
            indicator_.setMode(core::IndicatorMode::VoiceCommand);
            break;
        case core::SpeechEvent::Command:
            switch (command) {
                case 0: indicator_.setColor(core::IndicatorColor::Red); break;
                case 1: indicator_.setColor(core::IndicatorColor::Blue); break;
                case 2: indicator_.setColor(core::IndicatorColor::Green); break;
                case 3: indicator_.setColor(core::IndicatorColor::White); break;
                default: break;
            }
            break;
        case core::SpeechEvent::CommandTimeout:
            if (resume_after_voice_) {
                player_.resume();
                indicator_.setMode(core::IndicatorMode::Playing);
                resume_after_voice_ = false;
            } else {
                indicator_.setMode(core::IndicatorMode::Idle);
            }
            break;
    }
}

} // namespace core::app

FILE: main/core/app/media_controller.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

// Application media behaviour: playlist navigation, volume, play/pause/stop and
// voice-command feedback. Pure C++ built only on core ports.

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <mutex>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include "interface.hpp"

namespace core::app {

enum class MediaAction {
    None,
    VolumeUp,
    VolumeDown,
    TogglePlayPause,
    Stop,
    NextTrack,
    PreviousTrack,
};

struct ButtonBinding {
    core::ButtonId id;
    MediaAction    short_action;
    MediaAction    long_action;
};

struct MediaConfig {
    std::string url_prefix = "file://sdcard/";
    std::string music_dir  = "/sdcard";
    std::string music_ext  = ".mp3";

    uint8_t max_volume  = 100;
    uint8_t volume_step = 10;

    // Board key layout: which physical key does what.
    ButtonBinding bindings[3] = {
        {core::ButtonId::Previous,  MediaAction::VolumeUp,        MediaAction::PreviousTrack},
        {core::ButtonId::PlayPause, MediaAction::TogglePlayPause, MediaAction::Stop},
        {core::ButtonId::Next,      MediaAction::VolumeDown,      MediaAction::NextTrack},
    };
};

class MediaController {
public:
    MediaController(core::IMediaPlayer& player,
                    core::IIndicator& indicator,
                    core::IButtonInput& buttons,
                    core::ISpeechInput& speech,
                    core::IPlaylist& playlist,
                    core::ILogger& logger);

    bool start(const MediaConfig& config);

private:
    static void buttonTrampoline(core::ButtonId id, core::ButtonEvent event, void* ctx);
    static void speechTrampoline(core::SpeechEvent event, int command, void* ctx);

    void onButton(core::ButtonId id, core::ButtonEvent event);
    void onSpeech(core::SpeechEvent event, int command);
    void perform(MediaAction action);
    void playCurrent();
    void togglePlayPause();
    void changeVolume(int delta);
    MediaAction actionFor(core::ButtonId id, core::ButtonEvent event) const;

    core::IMediaPlayer& player_;
    core::IIndicator&   indicator_;
    core::IButtonInput& buttons_;
    core::ISpeechInput& speech_;
    core::IPlaylist&    playlist_;
    core::ILogger&      logger_;

    MediaConfig config_;
    std::vector<std::string> tracks_;
    std::size_t index_ = 0;
    bool resume_after_voice_ = false;

    // Serializes button (key task) and speech (detect task) entry points.
    std::mutex mutex_;
};

} // namespace core::app

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/core/app/net_manager.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
// Network module: worker task + SessionManager wiring from injected services.
// Thread-safety: every session/transport access is guarded by NetState::mutex.
// The worker holds the mutex only around session->tick() (never across the
// delay), and close_connection() never force-deletes the worker (killing a
// task that may hold the mutex would leave it locked forever).

#include "core/app/net_manager.hpp"

#include <atomic>
#include <memory>
#include <mutex>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

#include "core/app/session_factory.hpp"
#include "interface.hpp"

namespace Network {

namespace {

constexpr uint32_t kWorkerTickMs     = 10;
constexpr uint32_t kWorkerStackBytes = 8192;
constexpr int      kWorkerPriority   = 5;
constexpr int      kWorkerCore       = 1;
constexpr int      kWorkerExitWaitMs = 2000;

struct NetState {
    std::mutex mutex;

    std::unique_ptr<core::ITransport>     transport;
    std::unique_ptr<core::SessionManager> session;

    std::atomic<core::ITaskRunner::TaskId> handle{nullptr};
    std::atomic<bool>                      running{false};
};

NetState& net() {
    static NetState s;
    return s;
}

core::IAppServices*& services() {
    static core::IAppServices* s = nullptr;
    return s;
}

void network_worker_task(void* /*arg*/) {
    core::IAppServices& svc = *services();
    auto& st = net();

    svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Info, "Network worker task started");
    while (st.running.load()) {
        {
            // Lock only around tick(); never across the delay below.
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(st.mutex);
            if (st.session) {
                st.session->tick();
            }
        }
        svc.tasks.delayMs(kWorkerTickMs);
    }

    {
        // Clear the handle under the mutex: start() publishes it under the same
        // lock, so a fast-exiting task cannot erase the handle start() is about
        // to store on the other core.
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(st.mutex);
        st.handle.store(nullptr);
    }
    svc.tasks.exitCurrentTask();
}

} // namespace

void init(const char* host, const char* port, core::IAppServices& svc) {
    services() = &svc;
    auto& st = net();

    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(st.mutex);
    if (st.running.load()) {
        svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Warn, "Network already initialized");
        return;
    }

    st.transport = svc.transport_factory.create(
        host, port, core::app::nextConversationId(svc.random));
    if (!st.transport) {
        svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Error, "Failed to create transport");
        return;
    }

    st.session = core::app::makeSession(*st.transport, svc.link, svc.clock,
                                        svc.random, svc.crypto, svc.log);
    if (!st.session) {
        svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Error, "Failed to create session");
        st.transport.reset();
        return;
    }
    st.session->start();

    st.running.store(true);
    st.handle.store(svc.tasks.start("net_worker_io", kWorkerStackBytes,
                                    kWorkerPriority, kWorkerCore,
                                    network_worker_task, nullptr));
    if (st.handle.load() == nullptr) {
        svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Error, "Failed to create network worker task");
        st.running.store(false);
        st.session->stop();
        st.session.reset();
        st.transport.reset();
        return;
    }

    svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Info, "Network manager initialized");
}

void close_connection() {
    auto& st = net();
    core::IAppServices* svc = services();

    // 1. Ask the worker to exit WITHOUT holding the mutex.
    st.running.store(false);

    // 2. Wait for the worker to leave its loop. tick() only performs
    // non-blocking I/O and fast crypto, so it cannot block indefinitely.
    int waited_ms = 0;
    while (st.handle.load() != nullptr && waited_ms < kWorkerExitWaitMs) {
        if (svc) {
            svc->tasks.delayMs(10);
        }
        waited_ms += 10;
    }

    if (st.handle.load() != nullptr) {
        // Never force-delete here: the worker might hold NetState::mutex and
        // vTaskDelete would leave it locked forever.
        if (svc) {
            svc->log.logf(core::LogLevel::Error,
                          "Network worker did not exit; skipping session teardown");
        }
        return;
    }

    // 3. Worker is gone: teardown is now race-free.
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(st.mutex);
        if (st.session) {
            st.session->stop();
            st.session.reset();
        }
        st.transport.reset();
    }

    if (svc) {
        svc->log.logf(core::LogLevel::Info, "Network connection closed");
    }
}

int send(const std::vector<uint8_t>& data) {
    auto& st = net();
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(st.mutex);
    return st.session ? st.session->send(data) : -1;
}

std::vector<uint8_t> poll_and_receive() {
    auto& st = net();
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(st.mutex);
    if (!st.session) {
        return {};
    }
    auto msg = st.session->poll();
    return msg.has_value() ? std::move(*msg) : std::vector<uint8_t>{};
}

bool is_established() {
    auto& st = net();
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(st.mutex);
    return st.session && st.session->isEstablished();
}

uint32_t session_epoch() {
    auto& st = net();
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(st.mutex);
    return st.session ? st.session->epoch() : 0;
}

} // namespace Network

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/core/app/net_manager.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <vector>

namespace core { struct IAppServices; }

namespace Network {

    // Builds the transport/session from the injected services and starts the
    // network worker task.
    void init(const char* host, const char* port, core::IAppServices& services);
    void close_connection();

    // Data Flow APIs (Thread-safe, multi-producer compatible)
    int send(const std::vector<uint8_t>& data);
    std::vector<uint8_t> poll_and_receive();

    // Session status for the application loop (locked internally).
    bool is_established();
    uint32_t session_epoch();

} // namespace Network

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/core/app/prog.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
// Application: device registration + server message dispatch.
// Receives core::IAppServices from the platform layer; no hardware/root includes.

#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include "core/app/audio_service.hpp"
#include "core/app/net_manager.hpp"
#include "core/app/prog.hpp"

#include "IoT_identity_token.hpp"   // protocol::IoT::client device-query encode/decode
#include "interface.hpp"
#include "turn_on_audio.hpp"        // protocol::IoT record-control encode/decode

namespace {

constexpr uint32_t kLoopDelayMs = 10;
constexpr uint32_t kKeepaliveMs = 3000;  // 3 s: >=2 round-trips of margin vs the 8 s timeout
constexpr const char* kHost = "172.30.165.156";
constexpr const char* kPort = "8080";

void handle_incoming(const core::Bytes& packet, core::IAppServices& svc,
                     bool& firmware_marked) {
    // 1. Record start/stop commands from the server.
    if (protocol::IoT::client::isRecordControl(packet)) {
        protocol::IoT::RecordControlInfo info;
        if (!protocol::IoT::client::decodeRecordControl(packet, info)) {
            svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Warn, "Failed to decode valid RecordControl payload.");
            return;
        }

        if (info.command == protocol::IoT::RecordCommand::START) {
            svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Info,
                         "Record START (requestId=%u, ts=%lld)",
                         static_cast<unsigned>(info.requestId),
                         static_cast<long long>(info.timestampMs));
            // Always pass requestId and always ack (including already-running).
            AudioService::start(svc, info.requestId);
        } else if (info.command == protocol::IoT::RecordCommand::STOP) {
            svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Info,
                         "Record STOP (requestId=%u)",
                         static_cast<unsigned>(info.requestId));
            // Always ack (including already-stopped).
            AudioService::stop(info.requestId);
        }
        return;
    }

    // 2. Device-query acknowledgement.
    if (protocol::IoT::client::isDeviceQueryResponse(packet)) {
        protocol::IoT::BuisDeviceQueryResponse response;
        if (!protocol::IoT::client::decodeDeviceQueryResponse(packet, response)) {
            svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Warn, "Failed to decode BuisDeviceQueryResponse payload.");
            return;
        }
        if (response.status == protocol::IoT::Status_DeviceQuery::SUCCESS) {
            svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Info, "Device query acknowledged: %s",
                         response.message.c_str());

            // The device reached the server and works: cancel OTA rollback
            // exactly once.
            if (!firmware_marked && svc.firmware.markAppValid()) {
                firmware_marked = true;
                svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Info, "Firmware marked valid");
            }
        } else if (response.status == protocol::IoT::Status_DeviceQuery::AUTH_REQUIRED) {
            svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Warn,
                         "Device query requires authentication (challenge flow not implemented yet)");
        } else {
            svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Warn, "Device query rejected (status %u): %s",
                         static_cast<unsigned>(response.status), response.message.c_str());
        }
        return;
    }

    svc.log.logf(core::LogLevel::Info, "Received general server message or unhandled packet type.");
}

} // namespace

void app_run_loop(void* arg) {
    auto* svc = static_cast<core::IAppServices*>(arg);
    if (svc == nullptr) {
        return;
    }

    if (!svc->files.init()) {
        svc->log.logf(core::LogLevel::Warn, "Filesystem mount failed");
    }
    svc->firmware.init();
    svc->link.start();                 // WiFi provisioning / association
    Network::init(kHost, kPort, *svc);

    const std::string device_id = svc->device.deviceId();
    const std::vector<uint8_t> registration =
        protocol::IoT::client::encodeDeviceQuery(device_id);

    // Registration is sent on (re)connect transitions and then as a 3 s
    // keepalive; it is never spammed from the fast loop.
    bool registered = false;
    bool firmware_marked = false;
    uint32_t last_epoch = 0;
    uint32_t last_registration_ms = 0;

    while (true) {
        // Reconnect detection via the session epoch: every reset bumps the
        // epoch, so a fast Established->reset->Established cycle is never missed.
        const uint32_t epoch = Network::session_epoch();
        if (epoch != last_epoch) {
            last_epoch = epoch;
            registered = false;

            // A new server session must not inherit a running capture: the
            // server has to send a fresh START command after every reconnect.
            if (AudioService::is_running()) {
                AudioService::stop();
                svc->log.logf(core::LogLevel::Info, "Audio stopped after session reset");
            }
        }

        const bool established = Network::is_established();
        if (established &&
            (!registered ||
             (svc->clock.nowMs() - last_registration_ms) >= kKeepaliveMs)) {
            Network::send(registration);
            registered = true;
            last_registration_ms = svc->clock.nowMs();
        }

        // Drain everything the session produced since the last tick.
        core::Bytes incoming;
        while (!(incoming = Network::poll_and_receive()).empty()) {
            handle_incoming(incoming, *svc, firmware_marked);
        }

        svc->tasks.delayMs(kLoopDelayMs);   // yield to keep the task watchdog happy
    }

    svc->tasks.exitCurrentTask();           // unreachable; documents task lifecycle
}

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/core/audio/AudioPipeline.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#include "core/audio/AudioPipeline.hpp"

#include <cstdio>
#include <utility>

namespace core {

AudioPipeline::AudioPipeline(IAudioSource& source, IAudioEncoder& encoder, IClock& clock,
                             ILogger& logger, Sender sender)
    : source_(source),
      encoder_(encoder),
      clock_(clock),
      logger_(logger),
      sender_(std::move(sender)) {}

void AudioPipeline::reset() {
    frame_count_ = 0;
}

bool AudioPipeline::step() {
    if (!source_.readFrame(pcm_)) {
        return false;
    }

    Bytes compressed;
    if (!encoder_.encode(pcm_, compressed)) {
        char buf[96];
        std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "Encode failed (frame %u)",
                      static_cast<unsigned>(frame_count_));
        logger_.log(LogLevel::Error, buf);
        return false;
    }

    // Stream-relative time from frame progress: the first packet is exactly 0
    // (protocol: "0 = starts a new recording"), immune to scheduling jitter.
    const uint32_t timestamp_ms = frame_count_ * encoder_.frameDurationMs();
    if (sender_) {
        sender_(compressed, timestamp_ms);
    }
    ++frame_count_;
    return true;
}

} // namespace core

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/core/audio/AudioPipeline.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

// Domain core: capture -> encode -> emit. Pure C++ over the audio ports.
// The application decides what to do with the compressed frame (e.g. wrap it
// in the IoT protocol and queue it for the session transport).

#include "interface.hpp"

#include <functional>
#include <vector>

namespace core {

class AudioPipeline {
public:
    // Called once per encoded frame. timestampMs is stream-relative (0-based).
    using Sender = std::function<void(const Bytes& compressed, uint32_t timestampMs)>;

    AudioPipeline(IAudioSource& source, IAudioEncoder& encoder, IClock& clock,
                  ILogger& logger, Sender sender);

    // One capture -> encode -> emit cycle. Returns false when no frame was sent.
    bool step();

    // Restart the stream-relative timestamp (e.g. on a new recording).
    void reset();

private:
    IAudioSource&  source_;
    IAudioEncoder& encoder_;
    IClock&        clock_;
    ILogger&       logger_;
    Sender         sender_;

    std::vector<int16_t> pcm_;
    uint32_t frame_count_ = 0;
};

} // namespace core

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/core/session/SessionManager.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#include "core/session/SessionManager.hpp"

#include <cstdarg>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include <utility>

namespace core {
namespace {

constexpr uint32_t kHandshakeBaseRetryMs = 600;   // first handshake retry delay
constexpr uint32_t kHandshakeMaxRetryMs  = 3000;  // handshake retry ceiling
constexpr uint32_t kServerTimeoutMs      = 8000;  // no RX at all => server dead
constexpr uint32_t kHandshakeTimeoutMs   = 30000; // WaitAck ceiling -> fresh keypair
constexpr uint32_t kReopenThrottleMs     = 1000;  // retry transport.open() slowly
constexpr size_t   kMaxOutboundQueue     = 128;   // backpressure threshold

} // namespace

SessionManager::SessionManager(ITransport& transport, ILink& link, IClock& clock,
                               IRandom& random, ICrypto& crypto, ILogger& logger)
    : transport_(transport),
      link_(link),
      clock_(clock),
      random_(random),
      crypto_(crypto),
      logger_(logger) {}

SessionManager::~SessionManager() { stop(); }

void SessionManager::logf(LogLevel level, const char* fmt, ...) {
    char buf[192];
    va_list ap;
    va_start(ap, fmt);
    vsnprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), fmt, ap);
    va_end(ap);
    logger_.log(level, buf);
}

void SessionManager::start() {
    state_ = State::Idle;
    epoch_ = 0;
    aes_key_.clear();
    handshake_pkt_.clear();
    retry_count_ = 0;
    last_attempt_ms_ = 0;
    waitack_start_ms_ = 0;
    last_open_attempt_ms_ = 0;

    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(tx_mutex_);
        incoming_.clear();
        sending_.clear();
    }
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(rx_mutex_);
        std::queue<Bytes> empty;
        std::swap(rx_queue_, empty);
    }

    if (link_.isUp() && !transport_.open()) {
        logf(LogLevel::Error, "Transport open failed; will keep retrying");
    }
}

void SessionManager::stop() {
    transport_.close();
    state_ = State::Idle;
}

int SessionManager::send(const Bytes& app_message) {
    if (app_message.empty()) {
        return 0;
    }
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(tx_mutex_);
    if (incoming_.size() >= kMaxOutboundQueue) {
        return -1; // backpressure: caller should drop instead of buffering
    }
    incoming_.push_back(app_message);
    return 0;
}

std::optional<Bytes> SessionManager::poll() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(rx_mutex_);
    if (rx_queue_.empty()) {
        return std::nullopt;
    }
    Bytes out = std::move(rx_queue_.front());
    rx_queue_.pop();
    return out;
}

void SessionManager::tick() {
    const uint32_t now = clock_.nowMs();

    // 0. Ensure the transport is open (retry, but throttled). Only attempt
    // when the link is up: if kHost is a hostname, getaddrinfo() must not
    // stall this worker while WiFi is down.
    if (!transport_.isOpen() && link_.isUp()) {
        if (last_open_attempt_ms_ == 0 || (now - last_open_attempt_ms_) >= kReopenThrottleMs) {
            last_open_attempt_ms_ = now;
            if (transport_.open()) {
                logf(LogLevel::Info, "Transport opened");
            }
        }
    }
    bool transport_ok = transport_.isOpen();

    if (transport_ok) {
        // 1. Drive transport timers (retransmissions, acks, ...).
        transport_.tick(now);

        // 1b. KCP dead_link: retransmission limit exhausted -> reconnect early
        // instead of waiting for the full server-timeout window.
        if (state_ != State::Idle && transport_.isDead()) {
            resetSession("dead link");
            transport_ok = transport_.isOpen();   // reopen may have failed
        }

        if (transport_ok) {
            // 2. Handshake / reconnect state machine.
            handleHandshake(now);

            // 3. Inbound processing: drain ALL reassembled messages this tick
            // so bursts cannot pile up between ticks.
            while (auto msg = transport_.receive(now)) {
                handleIncoming(std::move(*msg), now);
            }
        }

        // 4. Server liveness: no datagram at all for kServerTimeoutMs.
        if (state_ == State::Established) {
            const uint32_t last_rx = transport_.lastRxMs();
            // Guard the subtraction: last_rx can be newer than the tick's
            // `now` if it was sampled later (uint32 underflow => bogus reset).
            if (last_rx != 0 && now > last_rx && (now - last_rx) > kServerTimeoutMs) {
                logf(LogLevel::Warn, "Server silent for %u ms; reconnecting",
                     static_cast<unsigned>(now - last_rx));
                resetSession("server timeout");
            }
        }
    }

    // 5. Swap producer buffer into the worker buffer.
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(tx_mutex_);
        if (!incoming_.empty()) {
            sending_.swap(incoming_);
        }
    }

    // 6. Encrypt & transmit (or drop when offline).
    if (!sending_.empty()) {
        const bool online = transport_ok && link_.isUp() && state_ == State::Established;
        if (online) {
            for (const auto& pkt : sending_) {
                auto encrypted = crypto_.encrypt(pkt, aes_key_);
                if (!encrypted.has_value()) {
                    logf(LogLevel::Error, "Encrypt failed; dropping packet");
                    continue;
                }
                if (transport_.sendReliable(*encrypted) < 0) {
                    logf(LogLevel::Warn, "Transport send saturated; dropping packet");
                }
            }
        } else {
            logf(LogLevel::Warn, "Dropped %u packet(s): session not established",
                 static_cast<unsigned>(sending_.size()));
        }
        sending_.clear();
    }
}

void SessionManager::handleHandshake(uint32_t now_ms) {
    if (!link_.isUp()) {
        if (state_ != State::Idle) {
            resetSession("link down");
        }
        return;
    }

    switch (state_) {
    case State::Idle: {
        auto pub = crypto_.generatePublicKey();
        if (!pub.has_value() || pub->empty()) {
            logf(LogLevel::Error, "ECDH key generation failed");
            return;
        }
        const uint32_t conv = transport_.conversationId();
        handshake_pkt_.resize(sizeof(uint32_t) + pub->size());
        std::memcpy(handshake_pkt_.data(), &conv, sizeof(uint32_t));
        std::memcpy(handshake_pkt_.data() + sizeof(uint32_t), pub->data(), pub->size());
        if (transport_.sendRaw(handshake_pkt_) < 0) {
            logf(LogLevel::Warn, "Handshake send failed; staying in Idle");
            return;
        }
        last_attempt_ms_ = now_ms;
        retry_count_ = 1;
        state_ = State::WaitAck;
        waitack_start_ms_ = now_ms;
        logf(LogLevel::Info, "Handshake dispatched (conv=%u)", static_cast<unsigned>(conv));
        break;
    }
    case State::WaitAck: {
        if ((now_ms - waitack_start_ms_) > kHandshakeTimeoutMs) {
            logf(LogLevel::Warn, "Handshake timed out; restarting with a fresh keypair");
            resetSession("handshake timeout");
            return;
        }
        uint32_t interval = retry_count_ * kHandshakeBaseRetryMs;
        if (interval > kHandshakeMaxRetryMs) {
            interval = kHandshakeMaxRetryMs;
        }
        if ((now_ms - last_attempt_ms_) >= interval) {
            retry_count_++;
            transport_.sendRaw(handshake_pkt_);
            last_attempt_ms_ = now_ms;
            logf(LogLevel::Warn, "Handshake ACK timeout; retry #%u",
                 static_cast<unsigned>(retry_count_));
        }
        break;
    }
    case State::Established:
        break; // liveness handled in tick()
    }
}

void SessionManager::handleIncoming(Bytes message, uint32_t now_ms) {
    (void)now_ms;
    if (state_ == State::WaitAck) {
        auto secret = crypto_.computeSharedSecret(message);
        if (secret.has_value() && !secret->empty()) {
            aes_key_ = std::move(*secret);
            handshake_pkt_.clear();
            retry_count_ = 0;
            state_ = State::Established;
            logf(LogLevel::Info, "Handshake complete; AES-256-GCM key established");
        } else {
            logf(LogLevel::Error, "Corrupt handshake response");
        }
        return;
    }
    if (state_ == State::Established) {
        auto decrypted = crypto_.decrypt(message, aes_key_);
        if (!decrypted.has_value()) {
            logf(LogLevel::Error, "Failed to decrypt incoming packet");
            return;
        }
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(rx_mutex_);
        rx_queue_.push(std::move(*decrypted));
    }
}

void SessionManager::resetSession(const char* reason) {
    logf(LogLevel::Warn, "Session reset (%s)", reason);
    state_ = State::Idle;
    aes_key_.clear();
    handshake_pkt_.clear();
    retry_count_ = 0;
    last_attempt_ms_ = 0;
    waitack_start_ms_ = 0;
    if (link_.isUp()) {
        // Recreate the transport with a FRESH conversation id: reusing the old
        // conv would desync KCP sequence numbers if the peer is still alive.
        transport_.reset(random_.u32());
    } else {
        // Link is down: no socket/DNS work now; tick() reopens once isUp().
        transport_.close();
    }
    epoch_++;
    last_open_attempt_ms_ = clock_.nowMs();
}

} // namespace core

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/core/session/SessionManager.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

// Domain core: server session state machine (handshake, liveness, reconnect,
// encryption, outbound queue). Pure C++: it only knows the ports, never
// ESP-IDF / FreeRTOS / KCP / WiFi. This makes it unit-testable on the host.

#include "interface.hpp"

#include <mutex>
#include <queue>
#include <vector>

namespace core {

class SessionManager {
public:
    SessionManager(ITransport& transport, ILink& link, IClock& clock,
                   IRandom& random, ICrypto& crypto, ILogger& logger);
    ~SessionManager();

    SessionManager(const SessionManager&) = delete;
    SessionManager& operator=(const SessionManager&) = delete;

    // Reset state and open the transport. Call once before the worker starts.
    void start();
    // Close the transport and stop the state machine.
    void stop();

    // Drive one iteration of the state machine (call every ~10 ms).
    void tick();

    // Enqueue an application message (bounded). Returns -1 on backpressure.
    int send(const Bytes& app_message);

    // Dequeue one decrypted application message, if any.
    std::optional<Bytes> poll();

    bool isEstablished() const { return state_ == State::Established; }

    // Monotonic counter bumped on every session reset. Applications use it to
    // detect (re)connection transitions even inside one tick interval.
    uint32_t epoch() const { return epoch_; }

private:
    enum class State { Idle, WaitAck, Established };

    void handleHandshake(uint32_t now_ms);
    void handleIncoming(Bytes message, uint32_t now_ms);
    void resetSession(const char* reason);
    void logf(LogLevel level, const char* fmt, ...);

    ITransport& transport_;
    ILink&      link_;
    IClock&     clock_;
    IRandom&    random_;
    ICrypto&    crypto_;
    ILogger&    logger_;

    State    state_ = State::Idle;
    uint32_t epoch_ = 0;
    Bytes    aes_key_;
    Bytes    handshake_pkt_;
    uint32_t retry_count_ = 0;
    uint32_t last_attempt_ms_ = 0;
    uint32_t waitack_start_ms_ = 0;
    uint32_t last_open_attempt_ms_ = 0;

    // Outbound double buffer: producers append to incoming_, worker swaps.
    std::mutex         tx_mutex_;
    std::vector<Bytes> incoming_;
    std::vector<Bytes> sending_;

    // Decrypted inbound messages for the application.
    std::mutex        rx_mutex_;
    std::queue<Bytes> rx_queue_;
};

} // namespace core

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/adapters/audio/EspI2sAudioSource.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

// Adapter: microphone capture through the I2S hardware driver.
// The driver is created on open() so constructing the adapter (in AppContext)
// does not touch the hardware until recording actually starts.

#include "interface.hpp"

#include <memory>
#include <vector>

#include "audio_capture_driver.hpp"

namespace adapters {

class EspI2sAudioSource : public core::IAudioSource {
public:
    explicit EspI2sAudioSource(const core::AudioConfig& config) : config_(config) {}

    bool open() override {
        if (!driver_) {
            driver_ = std::make_unique<Hardware::AudioCaptureDriver>(config_);
            if (!driver_->isReady()) {
                driver_.reset();   // PSRAM allocation failed
                return false;
            }
        }
        return true;
    }

    void close() override { driver_.reset(); }

    bool readFrame(std::vector<int16_t>& pcm) override {
        if (!driver_) {
            return false;
        }
        if (driver_->readSamples() != ESP_OK) {
            return false;
        }
        pcm = driver_->getFrameBuffer();
        return !pcm.empty();
    }

private:
    core::AudioConfig config_;
    std::unique_ptr<Hardware::AudioCaptureDriver> driver_;
};

} // namespace adapters


--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/adapters/audio/EspMediaPlayer.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

// Adapter: core::IMediaPlayer over the ESP-ADF simple player.

#include "interface.hpp"

#include "audio_driver.h"

namespace adapters {

class EspMediaPlayer : public core::IMediaPlayer {
public:
    bool init() override {
        Audio_Play_Init();
        return true;
    }

    bool play(const std::string& url) override {
        return Audio_Play_Music(url.c_str()) == ESP_GMF_ERR_OK;
    }

    void stop() override { Audio_Stop_Play(); }
    void pause() override { Audio_Pause_Play(); }
    void resume() override { Audio_Resume_Play(); }

    core::MediaState state() const override {
        switch (Audio_Get_Current_State()) {
            case ESP_ASP_STATE_RUNNING:  return core::MediaState::Running;
            case ESP_ASP_STATE_PAUSED:   return core::MediaState::Paused;
            case ESP_ASP_STATE_STOPPED:  return core::MediaState::Stopped;
            case ESP_ASP_STATE_FINISHED: return core::MediaState::Finished;
            case ESP_ASP_STATE_ERROR:    return core::MediaState::Error;
            default:                     return core::MediaState::None;
        }
    }

    uint8_t volume() const override { return get_audio_volume(); }
    void setVolume(uint8_t volume) override { Volume_Adjustment(volume); }
};

} // namespace adapters

#pragma once

// Adapter: Opus encoder bound to the project's audio arguments.

#include "interface.hpp"

#include <vector>

#include "Opus.hpp"

namespace adapters {

class OpusEncoder : public core::IAudioEncoder {
public:
    explicit OpusEncoder(const core::AudioConfig& config) : config_(config) {}

    bool init() override {
        return encoder_.init(static_cast<int>(config_.sample_rate),
                             static_cast<int>(config_.channel_count),
                             static_cast<int>(config_.bits_per_chan),
                             static_cast<int>(config_.target_frame_duration_ms));
    }

    bool encode(const std::vector<int16_t>& pcm, core::Bytes& out) override {
        auto encoded = encoder_.encode(pcm.data());
        if (encoded.empty()) {
            return false;
        }
        out.assign(encoded.begin(), encoded.end());
        return true;
    }

    uint32_t frameDurationMs() const override {
        return config_.target_frame_duration_ms;
    }

    uint32_t sampleRate() const override { return config_.sample_rate; }
    uint32_t channelCount() const override { return config_.channel_count; }

private:
    core::AudioConfig config_;
    Opus encoder_;
};

} // namespace adapters

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/adapters/crypto/EspCrypto.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

// Adapter: core::ICrypto over the portable PSA crypto module.
// The thin platform wrapper (PSA init + RNG) is implemented here; all protocol
// logic (AES-GCM layout, ECDH handshake) stays in general/crypto.

#include "interface.hpp"

#include "crypto.hpp"

namespace adapters {

class EspCryptoBackend : public encryption::CryptoBackend {
public:
    bool init() override {
        static bool initialized = false;
        if (!initialized) {
            if (psa_crypto_init() != PSA_SUCCESS) {
                return false;
            }
            initialized = true;
        }
        return true;
    }

    void fillRandom(uint8_t* output, std::size_t len) override {
        psa_generate_random(output, len);
    }
};

class EspCrypto : public core::ICrypto {
public:
    std::optional<core::Bytes> generatePublicKey() override {
        return ecdh_.generatePublicKey(backend_);
    }

    std::optional<core::Bytes> computeSharedSecret(const core::Bytes& peer_public_key) override {
        return ecdh_.computeSharedSecret(peer_public_key, backend_);
    }

    std::optional<core::Bytes> encrypt(const core::Bytes& plaintext,
                                       const core::Bytes& key) override {
        return encryption::aes_gcm_encrypt(plaintext, key, backend_);
    }

    std::optional<core::Bytes> decrypt(const core::Bytes& packet,
                                       const core::Bytes& key) override {
        return encryption::aes_gcm_decrypt(packet, key, backend_);
    }

private:
    EspCryptoBackend       backend_;
    encryption::EcdhHandshake ecdh_;
};

} // namespace adapters

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/adapters/device/EspDeviceInfo.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

// Adapter: hardware identity for the ESP-IDF platform (IDeviceInfo port).
// MAC/chip discovery stays here; string formatting lives in general/util/format.

#include "interface.hpp"

#include "esp_mac.h"

#include <string>

#include "general/util/format.hpp"

namespace adapters {

class EspDeviceInfo : public core::IDeviceInfo {
public:
    std::string mac() const override {
        uint8_t m[6] = {};
        if (esp_read_mac(m, ESP_MAC_WIFI_STA) != ESP_OK) {
            return {};
        }
        return general::util::formatMac(m);
    }

    std::string chip() const override {
#ifdef CONFIG_IDF_TARGET
        return CONFIG_IDF_TARGET;   // e.g. "esp32s3"
#else
        return "unknown";
#endif
    }

    std::string deviceId() const override {
        uint8_t m[6] = {};
        if (esp_read_mac(m, ESP_MAC_WIFI_STA) != ESP_OK) {
            return "unknown";
        }
        return general::util::formatDeviceId(chip(), m);
    }
};

} // namespace adapters


--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/adapters/input/EspButtonInput.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

// Adapter: core::IButtonInput over the board key driver.
// The debounce/long-press decision is made by general/input/ButtonStateMachine;
// this adapter only maps physical keys/events to the core enums.

#include "interface.hpp"

#include "button_driver.h"

namespace adapters {

class EspButtonInput;

inline void espKeyHandler(key_id_t id, key_event_t event, void* user_data);

class EspButtonInput : public core::IButtonInput {
public:
    bool start(Handler handler, void* ctx) override {
        handler_ = handler;
        ctx_ = ctx;

        // Register the callback first, then start the scan task (the task can
        // deliver events from the very first scan).
        if (key_register_callback(&espKeyHandler) != ESP_OK) {
            return false;
        }
        // The driver forwards this pointer back as the callback's user_data.
        return key_module_init(this) == ESP_OK;
    }

    void handle(key_id_t id, key_event_t event) {
        if (handler_ == nullptr) {
            return;
        }

        core::ButtonId button;
        switch (id) {
            case KEY_ID_9:  button = core::ButtonId::Previous; break;
            case KEY_ID_10: button = core::ButtonId::PlayPause; break;
            case KEY_ID_11: button = core::ButtonId::Next; break;
            default: return;
        }

        const core::ButtonEvent ev = (event == KEY_EVENT_LONG_PRESS)
            ? core::ButtonEvent::LongPress
            : core::ButtonEvent::ShortPress;
        handler_(button, ev, ctx_);
    }

private:
    Handler handler_ = nullptr;
    void* ctx_ = nullptr;
};

inline void espKeyHandler(key_id_t id, key_event_t event, void* user_data) {
    auto* self = static_cast<EspButtonInput*>(user_data);
    if (self != nullptr) {
        self->handle(id, event);
    }
}

} // namespace adapters

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/adapters/input/EspSpeechInput.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

// Adapter: core::ISpeechInput over the ESP-SR callback.
// The speech driver invokes its callback with user_data == NULL, so the adapter
// keeps a singleton pointer to the active instance.

#include "interface.hpp"

#include "mic_speech.h"

namespace adapters {

class EspSpeechInput;

inline void espSpeechHandler(esp_sr_rec_event_t event, esp_sr_evt_data_t data,
                             void* user_data);

class EspSpeechInput : public core::ISpeechInput {
public:
    bool start(Handler handler, void* ctx) override {
        handler_ = handler;
        ctx_ = ctx;
        instance() = this;
        return Speech_register_callback(&espSpeechHandler) == ESP_OK;
    }

    void handle(esp_sr_rec_event_t event, esp_sr_evt_data_t data) {
        if (handler_ == nullptr) {
            return;
        }

        switch (event) {
            case ESP_SR_EVT_AWAKEN:
                handler_(core::SpeechEvent::Awake, 0, ctx_);
                break;
            case ESP_SR_EVT_CMD:
                handler_(core::SpeechEvent::Command, static_cast<int>(data.sr_cmd), ctx_);
                break;
            case ESP_SR_EVT_CMD_TIMEOUT:
                handler_(core::SpeechEvent::CommandTimeout, 0, ctx_);
                break;
            default:
                break;
        }
    }

    static EspSpeechInput*& instance() {
        static EspSpeechInput* s_instance = nullptr;
        return s_instance;
    }

private:
    Handler handler_ = nullptr;
    void* ctx_ = nullptr;
};

inline void espSpeechHandler(esp_sr_rec_event_t event, esp_sr_evt_data_t data,
                             void* /*user_data*/) {
    auto* self = EspSpeechInput::instance();
    if (self != nullptr) {
        self->handle(event, data);
    }
}

} // namespace adapters

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/adapters/link/EspWifiLink.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

// Adapter: WiFi connectivity through the existing WiFi manager (ILink port).
// "Up" means associated AND holding a real IP (DHCP completed), so the session
// handshake cannot start before the stack is actually usable.
// Swap this for a Cellular4GLink / EthernetLink later without touching the core.

#include "interface.hpp"

#include <atomic>
#include <cstring>

#include "esp_netif_sntp.h"
#include "esp_timer.h"
#include "WiFiManager.hpp"
#include "wifi/shared.hpp"

namespace adapters {

class EspWifiLink : public core::ILink {
public:
    bool start() override {
        // No RTC: once WiFi comes up, sync wall-clock time so nowUnixMs() is
        // real instead of 1970 (RecordAck timestamps depend on it).
        static bool sntp_started = false;
        if (!sntp_started) {
            esp_sntp_config_t config = ESP_NETIF_SNTP_DEFAULT_CONFIG("pool.ntp.org");
            sntp_started = (esp_netif_sntp_init(&config) == ESP_OK);
        }
        wifi::init();
        return true;
    }

    bool isUp() const override {
        // wifi_manager_get_status() is not free; cache the answer briefly.
        const uint32_t now = static_cast<uint32_t>(esp_timer_get_time() / 1000ULL);
        const uint32_t last = last_check_ms_.load();
        if (last != 0 && (now - last) < kCacheMs) {
            return last_state_.load();
        }

        wifi_status_t status{};
        const bool up = wifi::isConnected() &&
                        wifi::getStatus(status) &&
                        status.ip[0] != '\0' &&
                        std::strcmp(status.ip, "0.0.0.0") != 0;
        last_state_.store(up);
        last_check_ms_.store(now);
        return up;
    }

private:
    static constexpr uint32_t kCacheMs = 500;
    mutable std::atomic<uint32_t> last_check_ms_{0};
    mutable std::atomic<bool>     last_state_{false};
};

} // namespace adapters

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/adapters/platform/EspPlatform.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

// ESP-IDF implementations of the platform ports (IClock / IRandom / ILogger).

#include "interface.hpp"

#include <sys/time.h>

#include "esp_log.h"
#include "esp_random.h"
#include "esp_timer.h"

namespace adapters {

class EspClock : public core::IClock {
public:
    uint32_t nowMs() const override {
        return static_cast<uint32_t>(esp_timer_get_time() / 1000ULL);
    }

    int64_t nowUnixMs() const override {
        struct timeval tv;
        gettimeofday(&tv, nullptr);
        return static_cast<int64_t>(tv.tv_sec) * 1000LL + tv.tv_usec / 1000LL;
    }
};

class EspRandom : public core::IRandom {
public:
    uint32_t u32() override { return static_cast<uint32_t>(esp_random()); }
};

class EspLogger : public core::ILogger {
public:
    void log(core::LogLevel level, const char* msg) override {
        switch (level) {
            case core::LogLevel::Info:  ESP_LOGI("CORE", "%s", msg); break;
            case core::LogLevel::Warn:  ESP_LOGW("CORE", "%s", msg); break;
            case core::LogLevel::Error: ESP_LOGE("CORE", "%s", msg); break;
        }
    }
};

} // namespace adapters


--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/adapters/platform/EspTaskRunner.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

// Adapter: core::ITaskRunner on top of FreeRTOS tasks.

#include "interface.hpp"

#include "freertos/FreeRTOS.h"
#include "freertos/task.h"

namespace adapters {

class EspTaskRunner : public core::ITaskRunner {
public:
    TaskId start(const char* name, uint32_t stack_bytes, int priority, int core,
                 void (*fn)(void*), void* arg) override {
        TaskHandle_t handle = nullptr;
        const BaseType_t created =
            (core < 0)
                ? xTaskCreate(fn, name, stack_bytes, arg, priority, &handle)
                : xTaskCreatePinnedToCore(fn, name, stack_bytes, arg, priority, &handle, core);
        if (created != pdPASS) {
            return nullptr;
        }
        return static_cast<TaskId>(handle);
    }

    void stop(TaskId task) override {
        if (task != nullptr) {
            vTaskDelete(static_cast<TaskHandle_t>(task));
        }
    }

    void delayMs(uint32_t ms) override { vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(ms)); }

    void exitCurrentTask() override { vTaskDelete(nullptr); }
};

} // namespace adapters

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/adapters/storage/EspNvsStorage.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

// Adapter: ESP-IDF NVS implementation of the core::IStorage port.

#include "interface.hpp"

#include <string>
#include <utility>

#include "nvs.h"
#include "nvs_flash.h"

namespace adapters {

class EspNvsStorage : public core::IStorage {
public:
    explicit EspNvsStorage(std::string namespace_name)
        : namespace_(std::move(namespace_name)) {
        // Idempotent: safe when the WiFi manager (or anyone else) initialized NVS
        // first. Handles the two states that require a full erase.
        esp_err_t err = nvs_flash_init();
        if (err == ESP_ERR_NVS_NO_FREE_PAGES || err == ESP_ERR_NVS_NEW_VERSION_FOUND) {
            nvs_flash_erase();
            nvs_flash_init();
        }
    }

    bool set(const std::string& key, const core::Bytes& value) override {
        nvs_handle_t h;
        if (nvs_open(namespace_.c_str(), NVS_READWRITE, &h) != ESP_OK) {
            return false;
        }
        bool ok = (nvs_set_blob(h, key.c_str(), value.data(), value.size()) == ESP_OK);
        ok = ok && (nvs_commit(h) == ESP_OK);
        nvs_close(h);
        return ok;
    }

    bool get(const std::string& key, core::Bytes& out) override {
        nvs_handle_t h;
        if (nvs_open(namespace_.c_str(), NVS_READONLY, &h) != ESP_OK) {
            return false;
        }

        size_t len = 0;
        esp_err_t err = nvs_get_blob(h, key.c_str(), nullptr, &len);
        if (err != ESP_OK) {
            nvs_close(h);
            return false;
        }

        out.resize(len);
        err = nvs_get_blob(h, key.c_str(), out.data(), &len);
        nvs_close(h);

        if (err != ESP_OK) {
            out.clear();
            return false;
        }
        out.resize(len);
        return true;
    }

    bool remove(const std::string& key) override {
        nvs_handle_t h;
        if (nvs_open(namespace_.c_str(), NVS_READWRITE, &h) != ESP_OK) {
            return false;
        }
        bool ok = (nvs_erase_key(h, key.c_str()) == ESP_OK);
        ok = ok && (nvs_commit(h) == ESP_OK);
        nvs_close(h);
        return ok;
    }

    bool exists(const std::string& key) override {
        nvs_handle_t h;
        if (nvs_open(namespace_.c_str(), NVS_READONLY, &h) != ESP_OK) {
            return false;
        }
        size_t len = 0;
        esp_err_t err = nvs_get_blob(h, key.c_str(), nullptr, &len);
        nvs_close(h);
        return err == ESP_OK;
    }

private:
    std::string namespace_;
};

} // namespace adapters

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/adapters/transport/KcpUdpTransport.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#include "adapters/transport/KcpUdpTransport.hpp"

#include "esp_hardware/net/esp_udp_datagram_io.hpp"

namespace adapters {

KcpUdpTransport::KcpUdpTransport(const char* host, const char* port, uint32_t conv_id)
    : host_(host ? host : ""), port_(port ? port : ""), conv_id_(conv_id) {}

KcpUdpTransport::~KcpUdpTransport() { close(); }

bool KcpUdpTransport::open() {
    if (kcp_ && kcp_->isOpen()) {
        return true;
    }

    io_ = std::make_unique<EspUdpDatagramIo>(host_.c_str(), port_.c_str());
    if (!io_->isOpen()) {
        io_.reset();
        return false;
    }

    general::kcp::KcpConfig config;
    config.conversation_id = conv_id_;
    kcp_ = std::make_unique<general::kcp::KcpSession>(*io_, config);
    if (!kcp_->open()) {
        kcp_.reset();
        io_.reset();
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

void KcpUdpTransport::close() {
    if (kcp_) {
        kcp_->close();
        kcp_.reset();
    }
    io_.reset();
}

bool KcpUdpTransport::isOpen() const {
    return kcp_ && kcp_->isOpen();
}

void KcpUdpTransport::reset(uint32_t new_conv_id) {
    // Recreate socket + KCP state with a fresh conversation id so the stream
    // restarts cleanly even if the old peer session is still alive.
    conv_id_ = new_conv_id;
    close();
    open();
}

void KcpUdpTransport::tick(uint32_t now_ms) {
    if (kcp_) {
        kcp_->update(now_ms);
    }
}

int KcpUdpTransport::sendReliable(const core::Bytes& data) {
    if (!kcp_ || data.empty()) {
        return -1;
    }
    return kcp_->send(data.data(), data.size());
}

int KcpUdpTransport::sendRaw(const core::Bytes& data) {
    if (!io_ || data.empty()) {
        return -1;
    }
    return io_->send(data.data(), data.size());
}

std::optional<core::Bytes> KcpUdpTransport::receive(uint32_t now_ms) {
    if (!kcp_) {
        return std::nullopt;
    }
    auto message = kcp_->poll(now_ms);
    if (message.empty()) {
        return std::nullopt;
    }
    return message;
}

uint32_t KcpUdpTransport::lastRxMs() const {
    return kcp_ ? kcp_->lastRxMs() : 0;
}

} // namespace adapters

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/adapters/transport/KcpUdpTransport.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

// Adapter: implements core::ITransport on top of the portable KCP session and
// the ESP UDP datagram channel. KCP logic lives in general/kcp/KcpSession.

#include "interface.hpp"

#include <memory>

namespace adapters {

class KcpUdpTransport : public core::ITransport {

    ~KcpUdpTransport() override;

    bool open() override;
    void close() override;
    bool isOpen() const override;
    void reset(uint32_t new_conv_id) override;
    void tick(uint32_t now_ms) override;

    int sendRaw(const core::Bytes& data) override;

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/drivers/audio_capture/audio_capture_driver.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#include "audio_capture_driver.hpp"

#include "esp_heap_caps.h"
#include "esp_log.h"
#include "bsp_board.h"
#include "driver/i2s_std.h"
#include "portmacro.h"

#include "general/audio/frame_math.hpp"

static const char* TAG = "AudioCaptureDriver";

namespace Hardware {

AudioCaptureDriver::AudioCaptureDriver(const core::AudioConfig& config)
    : config_(config) {
    channel_count_ = config_.channel_count;
    chunk_size_ = general::audio::samplesPerFrame(config_.sample_rate,
                                                  config_.target_frame_duration_ms);

    const std::size_t buffer_bytes = general::audio::bytesPerFrame(
        config_.sample_rate, config_.target_frame_duration_ms,
        config_.channel_count, config_.bits_per_chan);

    raw_dma_buffer_ = static_cast<int16_t*>(
        heap_caps_malloc(buffer_bytes, MALLOC_CAP_SPIRAM | MALLOC_CAP_8BIT));

    if (raw_dma_buffer_ == nullptr) {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Failed to allocate %zu bytes in PSRAM for audio buffer", buffer_bytes);
    } else {
        ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Audio Hardware Capture initialized (%zu channels, %zu samples/channel, %lu ms duration)",
                 channel_count_, chunk_size_,
                 static_cast<unsigned long>(config_.target_frame_duration_ms));
    }
}

AudioCaptureDriver::~AudioCaptureDriver() {
    freeBuffer();
}

AudioCaptureDriver::AudioCaptureDriver(AudioCaptureDriver&& other) noexcept
    : config_(other.config_),
      channel_count_(other.channel_count_),
      chunk_size_(other.chunk_size_),
      raw_dma_buffer_(other.raw_dma_buffer_) {
    other.raw_dma_buffer_ = nullptr;
    other.channel_count_ = 0;
    other.chunk_size_ = 0;
}

AudioCaptureDriver& AudioCaptureDriver::operator=(AudioCaptureDriver&& other) noexcept {
    if (this != &other) {
        freeBuffer();
        config_ = other.config_;
        channel_count_ = other.channel_count_;
        chunk_size_ = other.chunk_size_;
        raw_dma_buffer_ = other.raw_dma_buffer_;
        other.raw_dma_buffer_ = nullptr;
        other.channel_count_ = 0;
        other.chunk_size_ = 0;
    }
    return *this;
}

void AudioCaptureDriver::freeBuffer() noexcept {
    if (raw_dma_buffer_ != nullptr) {
        heap_caps_free(raw_dma_buffer_);
        raw_dma_buffer_ = nullptr;
    }
}

esp_err_t AudioCaptureDriver::readSamples() {
    if (raw_dma_buffer_ == nullptr) {
        return ESP_ERR_NO_MEM;
    }

    const std::size_t buffer_bytes = general::audio::bytesPerFrame(
        config_.sample_rate, config_.target_frame_duration_ms,
        config_.channel_count, config_.bits_per_chan);
    size_t bytes_read = 0;

    esp_err_t err = i2s_channel_read(bsp_i2s_get_rx_handle(), raw_dma_buffer_,
                                     buffer_bytes, &bytes_read,
                                     /*timeout_ms=*/100);  // < AudioService stop window
    if (err != ESP_OK || bytes_read == 0) {
        ESP_LOGW(TAG, "Failed to read PCM data directly from I2S");
        return ESP_FAIL;
    }

    if (bytes_read != buffer_bytes) {
        ESP_LOGW(TAG, "Short I2S read: %u / %u bytes",
                 static_cast<unsigned>(bytes_read),
                 static_cast<unsigned>(buffer_bytes));
        return ESP_FAIL;
    }

    return ESP_OK;
}

std::vector<int16_t> AudioCaptureDriver::getFrameBuffer() const {
    if (raw_dma_buffer_ == nullptr) {
        return {};
    }

    const std::size_t total_samples = chunk_size_ * channel_count_;

    int factor = 1;
    if (config_.bits_per_chan == 32) {
        factor = 2;
    }

    return std::vector<int16_t>(raw_dma_buffer_, raw_dma_buffer_ + total_samples * factor);
}

} // namespace Hardware

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/drivers/audio_capture/audio_capture_driver.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
 #pragma once

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <vector>

#include "esp_err.h"
#include "interface.hpp"

namespace Hardware {

class AudioCaptureDriver {
public:
    explicit AudioCaptureDriver(const core::AudioConfig& config);
    ~AudioCaptureDriver();

    AudioCaptureDriver(const AudioCaptureDriver&) = delete;
    AudioCaptureDriver& operator=(const AudioCaptureDriver&) = delete;

    AudioCaptureDriver(AudioCaptureDriver&& other) noexcept;
    AudioCaptureDriver& operator=(AudioCaptureDriver&& other) noexcept;

    [[nodiscard]] esp_err_t readSamples();
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<int16_t> getFrameBuffer() const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t channelCount() const noexcept { return channel_count_; }
    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t chunkSize() const noexcept { return chunk_size_; }

    // True when the PSRAM DMA buffer was allocated successfully.
    [[nodiscard]] bool isReady() const noexcept { return raw_dma_buffer_ != nullptr; }

private:
    void freeBuffer() noexcept;

    core::AudioConfig config_{};
    std::size_t channel_count_{0};
    std::size_t chunk_size_{0};
    int16_t* raw_dma_buffer_{nullptr};
};

} // namespace Hardware

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/drivers/audio_play/audio_driver.c
--------------------------------------------------------------
#include "audio_driver.h"

#include "tca9555_driver.h"
#include "bsp_board.h"

static const char *TAG = "audio play";


static uint8_t Volume = PLAYER_VOLUME;
static esp_asp_handle_t handle = NULL;

static void Audio_PA_EN(void)
{
    Set_EXIO(IO_EXPANDER_PIN_NUM_8,true);
    vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(10));
}
static void Audio_PA_DIS(void)
{
    Set_EXIO(IO_EXPANDER_PIN_NUM_8,false);
    vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(10));
}

static int out_data_callback(uint8_t *data, int data_size, void *ctx)
{
    esp_audio_play((int16_t*)data,data_size,500 / portTICK_PERIOD_MS);
    return 0;
}

static int in_data_callback(uint8_t *data, int data_size, void *ctx)
{
    int ret = fread(data, 1, data_size, ctx);
    ESP_LOGD(TAG, "%s-%d,rd size:%d", __func__, __LINE__, ret);
    return ret;
}

static int mock_event_callback(esp_asp_event_pkt_t *event, void *ctx)
{
    if (event->type == ESP_ASP_EVENT_TYPE_MUSIC_INFO) 
    {
        esp_asp_music_info_t info = {0};
        memcpy(&info, event->payload, event->payload_size);
        ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Get info, rate:%d, channels:%d, bits:%d ,bitrate = %d", info.sample_rate, info.channels, info.bits,info.bitrate);
    } 
    else if (event->type == ESP_ASP_EVENT_TYPE_STATE) 
    {
        esp_asp_state_t st = 0;
        memcpy(&st, event->payload, event->payload_size);

        ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Get State, %d,%s", st, esp_audio_simple_player_state_to_str(st));
        /*if(st == ESP_ASP_STATE_FINISHED)
        {

        }*/
    }
    return 0;
}


static void pipeline_init(void)
{
    esp_log_level_set("*", ESP_LOG_INFO);

    esp_asp_cfg_t cfg = {
        .in.cb = NULL,
        .in.user_ctx = NULL,
        .out.cb = out_data_callback,
        .out.user_ctx = NULL,
    };

    esp_gmf_err_t err = esp_audio_simple_player_new(&cfg, &handle);
    if (err != ESP_GMF_ERR_OK || handle == NULL) {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "esp_audio_simple_player_new failed: %d", err);
        return;
    }
    err = esp_audio_simple_player_set_event(handle, mock_event_callback, NULL);
    if (err != ESP_GMF_ERR_OK) {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "esp_audio_simple_player_set_event failed: %d", err);
    }
}


esp_gmf_err_t Audio_Play_Music(const char* url)
{
    if (handle == NULL) return ESP_GMF_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    esp_audio_simple_player_stop(handle);
    esp_gmf_err_t err = esp_audio_simple_player_run(handle, url, NULL);
    if (err == ESP_GMF_ERR_OK) {
        Audio_PA_EN();
    } else {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "esp_audio_simple_player_run failed: %d", err);
    }
    return err;
}

esp_gmf_err_t Audio_Stop_Play(void)
{
    if (handle == NULL) return ESP_GMF_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    esp_gmf_err_t err = esp_audio_simple_player_stop(handle);
    Audio_PA_DIS();
    return err;
}

esp_gmf_err_t Audio_Resume_Play(void)
{
    if (handle == NULL) return ESP_GMF_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    esp_gmf_err_t err = esp_audio_simple_player_resume(handle);
    if (err == ESP_GMF_ERR_OK) {
        Audio_PA_EN();
    }
    return err;
}

esp_gmf_err_t Audio_Pause_Play(void)
{
    if (handle == NULL) return ESP_GMF_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    esp_gmf_err_t err = esp_audio_simple_player_pause(handle);
    Audio_PA_DIS();
    return err;
}

esp_asp_state_t Audio_Get_Current_State(void) 
{
    if (handle == NULL) return ESP_ASP_STATE_NONE;
    esp_asp_state_t state;
    esp_gmf_err_t err = esp_audio_simple_player_get_state(handle, &state);
    if (err != ESP_GMF_ERR_OK) {
        ESP_LOGE("AUDIO", "Get state failed: %d", err);
        return ESP_ASP_STATE_ERROR;
    }
    return state;
}


void Audio_Play_Init(void) 
{
    pipeline_init();
}

void Volume_Adjustment(uint8_t Vol)
{
    if(Vol > Volume_MAX )
    {
        printf("Audio : The volume value is incorrect. Please enter 0 to 21\r\n");
    }
    else  
    {
        esp_audio_set_play_vol(Vol);
        Volume = Vol;
    }
}

uint8_t get_audio_volume(void)
{
    return Volume;
}
