#define ADC_I2S_CHANNEL 4

static sdmmc_card_t *card;
static const char *TAG = "board";
static int s_play_sample_rate = 16000;
static int s_play_channel_format = 1;
static int s_bits_per_chan = 16;

#if ESP_IDF_VERSION >= ESP_IDF_VERSION_VAL(5, 0, 0)
static i2s_chan_handle_t            tx_handle = NULL;        // I2S tx channel handler
static i2s_chan_handle_t            rx_handle = NULL;        // I2S rx channel handler
#endif
static audio_codec_data_if_t *record_data_if  = NULL;
static audio_codec_ctrl_if_t *record_ctrl_if  = NULL;
static audio_codec_if_t *record_codec_if      = NULL;
static esp_codec_dev_handle_t record_dev      = NULL;

static audio_codec_data_if_t *play_data_if    = NULL;
static audio_codec_ctrl_if_t *play_ctrl_if    = NULL;
static audio_codec_gpio_if_t *play_gpio_if    = NULL;
static audio_codec_if_t *play_codec_if        = NULL;
static esp_codec_dev_handle_t play_dev        = NULL;
static i2c_master_bus_handle_t i2c_bus_handle = NULL;
static uint32_t SDCard_Size  = 0;

i2s_chan_handle_t bsp_i2s_get_rx_handle(void)
{
    return rx_handle;
}

static esp_err_t i2c_master_init(void)
{
    const i2c_master_bus_config_t bus_config = {
        .i2c_port = I2C_NUM,
        .sda_io_num = GPIO_I2C_SDA,
        .scl_io_num = GPIO_I2C_SCL,
        .clk_source = I2C_CLK_SRC_DEFAULT,
    };

    esp_err_t ret = i2c_new_master_bus(&bus_config, &i2c_bus_handle);
    if (ret != ESP_OK) {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Failed to initialize I2C bus: %s", esp_err_to_name(ret));
        return ret;
    }

    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "I2C bus initialized successfully");
    return ESP_OK;  // 返回 ESP_OK 表示成功
}


esp_err_t bsp_codec_adc_init(int sample_rate)
{
    esp_err_t ret_val = ESP_OK;

    // Do initialize of related interface: data_if, ctrl_if and gpio_if
    audio_codec_i2s_cfg_t i2s_cfg = {
        .port = I2S_NUM_1,
#if ESP_IDF_VERSION >= ESP_IDF_VERSION_VAL(5, 0, 0)
        .rx_handle = rx_handle,
        .tx_handle = NULL,
#endif
    };
    record_data_if = audio_codec_new_i2s_data(&i2s_cfg);

    audio_codec_i2c_cfg_t i2c_cfg = {.addr = ES7210_CODEC_DEFAULT_ADDR,.bus_handle = i2c_bus_handle};
    record_ctrl_if = audio_codec_new_i2c_ctrl(&i2c_cfg);
    // New input codec interface
    es7210_codec_cfg_t es7210_cfg = {
        .ctrl_if = record_ctrl_if,
        .mic_selected = ES7210_SEL_MIC1 | ES7210_SEL_MIC2 | ES7210_SEL_MIC3 | ES7210_SEL_MIC4,
    };  
    record_codec_if = es7210_codec_new(&es7210_cfg);
    // New input codec device
    esp_codec_dev_cfg_t dev_cfg = {
        .codec_if = record_codec_if,
        .data_if = record_data_if,
        .dev_type = ESP_CODEC_DEV_TYPE_IN,
    };
    record_dev = esp_codec_dev_new(&dev_cfg);

    esp_codec_dev_sample_info_t fs = {
        .sample_rate = 16000,
        .channel = 2,
        .bits_per_sample = 32,
    };
    esp_codec_dev_open(record_dev, &fs);
    // esp_codec_dev_set_in_gain(record_dev, RECORD_VOLUME);
    esp_codec_dev_set_in_channel_gain(record_dev, ESP_CODEC_DEV_MAKE_CHANNEL_MASK(0), RECORD_VOLUME);
    esp_codec_dev_set_in_channel_gain(record_dev, ESP_CODEC_DEV_MAKE_CHANNEL_MASK(1), RECORD_VOLUME);
    esp_codec_dev_set_in_channel_gain(record_dev, ESP_CODEC_DEV_MAKE_CHANNEL_MASK(2), RECORD_VOLUME);
    esp_codec_dev_set_in_channel_gain(record_dev, ESP_CODEC_DEV_MAKE_CHANNEL_MASK(3), RECORD_VOLUME);

    return ret_val;
}

esp_err_t bsp_codec_dac_init(int sample_rate, int channel_format, int bits_per_chan)
{
    esp_err_t ret_val = ESP_OK;

    // Do initialize of related interface: data_if, ctrl_if and gpio_if
    audio_codec_i2s_cfg_t i2s_cfg = {
        .port = I2S_NUM_1,
#if ESP_IDF_VERSION >= ESP_IDF_VERSION_VAL(5, 0, 0)
        .rx_handle = NULL,
        .tx_handle = tx_handle,
#endif
    };
    play_data_if = audio_codec_new_i2s_data(&i2s_cfg);

    audio_codec_i2c_cfg_t i2c_cfg = {.addr = ES8311_CODEC_DEFAULT_ADDR,.bus_handle = i2c_bus_handle};
    play_ctrl_if = audio_codec_new_i2c_ctrl(&i2c_cfg);
    play_gpio_if = audio_codec_new_gpio();
    // New output codec interface
    es8311_codec_cfg_t es8311_cfg = {
        .codec_mode = ESP_CODEC_DEV_WORK_MODE_DAC,
        .ctrl_if = play_ctrl_if,
        .gpio_if = play_gpio_if,
        .pa_pin = GPIO_PWR_CTRL,
        .use_mclk = false,
    };
    play_codec_if = es8311_codec_new(&es8311_cfg);
    // New output codec device
    esp_codec_dev_cfg_t dev_cfg = {
        .codec_if = play_codec_if,
        .data_if = play_data_if,
        .dev_type = ESP_CODEC_DEV_TYPE_OUT,
    };
    play_dev = esp_codec_dev_new(&dev_cfg);

    esp_codec_dev_sample_info_t fs = {
        .bits_per_sample = bits_per_chan,
        .sample_rate = sample_rate,
        .channel = channel_format,
    };
    esp_codec_dev_set_out_vol(play_dev, PLAYER_VOLUME);
    esp_codec_dev_open(play_dev, &fs);

    return ret_val;
}

static esp_err_t bsp_codec_adc_deinit()
{
    esp_err_t ret_val = ESP_OK;

    if (record_dev) {
        esp_codec_dev_close(record_dev);
        esp_codec_dev_delete(record_dev);
        record_dev = NULL;
    }

    // Delete codec interface
    if (record_codec_if) {
        audio_codec_delete_codec_if(record_codec_if);
        record_codec_if = NULL;
    }
    
    // Delete codec control interface
    if (record_ctrl_if) {
        audio_codec_delete_ctrl_if(record_ctrl_if);
        record_ctrl_if = NULL;
    }
    
    // Delete codec data interface
    if (record_data_if) {
        audio_codec_delete_data_if(record_data_if);
        record_data_if = NULL;
    }

    return ret_val;
}

static esp_err_t bsp_codec_dac_deinit()
{
    esp_err_t ret_val = ESP_OK;

    if (play_dev) {
        esp_codec_dev_close(play_dev);
        esp_codec_dev_delete(play_dev);
        play_dev = NULL;
    }

    // Delete codec interface
    if (play_codec_if) {
        audio_codec_delete_codec_if(play_codec_if);
        play_codec_if = NULL;
    }
    
    // Delete codec control interface
    if (play_ctrl_if) {
        audio_codec_delete_ctrl_if(play_ctrl_if);
        play_ctrl_if = NULL;
    }
    
    if (play_gpio_if) {
        audio_codec_delete_gpio_if(play_gpio_if);
        play_gpio_if = NULL;
    }
    
    // Delete codec data interface
    if (play_data_if) {
        audio_codec_delete_data_if(play_data_if);
        play_data_if = NULL;
    }

    return ret_val;
}

esp_err_t esp_audio_set_play_vol(int volume)
{
    if (!play_dev) {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "DAC codec init fail");
        return ESP_FAIL;
    }
    esp_codec_dev_set_out_vol(play_dev, volume);
    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t esp_audio_get_play_vol(int *volume)
{
    if (!play_dev) {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "DAC codec init fail");
        return ESP_FAIL;
    }
    esp_codec_dev_get_out_vol(play_dev, volume);
    return ESP_OK;
}

// static esp_err_t bsp_i2s_init(i2s_port_t i2s_num, uint32_t sample_rate, i2s_channel_fmt_t channel_format, i2s_bits_per_chan_t bits_per_chan)
//! emily change from     static esp_err_t bsp_i2s_init(i2s_port_t i2s_num, uint32_t sample_rate, int channel_format, int bits_per_chan)  to   static esp_err_t bsp_i2s_init(int i2s_num, uint32_t sample_rate, int channel_format, int bits_per_chan)
static esp_err_t bsp_i2s_init(int i2s_num, uint32_t sample_rate, int channel_format, int bits_per_chan)
{
    esp_err_t ret_val = ESP_OK;

#if ESP_IDF_VERSION >= ESP_IDF_VERSION_VAL(5, 0, 0)
    i2s_slot_mode_t channel_fmt = I2S_SLOT_MODE_STEREO;
    if (channel_format == 1) {
        channel_fmt = I2S_SLOT_MODE_MONO;
    } else if (channel_format == 2) {
        channel_fmt = I2S_SLOT_MODE_STEREO;
    } else {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Unable to configure channel_format %d", channel_format);
        channel_format = 1;
        channel_fmt = I2S_SLOT_MODE_MONO;
    }

    if (bits_per_chan != 16 && bits_per_chan != 32) {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Unable to configure bits_per_chan %d", bits_per_chan);
        bits_per_chan = 32;
    }

    i2s_chan_config_t chan_cfg = I2S_CHANNEL_DEFAULT_CONFIG(i2s_num, I2S_ROLE_MASTER);
    ret_val |= i2s_new_channel(&chan_cfg, &tx_handle, &rx_handle);
    i2s_std_config_t std_cfg = I2S_CONFIG_DEFAULT(sample_rate, channel_fmt, bits_per_chan);
    ret_val |= i2s_channel_init_std_mode(tx_handle, &std_cfg);
    ret_val |= i2s_channel_init_std_mode(rx_handle, &std_cfg);
    ret_val |= i2s_channel_enable(tx_handle);
    ret_val |= i2s_channel_enable(rx_handle);
#else
    i2s_channel_fmt_t channel_fmt = I2S_CHANNEL_FMT_RIGHT_LEFT;
    if (channel_format == 1) {
        channel_fmt = I2S_CHANNEL_FMT_ONLY_LEFT;
    } else if (channel_format == 2) {
        channel_fmt = I2S_CHANNEL_FMT_RIGHT_LEFT;
    } else {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Unable to configure channel_format %d", channel_format);
        channel_format = 1;
        channel_fmt = I2S_CHANNEL_FMT_ONLY_LEFT;
    }

    if (bits_per_chan != 16 && bits_per_chan != 32) {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Unable to configure bits_per_chan %d", bits_per_chan);
        bits_per_chan = 16;
    }

    i2s_config_t i2s_config = I2S_CONFIG_DEFAULT(sample_rate, channel_fmt, bits_per_chan);

    i2s_pin_config_t pin_config = {
        .bck_io_num = GPIO_I2S_SCLK,
        .ws_io_num = GPIO_I2S_LRCK,
        .data_out_num = GPIO_I2S_DOUT,
        .data_in_num = GPIO_I2S_SDIN,
        .mck_io_num = GPIO_I2S_MCLK,
    };

    ret_val |= i2s_driver_install(i2s_num, &i2s_config, 0, NULL);
    ret_val |= i2s_set_pin(i2s_num, &pin_config);
#endif

    return ret_val;
}
//! emily change from ///static esp_err_t bsp_i2s_deinit(i2s_port_t i2s_num) to // static esp_err_t bsp_i2s_deinit(int i2s_num)
static esp_err_t bsp_i2s_deinit(int i2s_num)
{
    esp_err_t ret_val = ESP_OK;

#if ESP_IDF_VERSION >= ESP_IDF_VERSION_VAL(5, 0, 0)
    if (i2s_num == I2S_NUM_1 && rx_handle) {
        ret_val |= i2s_channel_disable(rx_handle);
        ret_val |= i2s_del_channel(rx_handle);
        rx_handle = NULL;
    } else if (i2s_num == I2S_NUM_0  && tx_handle) {
        ret_val |= i2s_channel_disable(tx_handle);
        ret_val |= i2s_del_channel(tx_handle);
        tx_handle = NULL;
    }
#else
    ret_val |= i2s_stop(i2s_num);
    ret_val |= i2s_driver_uninstall(i2s_num);
#endif

    return ret_val;
}

static esp_err_t bsp_codec_init(int adc_sample_rate, int dac_sample_rate, int dac_channel_format, int dac_bits_per_chan)
{
    esp_err_t ret_val = ESP_OK;

    ret_val |= bsp_codec_adc_init(adc_sample_rate);
    ret_val |= bsp_codec_dac_init(dac_sample_rate, dac_channel_format, dac_bits_per_chan);

    return ret_val;
}

static esp_err_t bsp_codec_deinit()
{
    esp_err_t ret_val = ESP_OK;

    ret_val |= bsp_codec_adc_deinit();
    ret_val |= bsp_codec_dac_deinit();
    return ret_val;
}

esp_err_t esp_audio_play(const int16_t* data, int length, uint32_t ticks_to_wait)
{
    size_t bytes_write = 0;
    esp_err_t ret = ESP_OK;
    if (!play_dev || data == NULL || length <= 0) {
        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    }

    int out_length= length;
    int audio_time = 1;
    audio_time *= (16000 / s_play_sample_rate);
    audio_time *= (2 / s_play_channel_format);

    int *data_out = NULL;
    if (s_bits_per_chan != 32) {
        out_length = length * 2;
        data_out = malloc(out_length);
        if (data_out == NULL) {
            return ESP_ERR_NO_MEM;
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < (int)(length / sizeof(int16_t)); i++) {
            int ret = data[i];
            data_out[i] = ret << 16;
        }
    }

    int *data_out_1 = NULL;
    if (s_play_channel_format != 2 || s_play_sample_rate != 16000) {
        out_length *= audio_time;
        data_out_1 = malloc(out_length);
        if (data_out_1 == NULL) {
            if (data_out != NULL) {
                free(data_out);
            }
            return ESP_ERR_NO_MEM;
        }
        int *tmp_data = NULL;
        if (data_out != NULL) {
            tmp_data = data_out;
        } else {
            tmp_data = (int *)data;
        }

        for (int i = 0; i < (int)(out_length / (audio_time * sizeof(int))); i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < audio_time; j++) {
                data_out_1[audio_time * i + j] = tmp_data[i];
            }
        }
        if (data_out != NULL) {
            free(data_out);
            data_out = NULL;
        }
    }

    if (data_out != NULL) {
        ret = esp_codec_dev_write(play_dev, (void *)data_out, out_length);
        free(data_out);
    } else if (data_out_1 != NULL) {
        ret = esp_codec_dev_write(play_dev, (void *)data_out_1, out_length);
        free(data_out_1);
    } else {
        ret = esp_codec_dev_write(play_dev, (void *)data, length);
    }

    return ret;
}

esp_err_t esp_get_feed_data(bool is_get_raw_channel, int16_t *buffer, int buffer_len)
{
    esp_err_t ret = ESP_OK;
    size_t bytes_read;
    int audio_chunksize = buffer_len / (sizeof(int16_t) * ADC_I2S_CHANNEL);

    ret = esp_codec_dev_read(record_dev, (void *)buffer, buffer_len);
    if (!is_get_raw_channel) {
        for (int i = 0; i < audio_chunksize; i++) {
            int16_t ref = buffer[4 * i + 0];
            buffer[3 * i + 0] = buffer[4 * i + 1];
            buffer[3 * i + 1] = buffer[4 * i + 3];
            buffer[3 * i + 2] = ref;
        }
    }

    return ret;
}

int esp_get_feed_channel(void)
{
    return ADC_I2S_CHANNEL;
}

char* esp_get_input_format(void)
{
    return "RMNM";
}





esp_err_t esp_board_init(uint32_t sample_rate, int channel_format, int bits_per_chan)
{
    /*!< Initialize I2C bus, used for audio codec*/

    i2c_master_init();
    s_play_sample_rate = sample_rate;

    if (channel_format != 2 && channel_format != 1) {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Unable to configure channel_format");
        channel_format = 2;
    }
    s_play_channel_format = channel_format;

    if (bits_per_chan != 32 && bits_per_chan != 16) {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Unable to configure bits_per_chan");
        bits_per_chan = 32;
    }
    s_bits_per_chan = bits_per_chan;
// Forward the caller's AudioConfig instead of hardcoding 16000/2/32.
    bsp_i2s_init(I2S_NUM_1, (uint32_t)sample_rate, channel_format, bits_per_chan);
    // Because record and play use the same i2s.
    bsp_codec_init((int)sample_rate, (int)sample_rate, channel_format, bits_per_chan);

    /* Initialize PA */
     /*gpio_config_t  io_conf;
     memset(&io_conf, 0, sizeof(io_conf));
     io_conf.intr_type = GPIO_INTR_DISABLE;
     io_conf.mode = GPIO_MODE_OUTPUT;
     io_conf.pin_bit_mask = ((1ULL << GPIO_PWR_CTRL));
     io_conf.pull_down_en = 0;
     io_conf.pull_up_en = 0;
     gpio_config(&io_conf);
     gpio_set_level(GPIO_PWR_CTRL, 1);*/

    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t esp_sdcard_init(char *mount_point, size_t max_files)
{
    if (NULL != card) {
        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
    }

    /* Check if SD crad is supported */
    if (!FUNC_SDMMC_EN && !FUNC_SDSPI_EN) {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "SDMMC and SDSPI not supported on this board!");
        return ESP_ERR_NOT_SUPPORTED;
    }

    esp_err_t ret_val = ESP_OK;

    /**
     * @brief Options for mounting the filesystem.
     *   If format_if_mount_failed is set to true, SD card will be partitioned and
     *   formatted in case when mounting fails.
     *
     */
    esp_vfs_fat_sdmmc_mount_config_t mount_config = {
        .format_if_mount_failed = false,
        .max_files = max_files,
        .allocation_unit_size = 16 * 1024
    };

    /**
     * @brief Use settings defined above to initialize SD card and mount FAT filesystem.
     *   Note: esp_vfs_fat_sdmmc/sdspi_mount is all-in-one convenience functions.
     *   Please check its source code and implement error recovery when developing
     *   production applications.
     *
     */
    sdmmc_host_t host =
#if FUNC_SDMMC_EN
        SDMMC_HOST_DEFAULT();
#else
        SDSPI_HOST_DEFAULT();
    spi_bus_config_t bus_cfg = {
        .mosi_io_num = GPIO_SDSPI_MOSI,
        .miso_io_num = GPIO_SDSPI_MISO,
        .sclk_io_num = GPIO_SDSPI_SCLK,
        .quadwp_io_num = GPIO_NUM_NC,
        .quadhd_io_num = GPIO_NUM_NC,
        .max_transfer_sz = 4000,
    };
    ret_val = spi_bus_initialize(host.slot, &bus_cfg, SPI_DMA_CH_AUTO);
    if (ret_val != ESP_OK) {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Failed to initialize bus.");
        return ret_val;
    }
#endif

    /**
     * @brief This initializes the slot without card detect (CD) and write protect (WP) signals.
     *   Modify slot_config.gpio_cd and slot_config.gpio_wp if your board has these signals.
     *
     */
#if FUNC_SDMMC_EN
    sdmmc_slot_config_t slot_config = SDMMC_SLOT_CONFIG_DEFAULT();
#else
    sdspi_device_config_t slot_config = SDSPI_DEVICE_CONFIG_DEFAULT();
#endif

#if FUNC_SDMMC_EN
    /* Config SD data width. 0, 4 or 8. Currently for SD card, 8 bit is not supported. */
    slot_config.width = SDMMC_BUS_WIDTH;

    /**
     * @brief On chips where the GPIOs used for SD card can be configured, set them in
     *   the slot_config structure.
     *
     */
#if SOC_SDMMC_USE_GPIO_MATRIX
    slot_config.clk = GPIO_SDMMC_CLK;
    slot_config.cmd = GPIO_SDMMC_CMD;
    slot_config.d0 = GPIO_SDMMC_D0;
    slot_config.d1 = GPIO_SDMMC_D1;
    slot_config.d2 = GPIO_SDMMC_D2;
    slot_config.d3 = GPIO_SDMMC_D3;
#endif
    slot_config.cd = GPIO_SDMMC_DET;
    slot_config.flags |= SDMMC_SLOT_FLAG_INTERNAL_PULLUP;
#else
    slot_config.gpio_cs = GPIO_SDSPI_CS;
    slot_config.host_id = host.slot;
#endif
    /**
     * @brief Enable internal pullups on enabled pins. The internal pullups
     *   are insufficient however, please make sure 10k external pullups are
     *   connected on the bus. This is for debug / example purpose only.
     */

    /* get FAT filesystem on SD card registered in VFS. */
    ret_val =
#if FUNC_SDMMC_EN
        esp_vfs_fat_sdmmc_mount(mount_point, &host, &slot_config, &mount_config, &card);
#else
        esp_vfs_fat_sdspi_mount(mount_point, &host, &slot_config, &mount_config, &card);
#endif

    /* Check for SDMMC mount result. */
    if (ret_val != ESP_OK) {
        if (ret_val == ESP_FAIL) {
            ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Failed to mount filesystem. "
                     "If you want the card to be formatted, set the EXAMPLE_FORMAT_IF_MOUNT_FAILED menuconfig option.");
        } else {
            ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Failed to initialize the card (%s). "
                     "Make sure SD card lines have pull-up resistors in place.", esp_err_to_name(ret_val));
        }
        return ret_val;
    }

    /* Card has been initialized, print its properties. */
    sdmmc_card_print_info(stdout, card);
    SDCard_Size = ((uint64_t) card->csd.capacity) * card->csd.sector_size / (1024 * 1024);
    return ret_val;
}

esp_err_t esp_sdcard_deinit(char *mount_point)
{
    if (NULL == mount_point) {
        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
    }

    /* Unmount an SD card from the FAT filesystem and release resources acquired */
    esp_err_t ret_val = esp_vfs_fat_sdcard_unmount(mount_point, card);

    /* Make SD/MMC card information structure pointer NULL */
    card = NULL;

    return ret_val;
}

#define MOUNT_POINT "/sdcard"

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/drivers/button/button_driver.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#include "button_driver.h"

#include <atomic>

#include "esp_log.h"
#include "freertos/FreeRTOS.h"
#include "freertos/task.h"
#include "esp_timer.h"
#include "tca9555_driver.h"

#include "general/input/button_state_machine.hpp"

static const char *TAG = "button_driver";

// One pure state machine per key; this driver only reads GPIO and dispatches.
static general::input::ButtonStateMachine s_keys[3];
static key_callback_t s_callback = NULL;
static std::atomic<bool> s_running{false};
static std::atomic<bool> s_task_alive{false};
static void *s_user_data = NULL;

// 从掩码获取按键状态（按下返回true，根据硬件修改）
static bool get_key_state(uint32_t mask, key_id_t id) {
    // 假设：按键按下时对应位为0（低电平），未按下为1（高电平）
    return !(mask & (1ULL << id));
}

// 100ms扫描一次
static void scan_timer_ccan(void *arg) {
    (void)arg;
    while (s_running.load()) {
        uint32_t mask;
        // 一次读取所有关注的引脚（仅一次I2C操作）
        esp_err_t ret = esp_io_expander_get_level(
            io_expander,
            (1ULL << KEY_ID_9) | (1ULL << KEY_ID_10) | (1ULL << KEY_ID_11),
            &mask);
        if (ret != ESP_OK) {
            // Never return from a FreeRTOS task; keep scanning on transient
            // I2C failures (returning would abort the task).
            ESP_LOGE(TAG, "读取IO扩展芯片失败");
            vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(100));
            continue;
        }

        const uint32_t now = static_cast<uint32_t>(esp_timer_get_time() / 1000);

        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            const key_id_t key_id = static_cast<key_id_t>(KEY_ID_9 + i);
            const bool pressed = get_key_state(mask, key_id);
            const general::input::ButtonEvent event = s_keys[i].update(pressed, now);

            if (event == general::input::ButtonEvent::ShortPress && s_callback) {
                s_callback(key_id, KEY_EVENT_SHORT_PRESS, s_user_data);
            } else if (event == general::input::ButtonEvent::LongPress && s_callback) {
                s_callback(key_id, KEY_EVENT_LONG_PRESS, s_user_data);
            }
        }

        vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(100));
    }

    // Clean self-exit: drop the liveness flag first so key_module_deinit()
    // can wait on it; then self-delete.
    s_task_alive.store(false);
    vTaskDelete(NULL);
}

// 初始化
esp_err_t key_module_init(void *user_data) {
    if (s_task_alive.load()) {
        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;   // already running
    }
    s_user_data = user_data;
    s_running.store(true);
    s_task_alive.store(true);           // set before create: the task can exit instantly
    BaseType_t res = xTaskCreatePinnedToCore(scan_timer_ccan, "key_scan", 4096,
                                             NULL, 3, NULL, 0);
    if (res != pdPASS) {
        s_task_alive.store(false);
        s_running.store(false);
        return ESP_FAIL;
    }
    return ESP_OK;
}

// 注册回调
esp_err_t key_register_callback(key_callback_t callback) {
    if (!callback) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    s_callback = callback;
    return ESP_OK;
}

// 反初始化
esp_err_t key_module_deinit(void) {
    // Ask the task to leave its loop; it exits at the next 100 ms scan boundary,
    // clears its own handle and self-deletes. Wait on the handle (not a fixed
    // delay) and never kill it from outside while it may be mid-I2C.
    s_running.store(false);
    for (int i = 0; i < 50 && s_task_alive.load(); ++i) {
        vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(10));
    }
    s_callback = NULL;
    return ESP_OK;
}

FILE: main/esp_hardware/drivers/button/button_driver.h

#pragma once

#include "freertos/FreeRTOS.h"

#include "esp_err.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif


// 按键ID（对应扩展芯片引脚）
typedef enum {
    KEY_ID_9 = 9,
    KEY_ID_10 = 10,
    KEY_ID_11 = 11
} key_id_t;

// 按键事件
typedef enum {
    KEY_EVENT_SHORT_PRESS,  // 短按（按下后松开，时间<1s）
    KEY_EVENT_LONG_PRESS    // 长按（按下超过1s）
} key_event_t;

// 回调函数类型
typedef void (*key_callback_t)(key_id_t key_id, key_event_t event, void *user_data);

// 初始化按键模块
esp_err_t key_module_init(void *user_data);

// 注册回调函数
esp_err_t key_register_callback(key_callback_t callback);

// 反初始化
esp_err_t key_module_deinit(void);


#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/drivers/rgb_led/rgb_led_driver.c
--------------------------------------------------------------
#include "rgb_led_driver.h"

#include <stdio.h>
#include "esp_log.h"
#include "esp_err.h"
#include "bsp_board.h"

static const char *TAG = "rgb_led_driver";

typedef struct {
    uint8_t r;
    uint8_t g;
    uint8_t b;
} rgb_color_t;

// 3. 用定义的结构体类型创建映射数组
static const rgb_color_t color_rgb_map[RGB_COLOR_MAX] = {
    [RGB_COLOR_RED]    = {255, 0,   0},     // 红色
    [RGB_COLOR_BLUE]   = {0,   0,   255},   // 蓝色
    [RGB_COLOR_GREEN]  = {0,   255, 0},     // 绿色
    [RGB_COLOR_WHITE]  = {255, 255, 255},   // 白色
};

typedef enum {
    RGB_CMD_SET_MODE = 1,
    RGB_CMD_SET_COLOR, 
} rgb_queue_cmd_t;

typedef struct {
    rgb_queue_cmd_t cmd;     // 命令
    union {                    // 嵌套联合体：
        RGB_example_mode_t mode;    // 模式
        RGB_example_color_t color;     // 颜色
    } param;
} rgb_queue_data_t;


static led_strip_handle_t led_strip;
static QueueHandle_t g_msg_queue;
static RGB_example_color_t RGB_color = RGB_COLOR_WHITE;

led_strip_handle_t configure_led(void)
{
    // LED strip general initialization, according to your led board design
    led_strip_config_t strip_config = {
        .strip_gpio_num = LED_STRIP_GPIO_PIN, // The GPIO that connected to the LED strip's data line
        .max_leds = LED_STRIP_LED_COUNT,      // The number of LEDs in the strip,
        .led_model = LED_MODEL_WS2812,        // LED strip model
        .color_component_format = LED_STRIP_COLOR_COMPONENT_FMT_RGB, // The color order of the strip: GRB
        .flags = {
            .invert_out = false, // don't invert the output signal
        }
    };

    // LED strip backend configuration: RMT
    led_strip_rmt_config_t rmt_config = {
        .clk_src = RMT_CLK_SRC_DEFAULT,        // different clock source can lead to different power consumption
        .resolution_hz = 10 * 1000 * 1000, // RMT counter clock frequency
        .mem_block_symbols = 0, // the memory block size used by the RMT channel
        .flags = {
            .with_dma = 0,     // Using DMA can improve performance when driving more LEDs
        }
    };

    // LED Strip object handle
    led_strip_handle_t led_strip;
    ESP_ERROR_CHECK(led_strip_new_rmt_device(&strip_config, &rmt_config, &led_strip));
    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Created LED strip object with RMT backend");
    return led_strip;
}


void _set_single_led_color(RGB_example_color_t color ,uint32_t led_index)
{
    rgb_color_t current_color = color_rgb_map[color];

    /*
    
    */
    ESP_ERROR_CHECK(led_strip_set_pixel(led_strip, led_index, current_color.r, current_color.g, current_color.b));
    /* Refresh the strip to send data */
    ESP_ERROR_CHECK(led_strip_refresh(led_strip));
}

//100ms scan
void _example_playing(bool *reset)
{
    static uint16_t time_count = 0;
    static uint8_t led_num = 0;
    static bool clean_flag = 0;
    if(*reset)
    {
        time_count = 0;
        led_num = 0;
        *reset = 0;
        ESP_ERROR_CHECK(led_strip_clear(led_strip));
    }

    time_count++;
    if(time_count>=5)
    {
        time_count=0;

        if(clean_flag!=1)
        {
            _set_single_led_color(RGB_color,led_num++);
            if(led_num>=LED_STRIP_LED_COUNT)
            {
                led_num=0;
                clean_flag = 1;
            }
        }
        else
        {
            ESP_ERROR_CHECK(led_strip_clear(led_strip));
            clean_flag = 0;
        }
            
    }
}

//100ms scan
void _example_esp_sr_rec()
{
    static uint16_t time_count = 0;
    static bool led_on_off = 0;

    time_count++;
    if(time_count>=2)
    {
        time_count=0;
        if (led_on_off) 
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < LED_STRIP_LED_COUNT; i++) 
            {
                _set_single_led_color(RGB_color,i);
            }
        } 
        else 
        {
            ESP_ERROR_CHECK(led_strip_clear(led_strip));
        }

        led_on_off = !led_on_off;
    }
        

}

void _RGB_Example(void *arg)
{
    static RGB_example_mode_t RGB_mode = RGB_MODE_IDLE;
    rgb_queue_data_t msg;
    bool reset_playing_color = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < LED_STRIP_LED_COUNT; i++) {
        _set_single_led_color(RGB_color,i);
    }
    while (1) 
    {
        BaseType_t ret = xQueueReceive(g_msg_queue, &msg, pdMS_TO_TICKS(100));
        if (ret == pdPASS) 
        {
            switch (msg.cmd)
            {
                case RGB_CMD_SET_MODE:
                    RGB_mode = msg.param.mode;
                    //ESP_LOGI(TAG, "收到消息: 模式=%d", msg.param.mode);
                    ESP_ERROR_CHECK(led_strip_clear(led_strip));
                    if(RGB_mode == RGB_MODE_IDLE)
                    {
                        for (int i = 0; i < LED_STRIP_LED_COUNT; i++) {
                            _set_single_led_color(RGB_color,i);
                        }
                    }
                    break;
                case RGB_CMD_SET_COLOR:
                    RGB_color = msg.param.color;   
                    reset_playing_color=1;
                    // Idle mode has no periodic animation: redraw the strip
                    // immediately with the new color.
                    if (RGB_mode == RGB_MODE_IDLE) {
                        for (int i = 0; i < LED_STRIP_LED_COUNT; i++) {
                            _set_single_led_color(RGB_color, i);
                        }
                    }
                    //ESP_LOGI(TAG, "收到消息: 颜色=%d", msg.param.color);
                    break;
                default:
                    break;
            }
            

        } 
        else 
        {
            switch (RGB_mode)
            {
                case RGB_MODE_IDLE:
                    
                    break;
                case RGB_MODE_PLAYING:
                    _example_playing(&reset_playing_color);
                    break;
                case RGB_MODE_REC_COMMAND:
                    _example_esp_sr_rec();
                    break;
                default:
                    break;
            }
        }
    }
}

void RGB_Example(void)
{
    led_strip = configure_led();
    g_msg_queue = xQueueCreate(3, sizeof(rgb_queue_data_t));
    if (g_msg_queue == NULL) {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "队列创建失败");
        return;
    }
    xTaskCreatePinnedToCore(_RGB_Example, "RGB Demo",4096, NULL, 2, NULL, 0);
}


void set_rgb_mode(RGB_example_mode_t mode)
{
    if (g_msg_queue == NULL) {
        ESP_LOGW(TAG, "RGB not initialized yet (queue missing); ignoring mode %d", mode);
        return;
    }
    rgb_queue_data_t msg;
    msg.cmd = RGB_CMD_SET_MODE;
    msg.param.mode = mode;
        
    // 发送到队列（超时100ms）
    BaseType_t ret = xQueueSend(g_msg_queue, &msg, pdMS_TO_TICKS(1000));
    if (ret != pdPASS) 
    {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "指令发送失败！队列已满");
    }
}

void set_rgb_color(RGB_example_color_t color)
{
    if (g_msg_queue == NULL) {
        ESP_LOGW(TAG, "RGB not initialized yet (queue missing); ignoring color %d", color);
        return;
    }
    rgb_queue_data_t msg;
    msg.cmd = RGB_CMD_SET_COLOR;
    msg.param.color = color;
        
    // 发送到队列（超时100ms）
    BaseType_t ret = xQueueSend(g_msg_queue, &msg, pdMS_TO_TICKS(1000));
    if (ret != pdPASS) 
    {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "指令发送失败！队列已满");
    }
}

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/drivers/speech/mic_speech.c
--------------------------------------------------------------
#include "mic_speech.h"

#include "esp_wn_iface.h"
#include "esp_wn_models.h"
#include "esp_afe_sr_iface.h"
#include "esp_afe_sr_models.h"
#include "esp_mn_iface.h"
#include "esp_mn_models.h"
#include "model_path.h"
#include "esp_process_sdkconfig.h"

#include "bsp_board.h"
#include "esp_log.h"
#include "esp_task_wdt.h" 
static const char *TAG = "App/Speech";


int wakeup_flag = 0;
static esp_afe_sr_iface_t *afe_handle = NULL;
static volatile int task_flag = 0;
srmodel_list_t *models = NULL;
static esp_sr_event_callback_t spench_callback =  NULL;

void feed_Task(void *arg)
{
    esp_afe_sr_data_t *afe_data = arg;
    int audio_chunksize = afe_handle->get_feed_chunksize(afe_data);
    int nch = afe_handle->get_feed_channel_num(afe_data);
    int feed_channel = esp_get_feed_channel();
    assert(nch == feed_channel);
    int16_t *i2s_buff = heap_caps_malloc(audio_chunksize * sizeof(int16_t) * feed_channel,MALLOC_CAP_SPIRAM);

    assert(i2s_buff);

    esp_task_wdt_add(NULL);      // 先订阅当前任务
    //* emily: this is where the audio stream borned ;
    while (task_flag) {
        esp_get_feed_data(true, i2s_buff, audio_chunksize * sizeof(int16_t) * feed_channel);
        afe_handle->feed(afe_data, i2s_buff);
        esp_task_wdt_reset();
    }
    if (i2s_buff) {
        free(i2s_buff);
        i2s_buff = NULL;
    }
    vTaskDelete(NULL);
}

void detect_Task(void *arg)
{
    esp_afe_sr_data_t *afe_data = arg;
    int afe_chunksize = afe_handle->get_fetch_chunksize(afe_data);
    char *mn_name = esp_srmodel_filter(models, ESP_MN_PREFIX, ESP_MN_CHINESE);
    printf("multinet:%s\n", mn_name);
    esp_mn_iface_t *multinet = esp_mn_handle_from_name(mn_name);
    model_iface_data_t *model_data = multinet->create(mn_name, 6000);
    esp_mn_commands_update_from_sdkconfig(multinet, model_data); // Add speech commands from sdkconfig
    int mu_chunksize = multinet->get_samp_chunksize(model_data);
    assert(mu_chunksize == afe_chunksize);
    multinet->print_active_speech_commands(model_data);

    esp_task_wdt_add(NULL);      // 先订阅当前任务

    while (task_flag) {
        afe_fetch_result_t* res = afe_handle->fetch(afe_data); 
        if (!res || res->ret_value == ESP_FAIL) {
            printf("fetch error!\n");
            break;
        }

        if (res->wakeup_state == WAKENET_DETECTED) {
            //printf("WAKEWORD DETECTED\n");
	        multinet->clean(model_data);
        }

        if (res->raw_data_channels == 1 && res->wakeup_state == WAKENET_DETECTED) {
            wakeup_flag = 1;
        } else if (res->raw_data_channels > 1 && res->wakeup_state == WAKENET_CHANNEL_VERIFIED) {
            // For a multi-channel AFE, it is necessary to wait for the channel to be verified.
            //printf("AFE_FETCH_CHANNEL_VERIFIED, channel index: %d\n", res->trigger_channel_id);
            esp_sr_evt_data_t evtdata;
            evtdata.awaken_channel = res->trigger_channel_id;
            if(spench_callback!=NULL)
            {
                spench_callback(ESP_SR_EVT_AWAKEN,evtdata,NULL);
            }
            wakeup_flag = 1;
        }

        esp_task_wdt_reset();
        if (wakeup_flag == 1) {
            
            esp_mn_state_t mn_state = multinet->detect(model_data, res->data);

            if (mn_state == ESP_MN_STATE_DETECTING) {
                continue;
            }

            if (mn_state == ESP_MN_STATE_DETECTED) {
                esp_mn_results_t *mn_result = multinet->get_results(model_data);

                //ESP_LOGI(TAG, "TOP %d, command_id: %d, phrase_id: %d, string:%s prob: %f\n", 
                //1, mn_result->command_id[0], mn_result->phrase_id[0], mn_result->string, mn_result->prob[0]);

                esp_sr_evt_data_t evtdata;
                evtdata.sr_cmd = mn_result->command_id[0];
                if(spench_callback!=NULL)
                {
                    spench_callback(ESP_SR_EVT_CMD,evtdata,NULL);
                }
            }

            if (mn_state == ESP_MN_STATE_TIMEOUT) {
                esp_mn_results_t *mn_result = multinet->get_results(model_data);
                //printf("timeout, string:%s\n", mn_result->string);
                afe_handle->enable_wakenet(afe_data);
                wakeup_flag = 0;

                esp_sr_evt_data_t evtdata;
                if(spench_callback!=NULL)
                {
                    spench_callback(ESP_SR_EVT_CMD_TIMEOUT,evtdata,NULL);
                }

                continue;
            }

        }
        esp_task_wdt_reset();
        
    }
    if (model_data) {
        multinet->destroy(model_data);
        model_data = NULL;
    }
    printf("detect exit\n");
    vTaskDelete(NULL);
}

void Speech_Init(void)
{
    //* emily : Loads Speech Models:
    models = esp_srmodel_init("model"); // partition label defined in partitions.csv


    afe_config_t *afe_config = afe_config_init(esp_get_input_format(), models, AFE_TYPE_SR, AFE_MODE_LOW_COST);
    afe_config->ns_init = false;
    afe_config->vad_init = false;
    afe_handle = esp_afe_handle_from_config(afe_config);
    esp_afe_sr_data_t *afe_data = afe_handle->create_from_config(afe_config);
    afe_config_free(afe_config);

    task_flag = 1;
    xTaskCreatePinnedToCore(&detect_Task, "detect", 8 * 1024, (void*)afe_data, 5, NULL, 1);
    xTaskCreatePinnedToCore(&feed_Task, "feed", 8 * 1024, (void*)afe_data, 5, NULL, 0);
}

// 注册回调
esp_err_t Speech_register_callback(esp_sr_event_callback_t callback) 
{
    if (!callback) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    spench_callback = callback;
    return ESP_OK;
}

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/drivers/tca9555/tca9555_driver.c
--------------------------------------------------------------
#include "tca9555_driver.h"
#include "esp_log.h"
#include "bsp_board.h"


esp_io_expander_handle_t io_expander = NULL;


void tca9555_driver_init(void)
{

    i2c_master_bus_handle_t i2c_bus = esp_ret_i2c_handle();
    esp_err_t ret = esp_io_expander_new_i2c_tca95xx_16bit(i2c_bus, ESP_IO_EXPANDER_I2C_TCA9555_ADDRESS_000, &io_expander);

   /* Test output level function */
   ret = esp_io_expander_set_dir(io_expander, (IO_EXPANDER_PIN_NUM_0 | IO_EXPANDER_PIN_NUM_1 | IO_EXPANDER_PIN_NUM_5 | IO_EXPANDER_PIN_NUM_6 | IO_EXPANDER_PIN_NUM_8), IO_EXPANDER_OUTPUT);
   if(ret != ESP_OK){
    printf("EXIO Mode setting failure!!\r\n");
   }
   
   ret = esp_io_expander_set_dir(io_expander, (IO_EXPANDER_PIN_NUM_2 | IO_EXPANDER_PIN_NUM_9 | IO_EXPANDER_PIN_NUM_10 | IO_EXPANDER_PIN_NUM_11), IO_EXPANDER_INPUT);   
   if(ret != ESP_OK){
    printf("EXIO Mode setting failure!!\r\n");
   }
}


/********************************************************** Set the EXIO output status **********************************************************/  
void Set_EXIO(uint32_t Pin,uint8_t State)                  // Sets the level state of the Pin without affecting the other pins(PIN：1~8)
{
    esp_err_t ret;
    ret = esp_io_expander_set_level(io_expander, Pin, State);      
    if(ret != ESP_OK){
        printf("EXIO level setting failure!!\r\n");
    }

}
/********************************************************** Read EXIO status **********************************************************/       
bool Read_EXIO(uint32_t Pin)                            // Read the level of the TCA9555PWR Pin
{
    
    esp_err_t ret;
    uint32_t input_level_mask = 0;            
    ret = esp_io_expander_get_level(io_expander, Pin, &input_level_mask);      
    if(ret != ESP_OK){
        printf("EXIO level reading failure!!\r\n");
    }          
    bool bitStatus = input_level_mask & Pin;                             
    return bitStatus;                                                              
}

#include "hardware.hpp"

#include "esp_err.h"
#include "esp_log.h"

#include "bsp_board.h"
#include "rgb_led_driver.h"
#include "tca9555_driver.h"

static const char* TAG = "hardware";

namespace hardware {

void boot(const core::AudioConfig& config) {
    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Booting hardware...");

    ESP_ERROR_CHECK(esp_board_init(config.sample_rate,
                                   static_cast<int>(config.channel_count),
                                   static_cast<int>(config.bits_per_chan)));
    tca9555_driver_init();
    esp_sdcard_init(const_cast<char*>("/sdcard"), 10);

    // Must run before any set_rgb_mode/set_rgb_color call: it creates the
    // message queue and the LED task used by EspIndicator.
    RGB_Example();

    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Hardware ready");
}

} // namespace hardware

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/net/esp_udp_datagram_io.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#include "esp_hardware/net/esp_udp_datagram_io.hpp"

namespace adapters {

EspUdpDatagramIo::EspUdpDatagramIo(const char* host, const char* port) {
    auto connection = general::net::openUdpConnection(host, port);
    if (connection.has_value()) {
        conn_ = *connection;
    }
}

EspUdpDatagramIo::~EspUdpDatagramIo() {
    if (conn_.is_valid()) {
        ::close(conn_.sock);
        conn_.sock = -1;
    }
}

int EspUdpDatagramIo::send(const uint8_t* data, std::size_t len) {
    return general::net::udpSend(conn_, data, len);
}

int EspUdpDatagramIo::receive(uint8_t* buffer, std::size_t capacity) {
    struct sockaddr_storage src_addr;
    socklen_t src_addr_len = sizeof(src_addr);
    return general::net::udpReceive(conn_.sock, buffer, capacity,
                                    &src_addr, &src_addr_len);
}

} // namespace adapters

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/net/esp_udp_datagram_io.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

// Adapter: DatagramIo over the portable POSIX UDP socket helpers.
// All socket logic lives in general/net/udp_socket.hpp; this class only binds
// the general socket code to the core::ITransport/DatagramIo seam. A 4G modem
// would replace this with an AT-command implementation of the same interface.

#include <cstddef>

#include "general/kcp/datagram_io.hpp"
#include "general/net/udp_socket.hpp"

namespace adapters {

class EspUdpDatagramIo : public general::kcp::DatagramIo {
public:
    EspUdpDatagramIo(const char* host, const char* port);

    // Closes the socket: close()/reset() would otherwise leak one FD per
    // reconnect (lwIP has a small MAX_SOCKETS budget).
    ~EspUdpDatagramIo() override;

    EspUdpDatagramIo(const EspUdpDatagramIo&) = delete;
    EspUdpDatagramIo& operator=(const EspUdpDatagramIo&) = delete;

    int send(const uint8_t* data, std::size_t len) override;
    int receive(uint8_t* buffer, std::size_t capacity) override;

    bool isOpen() const { return conn_.is_valid(); }

private:
    general::net::UdpConnection conn_;
};

} // namespace adapters

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/esp_hardware/storage/file_manager/file_manager.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
  

#include "file_manager.hpp" // Includes the Level 1 header declarations to verify prototype compliance
#include "esp_log.h"        // ESP-IDF logging framework used for console diagnostic and error logs
#include "esp_littlefs.h"   // Official LittleFS API for configuration, mounting, and driver stats
#include "general/io/path.hpp" // Portable path join (general/io/path.hpp)

namespace FileManager { // Opens the FileManager namespace implementation block

// Static tag used by the ESP-IDF logging macros to filter output from this specific module
static const char* TAG = "FileManager"; // Logging identifier shown in console messages

// Stores the base mount path prefix where LittleFS is registered in the Virtual Filesystem (VFS)
static std::string s_base_path = "/littlefs"; // Default mount path prefix stored as internal module state

// Stores the partition label matching the name configured in the partitions.csv partition table
static std::string s_partition_label = "my_files"; // Default label used to locate flash partition

// Tracks the current operational status of the LittleFS filesystem
static bool s_is_mounted = false; // Flag set to true upon successful mount and false upon unmount

std::string getFullPath(const std::string& filename) { // Converts any relative filename to its absolute VFS path
    return general::io::join(s_base_path, filename); // Portable path join (general/io/path.hpp)
} // Closes getFullPath function definition

esp_err_t init(const std::string& base_path, const std::string& partition_label, size_t max_files) { // Mounts LittleFS
    (void)max_files; // LittleFS allocates file descriptors dynamically, argument retained for API compatibility
    s_base_path = base_path; // Overwrites the default base path with caller's requested path
    s_partition_label = partition_label; // Overwrites the default label with caller's partition name

    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Initializing LittleFS [%s] at [%s]", s_partition_label.c_str(), s_base_path.c_str()); // Logs mount intent

    esp_vfs_littlefs_conf_t conf = { // Defines the configuration structure required by the ESP LittleFS driver
        .base_path = s_base_path.c_str(), // Assigns VFS mount root path pointer
        .partition_label = s_partition_label.c_str(), // Assigns partition table search label pointer
        .format_if_mount_failed = true, // Automatically formats flash partition if corrupt or unformatted
        .dont_mount = false // Requests immediate mounting upon driver registration
    }; // Closes conf structure initialization

    esp_err_t ret = esp_vfs_littlefs_register(&conf); // Requests ESP-IDF VFS to register and mount the driver

    if (ret != ESP_OK) { // Evaluates if the mount attempt encountered an error
        if (ret == ESP_FAIL) { // Mount failure code specifically indicating mounting or formatting issues
            ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Failed to mount or format filesystem"); // Logs mount failure explanation
        } else if (ret == ESP_ERR_NOT_FOUND) { // Partition label lookup failure
            ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Failed to find LittleFS partition '%s'", s_partition_label.c_str()); // Logs missing partition
        } else { // Catches any remaining driver-specific error codes
            ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Failed to initialize LittleFS (%s)", esp_err_to_name(ret)); // Logs translated error message
        } // Closes error classification ladder
        return ret; // Returns error status code early to caller without modifying state flags
    } // Closes mount check block

    s_is_mounted = true; // Updates internal flag to indicate filesystem is active and ready

    return ESP_OK; // Returns success code indicating clean initialization
} // Closes init function definition

esp_err_t deinit() { // Unmounts and tears down the registered LittleFS instance
    if (!s_is_mounted) { // Checks if filesystem is already unmounted to avoid redundant operations
        return ESP_OK; // Safely returns success if filesystem was not active
    } // Closes unmounted check block

    esp_err_t ret = esp_vfs_littlefs_unregister(s_partition_label.c_str()); // Requests VFS to unregister the partition
    if (ret == ESP_OK) { // Verifies unmount driver call completed successfully
        s_is_mounted = false; // Clears the internal mount state flag
        ESP_LOGI(TAG, "LittleFS unmounted successfully"); // Logs confirmation that filesystem is unloaded
    } else { // Triggered if the driver returns an error on unregister
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Failed to unmount LittleFS (%s)", esp_err_to_name(ret)); // Logs unregister error details
    } // Closes unmount status check
    return ret; // Returns the resulting status code back to the caller
} // Closes deinit function definition

bool isMounted() { // Accessor for checking module state externally
    return s_is_mounted; // Returns boolean flag reflecting active mount status
} // Closes isMounted function definition

} // namespace FileManager // Closes the FileManager namespace implementation block

#pragma once

// LittleFS mount + path resolution. File-level operations live in
// general/io/StdioFileStore (see adapters/storage/EspFileStore).

#include "esp_err.h"

#include <cstddef>
#include <string>

esp_err_t init(const std::string& base_path = "/littlefs",
               const std::string& partition_label = "my_files",
               size_t max_files = 5);

// Unmounts/unregisters the LittleFS partition.
esp_err_t deinit();

bool isMounted();

// Prefixes a relative path with the configured mount point.
std::string getFullPath(const std::string& filename);

} // namespace FileManager

//     esp_err_t ret = esp_ota_mark_app_invalid_cancel_rollback(); // Cancel rollback or flag current app state
//     if (ret != ESP_OK) { // Check if rollback operation failed
//         ESP_LOGW(TAG, "Rollback state change warning or error: %s", esp_err_to_name(ret)); // Log warning message
//         return false; // Return failure status
//     }

//     ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Rollback configuration updated successfully."); // Log successful action
//     return true; // Return success status
// }


// bool FirmwareSwitch::markAppValid() { // Method to mark current running application as stable and valid
//     if (!m_is_initialized) return false; // Verify module readiness state

//     // Call correct ESP-IDF API to mark app as working well and prevent automatic rollback
//     esp_err_t ret = esp_ota_mark_app_valid_cancel_rollback(); 
//     if (ret != ESP_OK) { // Check if the API call returned an error status
//         ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Failed to mark application as valid: %s", esp_err_to_name(ret)); // Log error description
//         return false; // Return failure status code
//     }

//     ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Application marked as valid and rollback cancelled successfully."); // Log success message
//     return true; // Return success status flag
// }

#include "firmware_switch.hpp" // Include corresponding header file definitions
#include "esp_log.h"           // Include ESP-IDF logging library header

FirmwareSwitch::FirmwareSwitch() // Constructor definition initializing member fields
    : m_running_partition(nullptr), // Initialize running partition pointer to null
      m_next_partition(nullptr),    // Initialize next partition pointer to null
      m_ota_handle(0),              // Initialize OTA write session handle to zero
      m_is_initialized(false),      // Initialize readiness flag to false state
      m_is_updating(false) {}       // Initialize active update flag to false state

FirmwareSwitch::~FirmwareSwitch() { // Destructor definition for clean lifecycle exit
    if (m_is_updating && m_ota_handle != 0) { // Check if an OTA session was left open unintentionally
        esp_ota_end(m_ota_handle);   // Abort/close active OTA session to prevent memory/handle leaks
    }
}

bool FirmwareSwitch::init() { // Initialization method checking OTA partition states
    m_running_partition = esp_ota_get_running_partition(); // Fetch partition currently executing application code
    if (m_running_partition == nullptr) { // Check if running partition query failed
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Failed to get running firmware partition"); // Log error message to console output
        return false; // Return failure status code
    }

    m_next_partition = esp_ota_get_next_update_partition(nullptr); // Fetch next alternate OTA slot partition
    if (m_next_partition == nullptr) { // Check if alternate partition query failed
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Failed to get next update firmware partition"); // Log error message to console output
        return false; // Return failure status code
    }

    m_is_initialized = true; // Mark component state as ready and initialized
    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "FirmwareSwitch initialized. Running: [%s], Alternate: [%s]", // Log success info with partition names
             m_running_partition->label, m_next_partition->label); // Pass partition labels to log macro
    return true; // Return success status flag
}

std::string FirmwareSwitch::getRunningPartitionName() const { // Method returning active partition name string
    if (!m_is_initialized) return ""; // Return empty string if component uninitialized
    return std::string(m_running_partition->label); // Return label string of running partition
}

std::string FirmwareSwitch::getNextPartitionName() const { // Method returning alternate partition name string
    if (!m_is_initialized) return ""; // Return empty string if component uninitialized
    return std::string(m_next_partition->label); // Return label string of alternate partition
}

bool FirmwareSwitch::switchToAlternateSlot() { // Method to switch boot target to the alternative slot
    if (!m_is_initialized) return false; // Verify module readiness state
    
    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Switching boot partition to alternate slot: [%s]", m_next_partition->label); // Log switch action
    esp_err_t ret = esp_ota_set_boot_partition(m_next_partition); // Call ESP-IDF API to set next boot target
    if (ret != ESP_OK) { // Check if setting boot partition returned an error
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Failed to set boot partition: %s", esp_err_to_name(ret)); // Log error details
        return false; // Return failure status
    }

    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Boot partition updated successfully. Restart required to apply."); // Log success notice
    return true; // Return success status
}

bool FirmwareSwitch::markAppValid() { // Method to mark current running application as stable and valid
    if (!m_is_initialized) return false; // Verify module readiness state

    esp_err_t ret = esp_ota_mark_app_valid_cancel_rollback(); // Call ESP-IDF API to confirm app status
    if (ret != ESP_OK) { // Check if the API call returned an error status
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Failed to mark application as valid: %s", esp_err_to_name(ret)); // Log error description
        return false; // Return failure status code
    }

    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Application marked as valid and rollback cancelled successfully."); // Log success message
    return true; // Return success status flag
}

bool FirmwareSwitch::beginOtaUpdate(size_t image_size) { // Method to start a new OTA update session on alternate slot
    if (!m_is_initialized) return false; // Verify module readiness state
    if (m_is_updating) { // Check if an OTA session is already open
        ESP_LOGW(TAG, "OTA update session is already in progress."); // Log warning notice
        return false; // Return failure status
    }

    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Beginning OTA update session on alternate partition: [%s]", m_next_partition->label); // Log action
    esp_err_t ret = esp_ota_begin(m_next_partition, image_size, &m_ota_handle); // Begin OTA write operation
    if (ret != ESP_OK) { // Check if starting OTA session failed
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "esp_ota_begin failed: %s", esp_err_to_name(ret)); // Log error explanation
        return false; // Return failure status
    }

    m_is_updating = true; // Set active update tracking flag to true
    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "OTA begin successful. Ready to receive firmware data chunks."); // Log readiness notice
    return true; // Return success status
}

bool FirmwareSwitch::writeOtaData(const void* data, size_t size) { // Method to write binary firmware data chunks
    if (!m_is_initialized || !m_is_updating) return false; // Fail early if uninitialized or session not active

    esp_err_t ret = esp_ota_write(m_ota_handle, (const void*)data, size); // Write data chunk to flash memory
    if (ret != ESP_OK) { // Check if write operation returned an error
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "esp_ota_write failed: %s; aborting OTA session", esp_err_to_name(ret));
        // Abort so a failed download cannot lock the updater until reboot.
        esp_ota_abort(m_ota_handle);
        m_ota_handle = 0;
        m_is_updating = false;
        return false; // Return failure status
    }

    return true; // Return success status for this chunk
}

bool FirmwareSwitch::endOtaUpdate() { // Method to finalize OTA write session and set boot partition
    if (!m_is_initialized || !m_is_updating) return false; // Verify module and session state

    esp_err_t ret = esp_ota_end(m_ota_handle); // Complete OTA session and verify image integrity/checksum
    m_is_updating = false; // Reset active session flag
    m_ota_handle = 0;      // Clear OTA session handle variable

    if (ret != ESP_OK) { // Check if finalization or image verification failed
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "esp_ota_end failed: %s", esp_err_to_name(ret)); // Log error description
        return false; // Return failure status
    }

    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "OTA update written successfully. Setting alternate slot as boot target..."); // Log progress
    return switchToAlternateSlot(); // Automatically set the updated alternate slot as the next boot target
}

#pragma once
 

#include "esp_err.h"        // Include ESP-IDF error definitions header
#include "esp_ota_ops.h"    // Include ESP-IDF Over-The-Air operations header
#include <string>           // Include standard C++ string library header

class FirmwareSwitch {      // Declare class managing firmware slot switching and OTA state
public:                     // Public access specifier for external component interaction
    FirmwareSwitch();       // Constructor signature for class initialization
    ~FirmwareSwitch();      // Destructor signature for lifecycle cleanup

    bool init();            // Initialize OTA switch manager status
    std::string getRunningPartitionName() const; // Retrieve name of currently running firmware slot
    std::string getNextPartitionName() const;    // Retrieve name of next target firmware slot
    bool switchToAlternateSlot();                // Switch active boot partition to the alternate slot
    bool markAppValid();                         // Mark current running application as valid and cancel rollback

    // Core OTA writing APIs to flash firmware into the alternate slot
    bool beginOtaUpdate(size_t image_size = OTA_SIZE_UNKNOWN); // Initialize an OTA write session on alternate slot
    bool writeOtaData(const void* data, size_t size);          // Write a chunk of binary firmware data to flash
    bool endOtaUpdate();                                       // Finalize OTA session and set boot partition

private:                    // Private access specifier for internal helper variables and logic
    const esp_partition_t* m_running_partition; // Pointer structure holding current running partition info
    const esp_partition_t* m_next_partition;    // Pointer structure holding next alternate partition info
    esp_ota_handle_t m_ota_handle;              // Handle identifier tracking active OTA flash write session
    bool m_is_initialized;                      // Boolean flag tracking initialization readiness state
    bool m_is_updating;                         // Boolean flag tracking active OTA write session state
    static constexpr const char* TAG = "FirmwareSwitch"; // Logging tag constant string for console outputs
};                          // End of FirmwareSwitch class declaration

FILE: main/esp_hardware/wifi/wifi-manager/WiFiManager.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------


#include "wifi-state/wifi-connect.hpp"
#include "wifi-state/wifi-disconnect.hpp"
#include "wifi-state/wifi-gotIp.hpp"
#include "WiFiManager.hpp"
#include <cstdint>
namespace wifi {





    


 
    // Initialize using the library's built-in multi-network, NVS, and HTTP features
    void begin(const std::string& fallbackAPName  ) {
 
        esp_err_t ret ;

    // NVS must be ready before the WiFi manager loads saved credentials.
    // Idempotent, so it is safe even if someone else initialized it first.
    esp_err_t nvs_err = nvs_flash_init();
    if (nvs_err == ESP_ERR_NVS_NO_FREE_PAGES || nvs_err == ESP_ERR_NVS_NEW_VERSION_FOUND) {
        nvs_flash_erase();
        nvs_flash_init();
    }

    // Initialize esp_bus first (required by wifi_manager)
    ret = esp_bus_init();
    if (ret != ESP_OK) {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Failed to initialize esp_bus: %s", esp_err_to_name(ret));
        return;
    }

   // Subscribe to WiFi events (before wifi_manager_init to catch early events)
    esp_bus_sub(WIFI_EVT(WIFI_MGR_EVT_CONNECTED), on_wifi_connected, NULL);
    esp_bus_sub(WIFI_EVT(WIFI_MGR_EVT_DISCONNECTED), on_wifi_disconnected, NULL);
    esp_bus_sub("wifi:got_ip", on_wifi_got_ip, NULL);
    //esp_bus_sub(WIFI_EVT(WIFI_MGR_EVT_VAR_CHANGED), on_var_changed, NULL);



        // 2. Configure the WiFi Manager using its native configuration struct
        wifi_manager_config_t config = {
            .default_networks = nullptr, // No hardcoded credentials; loads from NVS first!
            .default_network_count = 0,
            
            .auto_reconnect = true,
            
            // SoftAP & Captive portal settings if NVS is empty / connection fails
            .default_ap = {
                .ssid = "", // Will use fallback name below if needed
                .password = "12345678",
            },
            .enable_captive_portal = true, // Spawns the AP website automatically if all fails
            
            // Enable the built-in HTTP REST API provided by the library
            .http = {
                .enable = true,
                .api_base_path = "/api/wifi",
            },
        };

        // Copy fallback AP SSID safely
        strlcpy(config.default_ap.ssid, fallbackAPName.c_str(), sizeof(config.default_ap.ssid));

        // 3. Initialize the library (Handles NVS loading, background auto-connect, and web server)
        ESP_ERROR_CHECK(wifi_manager_init(&config));
        
        ESP_LOGI(TAG, "WiFi Manager started in background. Checking NVS credentials...");
    }

 
 
}

             info->ssid, static_cast<unsigned>(info->reason));

    // TODO(proto): once components/proto defines the "disconnected" message,
    // encode via protocol::IoT::client::encode...(...) and Network::send(...).
}

#pragma once

#include "esp_bus.h"

namespace wifi {
 

 /**
 * @brief Callback for got IP event
 * @param event Event identifier string (e.g., "got_ip")
 * @param data Pointer to event-specific data structure (esp_netif_ip_info_t)
 * @param len Length of data payload
 * @param ctx User-defined context pointer
 */
inline void on_wifi_got_ip(const char *event, const void *data, size_t len, void *ctx)
{
    // Log that the IP acquisition event has fired
    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "+++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++WiFi got IP event tag [%s] received", event);

    // Retrieve the full current status object from the wifi manager library
    wifi_status_t status;
    if (wifi_manager_get_status(&status) == ESP_OK) {
        // Log IP details. No protocol::IoT "got_ip" message exists yet, so we
        // intentionally do NOT emit a non-conforming packet here.
        ESP_LOGI(TAG, "WiFi got IP: ip=%s gateway=%s netmask=%s dns=%s mac=%s quality=%u",
                 status.ip, status.gateway, status.netmask, status.dns, status.mac,
                 static_cast<unsigned>(status.quality));

        // TODO(proto): once components/proto defines the "got_ip" message,
        // encode via protocol::IoT::client::encode...(...) and Network::send(...).

    } else {
        ESP_LOGW(TAG, "Failed to retrieve full WiFi status during got_ip event");
    }
}


} // namespace wifi

FILE: main/general/audio/frame_math.hpp

#pragma once

// Portable audio frame math shared by every audio backend.

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>

namespace general::audio {

constexpr std::size_t samplesPerFrame(uint32_t sample_rate, uint32_t frame_ms) {
    return static_cast<std::size_t>(sample_rate) * frame_ms / 1000u;
}

constexpr std::size_t bytesPerFrame(uint32_t sample_rate, uint32_t frame_ms,
                                    uint32_t channels, uint32_t bits_per_sample) {
    return samplesPerFrame(sample_rate, frame_ms) * channels * (bits_per_sample / 8u);
}

} // namespace general::audio

FILE: main/general/crypto/crypto.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

// Portable crypto primitives (PSA-backed AES-256-GCM + ECDH P-256).
// mbedTLS/PSA is a portable library; platform-specific init and randomness are
// injected through CryptoBackend (implemented by the hardware layer).

#include "psa/crypto.h"

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <vector>

#include "general/log.hpp"

namespace encryption {

static const char* TAG = "AES_GCM";

constexpr size_t AES_KEY_BYTES = 32;   // 256-bit AES key
constexpr size_t GCM_NONCE_BYTES = 12; // standard 12-byte nonce (IV)
constexpr size_t GCM_TAG_BYTES = 16;   // standard 16-byte auth tag

// Platform glue: one-time init + cryptographically secure randomness.
struct CryptoBackend {
    virtual ~CryptoBackend() = default;
    virtual bool init() = 0;
    virtual void fillRandom(uint8_t* output, std::size_t len) = 0;
};

// Encrypts plaintext using AES-256-GCM. Output layout: [12B nonce | 16B tag | ciphertext].
bool aes_gcm_encrypt(const uint8_t key[AES_KEY_BYTES],
                     const uint8_t* plaintext, size_t len,
                     uint8_t* out_buffer, size_t* out_len,
                     CryptoBackend& backend);

std::optional<std::vector<uint8_t>> aes_gcm_encrypt(const std::vector<uint8_t>& plaintext,
                                                    std::vector<uint8_t> aes_key,
                                                    CryptoBackend& backend);

// Decrypts a packet laid out as [12B nonce | 16B tag | ciphertext].
bool aes_gcm_decrypt(const uint8_t key[AES_KEY_BYTES],
                     const uint8_t* packet, size_t packet_len,
                     uint8_t* plaintext_out, size_t* plaintext_len,
                     CryptoBackend& backend);

std::optional<std::vector<uint8_t>> aes_gcm_decrypt(const std::vector<uint8_t>& packet,
                                                    std::vector<uint8_t> key,
                                                    CryptoBackend& backend);

// ECDH P-256 handshake. Owns the PSA key pair; generatePublicKey() and the
// destructor destroy any previous key so aborted handshakes cannot leak PSA
// key slots.
class EcdhHandshake {
public:
    EcdhHandshake() = default;
    ~EcdhHandshake();

    EcdhHandshake(const EcdhHandshake&) = delete;
    EcdhHandshake& operator=(const EcdhHandshake&) = delete;

    std::optional<std::vector<uint8_t>> generatePublicKey(CryptoBackend& backend);
    std::optional<std::vector<uint8_t>> computeSharedSecret(
        const std::vector<uint8_t>& peer_public_key, CryptoBackend& backend);

private:
    psa_key_id_t key_id_ = 0;
};

} // namespace encryption

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/general/crypto/decryption.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#include "crypto.hpp"

#include <cstring>

namespace encryption {

bool aes_gcm_decrypt(const uint8_t key[AES_KEY_BYTES],
                     const uint8_t* packet, size_t packet_len,
                     uint8_t* plaintext_out, size_t* plaintext_len,
                     CryptoBackend& backend)
{
    if (!backend.init()) {
        return false;
    }

    if (packet_len < GCM_NONCE_BYTES + GCM_TAG_BYTES) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Packet too short");
        return false;
    }

    const size_t ciphertext_len = packet_len - GCM_NONCE_BYTES - GCM_TAG_BYTES;
    if (*plaintext_len < ciphertext_len) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Output buffer too small");
        return false;
    }

    // Packet layout: [12B nonce | 16B tag | ciphertext]
    const uint8_t* nonce = packet;
    const uint8_t* tag = packet + GCM_NONCE_BYTES;
    const uint8_t* ciphertext = packet + GCM_NONCE_BYTES + GCM_TAG_BYTES;

    psa_key_attributes_t attributes = PSA_KEY_ATTRIBUTES_INIT;
    psa_set_key_usage_flags(&attributes, PSA_KEY_USAGE_DECRYPT);
    psa_set_key_algorithm(&attributes, PSA_ALG_GCM);
    psa_set_key_type(&attributes, PSA_KEY_TYPE_AES);
    psa_set_key_bits(&attributes, AES_KEY_BYTES * 8);

    psa_key_id_t key_id;
    psa_status_t status = psa_import_key(&attributes, key, AES_KEY_BYTES, &key_id);
    if (status != PSA_SUCCESS) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Failed to import key: %d", static_cast<int>(status));
        return false;
    }

    // PSA expects the tag concatenated immediately after the ciphertext.
    std::vector<uint8_t> combined(ciphertext_len + GCM_TAG_BYTES);
    std::memcpy(combined.data(), ciphertext, ciphertext_len);
    std::memcpy(combined.data() + ciphertext_len, tag, GCM_TAG_BYTES);

    size_t decrypted_size = 0;
    status = psa_aead_decrypt(
        key_id,
        PSA_ALG_GCM,
        nonce, GCM_NONCE_BYTES,
        nullptr, 0,  // no AAD
        combined.data(), combined.size(),
        plaintext_out, *plaintext_len,
        &decrypted_size);

    psa_destroy_key(key_id);

    if (status != PSA_SUCCESS) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Decryption or auth tag verification failed! Error: %d",
              static_cast<int>(status));
        return false;
    }

    *plaintext_len = decrypted_size;
    return true;
}

std::optional<std::vector<uint8_t>> aes_gcm_decrypt(const std::vector<uint8_t>& packet,
                                                    std::vector<uint8_t> key,
                                                    CryptoBackend& backend)
{
    if (key.size() != AES_KEY_BYTES) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Invalid key size: expected %u, got %u",
              static_cast<unsigned>(AES_KEY_BYTES),
              static_cast<unsigned>(key.size()));
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    if (packet.size() < GCM_NONCE_BYTES + GCM_TAG_BYTES) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Packet too short");
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    size_t plaintext_len = packet.size() - GCM_NONCE_BYTES - GCM_TAG_BYTES;
    std::vector<uint8_t> plaintext_out(plaintext_len);

    const bool ok = aes_gcm_decrypt(key.data(), packet.data(), packet.size(),
                                    plaintext_out.data(), &plaintext_len, backend);
    if (!ok) {
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    plaintext_out.resize(plaintext_len);
    return plaintext_out;
}

} // namespace encryption

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/general/crypto/ecdh-handshake.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#include "crypto.hpp"

#include <optional>
#include <vector>

namespace encryption {

EcdhHandshake::~EcdhHandshake() {
    if (key_id_ != 0) {
        psa_destroy_key(key_id_);
        key_id_ = 0;
    }
}

// Step 1: generate the local keypair and return our public key.
std::optional<std::vector<uint8_t>> EcdhHandshake::generatePublicKey(CryptoBackend& backend) {
    if (!backend.init()) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Crypto backend init failed");
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    // Destroy any previous (unconsumed) keypair: aborted handshakes must not
    // leak PSA key slots.
    if (key_id_ != 0) {
        psa_destroy_key(key_id_);
        key_id_ = 0;
    }

    psa_key_attributes_t attributes = PSA_KEY_ATTRIBUTES_INIT;
    psa_set_key_usage_flags(&attributes, PSA_KEY_USAGE_DERIVE);
    psa_set_key_algorithm(&attributes, PSA_ALG_ECDH);
    psa_set_key_type(&attributes, PSA_KEY_TYPE_ECC_KEY_PAIR(PSA_ECC_FAMILY_SECP_R1));
    psa_set_key_bits(&attributes, 256);

    psa_status_t status = psa_generate_key(&attributes, &key_id_);
    if (status != PSA_SUCCESS) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Failed to generate local ECDH key: %d", static_cast<int>(status));
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    // P-256 uncompressed public key max size is 65 bytes.
    std::vector<uint8_t> pub_key(65);
    size_t pub_len = 0;

    status = psa_export_public_key(key_id_, pub_key.data(), pub_key.size(), &pub_len);
    if (status != PSA_SUCCESS) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Failed to export local public key: %d", static_cast<int>(status));
        psa_destroy_key(key_id_);
        key_id_ = 0;
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    pub_key.resize(pub_len);
    return pub_key;
}

// Step 2: take the server's public key, derive the shared secret, drop the key.
std::optional<std::vector<uint8_t>> EcdhHandshake::computeSharedSecret(
    const std::vector<uint8_t>& peer_public_key, CryptoBackend& backend)
{
    if (!backend.init()) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Crypto backend init failed");
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    if (key_id_ == 0) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "No active local ECDH key found!");
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    // P-256 shared secret is always 32 bytes.
    std::vector<uint8_t> shared_secret(32);
    size_t secret_len = 0;

    psa_status_t status = psa_raw_key_agreement(
        PSA_ALG_ECDH,
        key_id_,
        peer_public_key.data(),
        peer_public_key.size(),
        shared_secret.data(),
        shared_secret.size(),
        &secret_len);

    if (status != PSA_SUCCESS) {
        // Keep the keypair: a stray/corrupt packet must not kill the pending
        // handshake. SessionManager retries and can still complete.
        LOG_E(TAG, "Failed to compute shared secret: %d", static_cast<int>(status));
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    psa_destroy_key(key_id_);
    key_id_ = 0;

    shared_secret.resize(secret_len);
    LOG_I(TAG, "Handshake complete! Shared secret derived successfully.");
    return shared_secret;
}

} // namespace encryption

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/general/crypto/encryption.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#include "crypto.hpp"

#include <cstring>

namespace encryption {

bool aes_gcm_encrypt(const uint8_t key[AES_KEY_BYTES],
                     const uint8_t* plaintext, size_t len,
                     uint8_t* out_buffer, size_t* out_len,
                     CryptoBackend& backend)
{
    if (!backend.init()) {
        return false;
    }

    if (*out_len < len + GCM_NONCE_BYTES + GCM_TAG_BYTES) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Buffer capacity too small");
        return false;
    }

    psa_key_attributes_t attributes = PSA_KEY_ATTRIBUTES_INIT;
    psa_set_key_usage_flags(&attributes, PSA_KEY_USAGE_ENCRYPT);
    psa_set_key_algorithm(&attributes, PSA_ALG_GCM);
    psa_set_key_type(&attributes, PSA_KEY_TYPE_AES);
    psa_set_key_bits(&attributes, AES_KEY_BYTES * 8);

    psa_key_id_t key_id;
    psa_status_t status = psa_import_key(&attributes, key, AES_KEY_BYTES, &key_id);
    if (status != PSA_SUCCESS) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Failed to import key: %d", static_cast<int>(status));
        return false;
    }

    uint8_t nonce[GCM_NONCE_BYTES];
    backend.fillRandom(nonce, GCM_NONCE_BYTES);

    std::vector<uint8_t> encrypted_payload(len + GCM_TAG_BYTES);
    size_t encrypted_output_len = 0;

    status = psa_aead_encrypt(
        key_id,
        PSA_ALG_GCM,
        nonce, GCM_NONCE_BYTES,
        nullptr, 0,  // no AAD
        plaintext, len,
        encrypted_payload.data(), encrypted_payload.size(),
        &encrypted_output_len);

    psa_destroy_key(key_id);

    if (status != PSA_SUCCESS) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Encryption failed: %d", static_cast<int>(status));
        return false;
    }

    // Output layout: [12B nonce | 16B tag | ciphertext]
    std::memcpy(out_buffer, nonce, GCM_NONCE_BYTES);
    std::memcpy(out_buffer + GCM_NONCE_BYTES, encrypted_payload.data() + len, GCM_TAG_BYTES);
    std::memcpy(out_buffer + GCM_NONCE_BYTES + GCM_TAG_BYTES, encrypted_payload.data(), len);

    *out_len = len + GCM_NONCE_BYTES + GCM_TAG_BYTES;
    return true;
}

std::optional<std::vector<uint8_t>> aes_gcm_encrypt(
    const std::vector<uint8_t>& plaintext, std::vector<uint8_t> aes_key,
    CryptoBackend& backend)
{
    if (aes_key.size() != AES_KEY_BYTES) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Invalid key size: expected %u, got %u",
              static_cast<unsigned>(AES_KEY_BYTES),
              static_cast<unsigned>(aes_key.size()));
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    size_t out_len = plaintext.size() + GCM_NONCE_BYTES + GCM_TAG_BYTES;
    std::vector<uint8_t> out_buffer(out_len);

    const bool ok = aes_gcm_encrypt(aes_key.data(), plaintext.data(), plaintext.size(),
                                    out_buffer.data(), &out_len, backend);
    if (!ok) {
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    out_buffer.resize(out_len);
    return out_buffer;
}

} // namespace encryption

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/general/fs/directory_lister.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#include "general/fs/directory_lister.hpp"

#include <cstring>
#include <dirent.h>
#include <strings.h>

namespace general::fs {

std::vector<std::string> listFiles(const std::string& directory,
                                   const std::string& extension,
                                   std::size_t max_files) {
    std::vector<std::string> files;

    DIR* dir = ::opendir(directory.c_str());
    if (dir == nullptr) {
        return files;
    }

    struct dirent* entry;
    while ((entry = ::readdir(dir)) != nullptr) {
        if (max_files != 0 && files.size() >= max_files) {
            break;
        }
        if (std::strcmp(entry->d_name, ".") == 0 || std::strcmp(entry->d_name, "..") == 0) {
            continue;
        }

        const char* dot = std::strrchr(entry->d_name, '.');
        if (dot == nullptr || dot == entry->d_name) {
            continue;
        }
        if (::strcasecmp(dot, extension.c_str()) != 0) {
            continue;
        }

        files.emplace_back(entry->d_name);
    }

    ::closedir(dir);
    return files;
}

} // namespace general::fs

namespace general::fs {

// Returns matching file names (not full paths). max_files == 0 means no limit.

                                   const std::string& extension,
                                   std::size_t max_files);
