--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/general/input/button_state_machine.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#include "general/input/button_state_machine.hpp"

namespace general::input {

ButtonStateMachine::ButtonStateMachine(uint32_t long_press_ms)
    : long_press_ms_(long_press_ms) {}

ButtonEvent ButtonStateMachine::update(bool pressed, uint32_t now_ms) {
    ButtonEvent event = ButtonEvent::None;
    pressed_ = pressed;

    if (pressed_ && !last_pressed_) {
        // Just pressed: remember when.
        press_start_ms_ = now_ms;
        long_triggered_ = false;
    } else if (!pressed_ && last_pressed_) {
        // Released: a short press only if the long press did not fire.
        const uint32_t press_time = now_ms - press_start_ms_;
        if (press_time < long_press_ms_ && !long_triggered_) {
            event = ButtonEvent::ShortPress;
        }
    } else if (pressed_ && !long_triggered_) {
        // Held down: fire the long press exactly once.
        if (now_ms - press_start_ms_ >= long_press_ms_) {
            event = ButtonEvent::LongPress;
            long_triggered_ = true;
        }
    }

    last_pressed_ = pressed_;
    return event;
}

} // namespace general::input

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/general/input/button_state_machine.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

// Portable button debounce + short/long-press state machine.
// The hardware layer only samples the pin and feeds (pressed, now_ms).

#include <cstdint>

namespace general::input {

enum class ButtonEvent {
    None,
    ShortPress,
    LongPress,
};

class ButtonStateMachine {
public:
    explicit ButtonStateMachine(uint32_t long_press_ms = 1000);

    // Call on every sample. Returns the event produced by this transition.
    ButtonEvent update(bool pressed, uint32_t now_ms);

private:
    uint32_t long_press_ms_;
    bool     pressed_ = false;
    bool     last_pressed_ = false;
    bool     long_triggered_ = false;
    uint32_t press_start_ms_ = 0;
};

} // namespace general::input

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/general/io/path.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

// Portable path joining (VFS-style, no std::filesystem dependency).

#include <string>

namespace general::io {

inline std::string join(const std::string& base, const std::string& name) {
    if (name.empty()) {
        return base;
    }
    if (name.front() == '/') {
        return base + name;
    }
    if (base.empty()) {
        return name;
    }
    if (base.back() == '/') {
        return base + name;
    }
    return base + "/" + name;
}

} // namespace general::io

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/general/io/random_access_file.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------


#include "general/io/random_access_file.hpp" // Includes RandomAccessFile class declaration
#include "general/log.hpp"       // Portable logging (general/ must not use ESP-IDF)
#include <unistd.h>    // POSIX ftruncate() and unlink()
#include <algorithm>       // std::min and std::max
#include <cerrno>          // System errno variable
#include <cstring>         // strerror()
#include <sys/stat.h>      // POSIX stat and fstat interfaces
#include <memory>          // std::unique_ptr for safe heap transfer buffers

namespace general::io { // Opens FileManager namespace implementation

static const char* TAG = "RandomAccessFile"; // Log filter tag used in ESP_LOG output

RandomAccessFile::RandomAccessFile(const std::string& full_path) // Constructor binding single file
    : m_fullPath(full_path) { // Stores the absolute path supplied by the caller
    openInternal(); // Automatically opens file so caller doesn't have to manage open states
} // Closes constructor definition

RandomAccessFile::~RandomAccessFile() { // Destructor
    if (m_file) { // Checks if file descriptor is open
        fflush(m_file); // Flushes any pending uncommitted data to flash
        fclose(m_file); // Releases the underlying system file descriptor
        m_file = nullptr; // Clears the pointer
    } // Closes open handle check
} // Closes destructor definition

RandomAccessFile::RandomAccessFile(RandomAccessFile&& other) noexcept // Move constructor
    : m_file(other.m_file), // Takes ownership of other's file pointer
      m_fullPath(std::move(other.m_fullPath)) { // Takes ownership of other's full path string
    other.m_file = nullptr; // Nullifies other's pointer to prevent it from closing the file
} // Closes move constructor definition

RandomAccessFile& RandomAccessFile::operator=(RandomAccessFile&& other) noexcept { // Move assignment operator
    if (this != &other) { // Protects against self-assignment
        if (m_file) { // Checks if this instance currently holds an open file
            fflush(m_file); // Flushes current file buffers
            fclose(m_file); // Closes current file descriptor
        } // Closes open check
        m_file = other.m_file; // Adopts new file descriptor
        m_fullPath = std::move(other.m_fullPath); // Adopts new full path
        other.m_file = nullptr; // Invalidates source object's pointer
    } // Closes self-assignment check
    return *this; // Returns reference to self
} // Closes move assignment definition

bool RandomAccessFile::openInternal() { // Internal helper that transparently manages opening
    if (m_file) return true; // File is already open; reuse handle directly

    errno = 0;
    m_file = fopen(m_fullPath.c_str(), "r+b"); // Attempts to open existing file for read/update
    if (!m_file && errno == ENOENT) { // Only create when it truly does not exist
        // A transient open error must never truncate an existing file.
        m_file = fopen(m_fullPath.c_str(), "w+b"); // Create and open for read/write
    } // Closes fallback check

    if (!m_file) { // Checks if both attempts failed
        LOG_E(TAG, "Failed to open or create file '%s' (errno %d: %s)", m_fullPath.c_str(), errno, strerror(errno)); // Logs failure
        return false; // Returns failure
    } // Closes validation check

    return true; // Returns success
} // Closes openInternal definition

bool RandomAccessFile::seek(long offset, int whence) { // Internal cursor positioning wrapper
    if (!openInternal()) return false; // Ensures file is open prior to seeking
    return (fseek(m_file, offset, whence) == 0); // Performs fseek and returns true on success
} // Closes seek definition

size_t RandomAccessFile::fileSize() {
    if (!openInternal()) return 0;

    // 1. Flush any pending libc buffers so ftell reflects all written bytes
    fflush(m_file);

    // 2. Remember current stream cursor
    long currentPos = ftell(m_file);

    // 3. Seek to EOF to measure length
    if (fseek(m_file, 0, SEEK_END) != 0) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "fseek(SEEK_END) failed on '%s' (errno %d: %s)", m_fullPath.c_str(), errno, strerror(errno));
        return 0;
    }

    long size = ftell(m_file);

    // 4. Restore original cursor position so we don't disrupt user's read/write offset
    if (currentPos >= 0) {
        fseek(m_file, currentPos, SEEK_SET);
    }

    return (size >= 0) ? static_cast<size_t>(size) : 0;
}
// Zero-copy internal write helper shared by both std::string and std::vector overloads
static bool writeRaw(FILE* file, const uint8_t* dataPtr, size_t totalBytes, long offset, RandomAccessFile* self) {
    // Avoid double fflush: self->fileSize() already flushes libc buffers to flash
    long currentSize = static_cast<long>(self->fileSize()); // Queries true file size

    // Map negative offset to append directly at EOF
    if (offset < 0) {
        offset = currentSize; // Clamps to end of file
    } // Closes negative offset handling

    // Offset exceeds EOF: fill intermediate gap with zeros to avoid undefined regions
    if (offset > currentSize) {
        if (!self->seek(0, SEEK_END)) { // Positions cursor to current EOF
            LOG_E(TAG, "Failed seeking to EOF for padding (errno %d: %s)", errno, strerror(errno)); // Logs seek failure
            return false; // Aborts on seek failure
        } // Closes seek EOF check

        long gap = offset - currentSize; // Computes number of zero bytes to pad
        constexpr size_t ZERO_BUF_SIZE = 256; // Stack-safe zero padding buffer
        const uint8_t zeroBuffer[ZERO_BUF_SIZE] = {0}; // Zero-initialized memory block

        while (gap > 0) { // Pads until target offset is reached
            size_t toWrite = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(gap), ZERO_BUF_SIZE); // Clamps to buffer size
            size_t written = fwrite(zeroBuffer, 1, toWrite, file); // Writes zeros to flash
            if (written != toWrite) { // Checks for short write error
                LOG_E(TAG, "Failed to write zero padding: wrote %zu of %zu (errno %d: %s)", written, toWrite, errno, strerror(errno)); // Logs error
                return false; // Aborts on write failure
            } // Closes write validation
            gap -= toWrite; // Decrements remaining gap
        } // Closes gap padding loop
    } else {
        // Seek to target write offset only if we didn't just pad up to it
        if (!self->seek(offset, SEEK_SET)) { // Moves file pointer to target write location
            LOG_E(TAG, "Failed seeking to offset %ld (errno %d: %s)", offset, errno, strerror(errno)); // Logs seek failure
            return false; // Aborts on seek failure
        } // Closes seek check
    } // Closes offset branch

    // Write actual data in 4 KB chunks matching LittleFS sector/erase boundaries
    if (totalBytes > 0 && dataPtr != nullptr) { // Verifies non-empty payload
        size_t bytesWrittenTotal = 0; // Running total of written bytes
        constexpr size_t CHUNK_SIZE = 4096; // 4 KB chunk size matching LittleFS block structure

        while (bytesWrittenTotal < totalBytes) { // Chunks data to minimize flash fragmentation
            size_t chunkBytes = std::min(CHUNK_SIZE, totalBytes - bytesWrittenTotal); // Clamps chunk
            size_t written = fwrite(dataPtr + bytesWrittenTotal, 1, chunkBytes, file); // Performs actual write

            if (written != chunkBytes) { // Checks if driver accepted all chunk bytes
                LOG_E(TAG, "Short write: wrote %zu of %zu bytes (total written: %zu, errno %d: %s)",
                         written, chunkBytes, bytesWrittenTotal, errno, strerror(errno)); // Logs error
                return false; // Aborts write sequence
            } // Closes short write check
            bytesWrittenTotal += written; // Advances total written count
        } // Closes chunk loop
    } // Closes payload check

    return true; // Write completed successfully
} // Closes writeRaw helper

bool RandomAccessFile::writeFile(const std::string& data, long offset) { // String overload with ZERO heap allocation
    if (!openInternal()) return false; // Ensures file handle is active
    return writeRaw(m_file, reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(data.data()), data.size(), offset, this); // Zero-copy delegation
} // Closes string writeFile definition

bool RandomAccessFile::writeFile(const std::vector<uint8_t>& data, long offset) { // Binary vector overload
    if (!openInternal()) return false; // Ensures file handle is active
    return writeRaw(m_file, data.data(), data.size(), offset, this); // Directly forwards binary pointer
} // Closes vector writeFile definition

std::vector<uint8_t> RandomAccessFile::readFileBytes(long offset, long size) { // Core read implementation
    if (!openInternal()) return {}; // Auto-opens file if needed; returns empty on fail

    long totalLength = static_cast<long>(fileSize()); // Queries file length (automatically flushed)
    if (totalLength <= 0) { // Checks if file is empty
        return {}; // Early exit without error if file has no content
    } // Closes empty file check

    if (offset < 0 || offset >= totalLength) { // Validates offset bounds
        LOG_W(TAG, "Read offset %ld out of bounds for '%s' (file size: %ld)", offset, m_fullPath.c_str(), totalLength); // Warns of out-of-bounds read
        return {}; // Returns empty vector on invalid bounds
    } // Closes bounds check

    size_t bytesToRead = 0; // Initializes read counter
    if (size < 0) { // If negative size, read everything up to EOF
        bytesToRead = static_cast<size_t>(totalLength - offset); // Calculates remaining bytes
    } else { // Explicit read size requested
        bytesToRead = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(size), static_cast<size_t>(totalLength - offset)); // Clamps to EOF
    } // Closes size calculation

    if (bytesToRead == 0) { // Avoids allocating empty vector or seeking
        return {}; // Early return for zero-byte read requests
    } // Closes zero check

    if (!seek(offset, SEEK_SET)) { // Positions cursor to starting offset
        LOG_E(TAG, "Failed seeking to offset %ld in '%s' (errno %d: %s)", offset, m_fullPath.c_str(), errno, strerror(errno)); // Logs seek failure
        return {}; // Returns empty if seek failed
    } // Closes seek check

    std::vector<uint8_t> result(bytesToRead); // Allocates output container only when ready to read
    size_t readCount = fread(result.data(), 1, bytesToRead, m_file); // Reads bytes from flash

    if (readCount != bytesToRead) { // Detects short read / unexpected premature EOF
        LOG_W(TAG, "Short read on '%s': expected %zu bytes, read %zu bytes (errno %d: %s)",
                 m_fullPath.c_str(), bytesToRead, readCount, errno, strerror(errno)); // Warns caller of truncated read
        result.resize(readCount); // Resizes buffer down to actual bytes read
    } // Closes short read check

    return result; // Returns read byte data
} // Closes readFileBytes definition


void RandomAccessFile::shiftBytes(size_t shiftDistance, bool left, long long startOffset, long long endOffset) {
    if (shiftDistance == 0) return;

    long currentFileSize = static_cast<long>(fileSize());
    if (currentFileSize <= 0) return;

    long actualStart = (startOffset < 0) ? 0 : static_cast<long>(startOffset);
    long actualEnd = (endOffset < 0 || endOffset > currentFileSize) ? currentFileSize : static_cast<long>(endOffset);

    if (actualStart >= currentFileSize || actualStart >= actualEnd) return;

    if (left && static_cast<long>(shiftDistance) > actualStart) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Cannot shift left: underflow offset 0");
        return;
    }

  
    // 1. Close current file handle properly
    if (m_file) {
        fclose(m_file);
        m_file = nullptr;
    }

    // Use your class's path variable/getter here:
    // (Replace `m_filename` with whichever variable holds the path in RandomAccessFile, e.g., m_filePath or getPath())
    std::string filePath = m_fullPath; 
    std::string tmpPath = filePath + ".tmp";
 
    FILE* src = fopen(filePath.c_str(), "rb");
    FILE* dst = fopen(tmpPath.c_str(), "wb");
    if (!src || !dst) {
                LOG_E(TAG, "shiftBytes fopen failed! src: %p, dst: %p, path: '%s' (errno %d: %s)",
                 src, dst, filePath.c_str(), errno, strerror(errno));
        if (src) fclose(src);
        if (dst) fclose(dst);
        unlink(tmpPath.c_str());   // drop a possibly-created temp file
        openInternal();
        return;
    }

    constexpr size_t BUF_SIZE = 4096; // 4 KB matches flash block size
    auto buf = std::unique_ptr<uint8_t[]>(new (std::nothrow) uint8_t[BUF_SIZE]);
    if (!buf) {
        fclose(src);
        fclose(dst);
        unlink(tmpPath.c_str());
        openInternal();
        return;
    }

    bool write_failed = false;
    auto copyRange = [&](long from, long to) {
        fseek(src, from, SEEK_SET);
        long remaining = to - from;
        while (remaining > 0 && !write_failed) {
            size_t n = std::min<size_t>(remaining, BUF_SIZE);
            size_t r = fread(buf.get(), 1, n, src);
            if (r == 0) {
                // Short read or error: never rename a truncated temp file
                // over the original.
                write_failed = true;
                break;
            }
            size_t w = fwrite(buf.get(), 1, r, dst);
            if (w != r) {
                write_failed = true;
                break;
            }
            remaining -= r;
        }
    };

    if (left) {
        // 1. Data before the destination: [0, actualStart - shiftDistance)
        if (actualStart > static_cast<long>(shiftDistance)) {
            copyRange(0, actualStart - shiftDistance);
        }
        // 2. The shifted payload: [actualStart, actualEnd) -> written sequentially!
        copyRange(actualStart, actualEnd);
        // 3. Any trailing data after actualEnd: [actualEnd, currentFileSize)
        if (actualEnd < currentFileSize) {
            copyRange(actualEnd, currentFileSize);
        }
    } else {
        // 1. Unchanged prefix: [0, actualStart)
        if (actualStart > 0) {
            copyRange(0, actualStart);
        }
        // 2. The insertion hole/gap: filled with zeros
        std::vector<uint8_t> zeros(std::min<size_t>(shiftDistance, BUF_SIZE), 0x00);
        size_t holeLeft = shiftDistance;
        while (holeLeft > 0 && !write_failed) {
            size_t n = std::min(holeLeft, zeros.size());
            size_t w = fwrite(zeros.data(), 1, n, dst);
            if (w != n) {
                write_failed = true;
                break;
            }
            holeLeft -= n;
        }
        // 3. The shifted payload: [actualStart, actualEnd)
        copyRange(actualStart, actualEnd);
        // 4. Any trailing data after actualEnd: [actualEnd, currentFileSize)
        if (actualEnd < currentFileSize) {
            copyRange(actualEnd, currentFileSize);
        }
    }

    fflush(dst);
    if (ferror(dst)) {
        write_failed = true;
    }
    fclose(src);
    fclose(dst);

    if (write_failed) {
        // Keep the original file untouched and drop the partial temp file.
        LOG_E(TAG, "shiftBytes write failed; keeping original '%s'", filePath.c_str());
        unlink(tmpPath.c_str());
        openInternal();
        return;
    }

    // Atomic replace: rename() overwrites the destination on POSIX and
    // LittleFS, so a power loss cannot leave the file missing.
    if (rename(tmpPath.c_str(), filePath.c_str()) != 0) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "shiftBytes rename failed: '%s' (errno %d: %s)",
              filePath.c_str(), errno, strerror(errno));
        unlink(tmpPath.c_str());
        openInternal();
        return;
    }

    // Reopen handle for caller
    openInternal();
}
bool RandomAccessFile::truncate(size_t newSize) { // Truncates or extends file using LittleFS ftruncate
    if (!openInternal()) return false; // Auto-opens file if needed
    fflush(m_file); // Flushes stdio stream buffers before applying low-level truncate
    if (ftruncate(fileno(m_file), newSize) != 0) { // Invokes POSIX ftruncate via native file descriptor
        LOG_E(TAG, "ftruncate to size %zu failed on '%s' (errno %d: %s)", newSize, m_fullPath.c_str(), errno, strerror(errno)); // Logs truncate failure
        return false; // Returns failure
    } // Closes ftruncate check
    return true; // Returns success
} // Closes truncate definition

bool RandomAccessFile::deleteFile() { // Deletes this file
    if (m_file) { // If handle is active
        fclose(m_file); // Must close handle first before deleting file on filesystem
        m_file = nullptr; // Resets pointer to null
    } // Closes active handle check
    return ::unlink(m_fullPath.c_str()) == 0; // POSIX unlink on the absolute path
} // Closes deleteFile definition

void RandomAccessFile::flush() { // Flushes pending unwritten data to flash
    if (m_file) { // Protects against null-pointer call which would flush all global streams
        fflush(m_file); // Flushes only this specific file stream
    } // Closes null check
} // Closes flush definition

} // namespace general::io // Closes namespace
--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/general/io/random_access_file.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once // Ensures this header file is included only once during compilation

#include <string>           // Provides std::string for data and path representation
#include <vector>           // Provides std::vector for raw binary byte buffers
#include <cstdint>          // Provides standard integer types such as uint8_t
#include <cstdio>           // Provides standard C file I/O types (FILE*)
#include <cstddef>          // Provides standard size types such as size_t

namespace general::io { // Encloses the class within the FileManager namespace

class RandomAccessFile { // Dedicated object encapsulating persistent state and I/O for a single file
public:
    // Binds this instance to an absolute path and automatically opens/creates it.
    explicit RandomAccessFile(const std::string& full_path); // Caller supplies the absolute path once here

    // Destructor that automatically flushes unwritten data and closes the open descriptor (RAII)
    ~RandomAccessFile(); // Ensures the file handle is safely closed when the object goes out of scope

    // Disable copy semantics to prevent two instances from holding and closing the same FILE* handle
    RandomAccessFile(const RandomAccessFile&) = delete; // Prevents copying of the file instance
    RandomAccessFile& operator=(const RandomAccessFile&) = delete; // Prevents copy assignment

    // Enable move semantics to allow transferring file handle ownership between functions/scopes
    RandomAccessFile(RandomAccessFile&& other) noexcept; // Move constructor transfers pointer and path
    RandomAccessFile& operator=(RandomAccessFile&& other) noexcept; // Move assignment operator transfers ownership

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Core APIs
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    // Writes string data starting at offset (>= 0 for seek with zero-padding, < 0 for append)
    bool writeFile(const std::string& data, long offset = 0); // Writes string buffer to file

    // Writes binary byte buffer starting at offset (>= 0 for seek with zero-padding, < 0 for append)
    bool writeFile(const std::vector<uint8_t>& data, long offset = 0); // Writes byte array to file

    // Reads binary bytes starting from offset (size < 0 reads until end-of-file)
    std::vector<uint8_t> readFileBytes(long offset = 0, long size = -1); // Reads chunk from file

    void shiftBytes(size_t shiftDistance, bool left, long long startOffset = -1, long long endOffset = -1); // Moves byte ranges

    // Truncates or extends the file to the specified byte size
    bool truncate(size_t newSize); // Adjusts physical file size

    // Closes the internal handle and deletes the file from storage
    bool deleteFile(); // Deletes this file from flash storage

    // Computes and returns the current total byte size of the file
    size_t fileSize(); // Returns file size in bytes
    void flush();
    bool seek(long offset, int whence = SEEK_SET); // Internal helper to position file cursor

private:
    FILE* m_file = nullptr; // Persistent standard C file stream handle
    std::string m_fullPath; // Absolute path bound to this object

    bool openInternal(); // Internal helper to open or create file if not currently open

}; // Closes RandomAccessFile class declaration

} // namespace general::io // Closes namespace
--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/general/io/stdio_file_store.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#include "general/io/stdio_file_store.hpp"

#include <cstdio>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <unistd.h>

namespace general::io {

bool StdioFileStore::exists(const std::string& full_path) {
    struct stat st;
    return ::stat(full_path.c_str(), &st) == 0;
}

bool StdioFileStore::remove(const std::string& full_path) {
    return ::unlink(full_path.c_str()) == 0;
}

bool StdioFileStore::readAll(const std::string& full_path, std::vector<uint8_t>& out) {
    FILE* f = std::fopen(full_path.c_str(), "rb");
    if (f == nullptr) {
        return false;
    }
    std::fseek(f, 0, SEEK_END);
    const long size = std::ftell(f);
    std::rewind(f);
    if (size < 0) {
        std::fclose(f);
        return false;
    }
    out.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(size));
    const std::size_t read = out.empty() ? 0 : std::fread(out.data(), 1, out.size(), f);
    std::fclose(f);
    return read == out.size();
}

bool StdioFileStore::writeAll(const std::string& full_path, const std::vector<uint8_t>& data) {
    FILE* f = std::fopen(full_path.c_str(), "wb");
    if (f == nullptr) {
        return false;
    }
    const std::size_t written = data.empty() ? 0 : std::fwrite(data.data(), 1, data.size(), f);
    std::fclose(f);
    return written == data.size();
}

} // namespace general::io

#pragma once

// A datagram channel used by the portable KCP session.
// Implementations: ESP UDP socket, PPP, in-memory test loop, ...

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>

namespace general::kcp {

struct DatagramIo {
    virtual ~DatagramIo() = default;

    // Returns 0 on success, < 0 on failure (a non-blocking drop is a failure).
    virtual int send(const uint8_t* data, std::size_t len) = 0;

    // Returns > 0 bytes read, 0 when no datagram is available, < 0 on error.
    virtual int receive(uint8_t* buffer, std::size_t capacity) = 0;
};

} // namespace general::kcp

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/general/kcp/kcp_session.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#include "general/kcp/kcp_session.hpp"

namespace general::kcp {

KcpSession::KcpSession(DatagramIo& io, const KcpConfig& config)
    : io_(io), config_(config) {}

KcpSession::~KcpSession() {
    close();
}

bool KcpSession::open() {
    if (kcp_ != nullptr) {
        return true;
    }

    kcp_ = ikcp_create(config_.conversation_id, this);
    if (kcp_ == nullptr) {
        return false;
    }

    ikcp_setoutput(kcp_, &KcpSession::outputCallback);
    ikcp_nodelay(kcp_, config_.nodelay, config_.interval_ms,
                 config_.resend, config_.no_congestion);
    ikcp_wndsize(kcp_, config_.send_window, config_.receive_window);
    return true;
}

void KcpSession::close() {
    if (kcp_ != nullptr) {
        ikcp_release(kcp_);
        kcp_ = nullptr;
    }
}

int KcpSession::outputCallback(const char* buf, int len, ikcpcb* /*kcp*/, void* user) {
    auto* self = static_cast<KcpSession*>(user);
    if (self == nullptr || buf == nullptr || len <= 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    return self->io_.send(reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(buf),
                          static_cast<std::size_t>(len));
}

int KcpSession::send(const uint8_t* data, std::size_t size) {
    if (kcp_ == nullptr || data == nullptr || size == 0) {
        return -1;
    }
    return ikcp_send(kcp_, reinterpret_cast<const char*>(data),
                     static_cast<int>(size));
}

std::vector<uint8_t> KcpSession::poll(uint32_t now_ms) {
    if (kcp_ == nullptr) {
        return {};
    }

    // Drain available datagrams (bounded so a UDP flood cannot starve the
    // worker task) and only count VALID KCP input as liveness.
    uint8_t rx_buffer[1500];
    constexpr int kMaxDatagramsPerPoll = 32;
    for (int datagrams = 0; datagrams < kMaxDatagramsPerPoll; ++datagrams) {
        const int received = io_.receive(rx_buffer, sizeof(rx_buffer));
        if (received <= 0) {
            break;
        }
        const int err = ikcp_input(kcp_, reinterpret_cast<const char*>(rx_buffer), received);
        if (err >= 0) {
            last_rx_ms_ = now_ms;
        }
    }

    const int peek_size = ikcp_peeksize(kcp_);
    if (peek_size <= 0) {
        return {};
    }

    std::vector<uint8_t> buffer(static_cast<std::size_t>(peek_size));
    const int bytes_read = ikcp_recv(kcp_, reinterpret_cast<char*>(buffer.data()),
                                     static_cast<int>(buffer.size()));
    if (bytes_read < 0) {
        return {};
    }

    buffer.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(bytes_read));
    return buffer;
}

bool KcpSession::isDead() const {
    return kcp_ != nullptr && kcp_->state == static_cast<IUINT32>(-1);
}

void KcpSession::update(uint32_t now_ms) {
    if (kcp_ != nullptr) {
        ikcp_update(kcp_, now_ms);
    }
}

} // namespace general::kcp

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/general/kcp/kcp_session.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

// Portable KCP session: control block, fast-mode tuning, send/input/reassembly
// and liveness timestamp. Transport I/O is injected through DatagramIo.

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <vector>

#include "general/kcp/datagram_io.hpp"
#include "ikcp.h"

namespace general::kcp {

struct KcpConfig {
    uint32_t conversation_id = 0;

    // ikcp_nodelay(nodelay, interval_ms, resend, nc)
    // nc disables congestion control ("no cwnd", fast mode).
    int nodelay       = 1;
    int interval_ms   = 10;
    int resend        = 2;
    int no_congestion = 1;

    // ikcp_wndsize(send, receive)
    int send_window    = 128;
    int receive_window = 128;
};

class KcpSession {
public:
    KcpSession(DatagramIo& io, const KcpConfig& config);
    ~KcpSession();

    KcpSession(const KcpSession&) = delete;
    KcpSession& operator=(const KcpSession&) = delete;

    bool open();
    void close();
    bool isOpen() const { return kcp_ != nullptr; }

    // True once ikcp reached its dead_link retransmission limit.
    bool isDead() const;

    int send(const uint8_t* data, std::size_t size);

    // Reads at most one datagram, feeds KCP, and returns one reassembled message
    // (empty when nothing is ready). now_ms is used for the liveness timestamp.
    std::vector<uint8_t> poll(uint32_t now_ms);

    void update(uint32_t now_ms);

    uint32_t lastRxMs() const { return last_rx_ms_; }

private:
    static int outputCallback(const char* buf, int len, ikcpcb* kcp, void* user);

    DatagramIo& io_;
    KcpConfig   config_;
    ikcpcb*     kcp_ = nullptr;
    uint32_t    last_rx_ms_ = 0;
};

} // namespace general::kcp

FILE: main/general/net/udp_socket.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

// Portable POSIX UDP socket helpers (getaddrinfo / socket / sendto / recvfrom).
// Works on ESP-IDF lwIP and on any POSIX host; no ESP-IDF includes. The
// hardware layer consumes this through its DatagramIo adapter.

#include <arpa/inet.h>
#include <cerrno>
#include <cstring>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <netdb.h>
#include <optional>
#include <sys/socket.h>
#include <unistd.h>

#include "general/log.hpp"

namespace general::net {

static const char* TAG = "UDP";

// Socket handle + resolved destination.
struct UdpConnection {
    int sock = -1;
    struct sockaddr_storage addr = {};
    socklen_t addr_len = 0;

    bool is_valid() const { return sock >= 0; }
};

inline bool makeSocketNonBlocking(int sock) {
    const int flags = fcntl(sock, F_GETFL, 0);
    if (flags < 0) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Failed to get socket flags: %d", errno);
        return false;
    }
    if (fcntl(sock, F_SETFL, flags | O_NONBLOCK) < 0) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Failed to set socket to non-blocking: %d", errno);
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

// Resolves the host (IPv4/IPv6), creates a non-blocking UDP socket.
inline std::optional<UdpConnection> openUdpConnection(const char* host, const char* port) {
    struct addrinfo hints = {};
    hints.ai_family = AF_UNSPEC;
    hints.ai_socktype = SOCK_DGRAM;
    hints.ai_protocol = IPPROTO_UDP;

    struct addrinfo* res = nullptr;
    const int err = getaddrinfo(host, port, &hints, &res);
    if (err != 0 || res == nullptr) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Failed to resolve hostname %s: error %d", host, err);
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    const int sock = ::socket(res->ai_family, res->ai_socktype, res->ai_protocol);
    if (sock < 0) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Failed to create socket for family %d", res->ai_family);
        freeaddrinfo(res);
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    if (!makeSocketNonBlocking(sock)) {
        ::close(sock);
        freeaddrinfo(res);
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    UdpConnection conn;
    conn.sock = sock;
    std::memcpy(&conn.addr, res->ai_addr, res->ai_addrlen);
    conn.addr_len = res->ai_addrlen;

    // Connected UDP: only datagrams from the resolved peer are delivered, so
    // stray LAN traffic never reaches ikcp_input().
    if (::connect(sock, reinterpret_cast<const struct sockaddr*>(&conn.addr),
                  conn.addr_len) != 0) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Failed to connect UDP socket: %d", errno);
        ::close(sock);
        freeaddrinfo(res);
        return std::nullopt;
    }

    freeaddrinfo(res);
    return conn;
}

// Returns 0 on success, -1 on failure (a non-blocking drop is a failure).
inline int udpSend(const UdpConnection& conn, const uint8_t* data, size_t len) {
    if (!conn.is_valid() || data == nullptr || len == 0) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Invalid parameters for send");
        return -1;
    }

    ssize_t sent;
    do {
        sent = sendto(conn.sock, data, len, 0,
                      reinterpret_cast<const struct sockaddr*>(&conn.addr),
                      conn.addr_len);
    } while (sent < 0 && errno == EINTR);

    if (sent < 0) {
        return -1;   // includes EWOULDBLOCK/EAGAIN (buffer full -> drop)
    }
    return (static_cast<size_t>(sent) == len) ? 0 : -1;
}

// Returns > 0 bytes, 0 when no datagram is available, -1 on error.
inline int udpReceive(int sock, uint8_t* buffer, size_t capacity,
                      struct sockaddr_storage* src_addr, socklen_t* src_addr_len) {
    if (sock < 0 || buffer == nullptr || capacity == 0 ||
        src_addr == nullptr || src_addr_len == nullptr) {
        return -1;
    }

    ssize_t received;
    do {
        received = recvfrom(sock, buffer, capacity, 0,
                            reinterpret_cast<struct sockaddr*>(src_addr),
                            src_addr_len);
    } while (received < 0 && errno == EINTR);

    if (received < 0) {
        return (errno == EWOULDBLOCK || errno == EAGAIN) ? 0 : -1;
    }
    return static_cast<int>(received);
}

} // namespace general::net

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/general/opus/Opus.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#include "Opus.hpp"
#include "general/log.hpp"  // Portable logging: general/ must not use ESP-IDF.

#include <cstring>

static const char* TAG = "Opus";
static constexpr int MAX_PACKET_SIZE = 1000;

Opus::Opus()
    : encoder_(nullptr),
      channelCount_(0),
      bitsPerChannel_(0),
      frameSize_(0) {}

Opus::~Opus() {
    release();
}

bool Opus::init(int sampleRate, int channelCount, int bitsPerChannel,
                int targetFrameDurationMs) {
    release(); // Releases any previous encoder.

    const bool validSampleRate =
        sampleRate == 8000 || sampleRate == 12000 ||
        sampleRate == 16000 || sampleRate == 24000 ||
        sampleRate == 48000;
    const bool validChannels = channelCount == 1 || channelCount == 2;
    const bool validBitDepth = bitsPerChannel == 16 || bitsPerChannel == 32;
    const bool validDuration =
        targetFrameDurationMs == 5 || targetFrameDurationMs == 10 ||
        targetFrameDurationMs == 20 || targetFrameDurationMs == 40 ||
        targetFrameDurationMs == 60;

    if (!validSampleRate || !validChannels || !validBitDepth || !validDuration) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Invalid Opus configuration");
        return false;
    }

    frameSize_ = sampleRate * targetFrameDurationMs / 1000;

    int error = OPUS_OK;
    encoder_ = opus_encoder_create(sampleRate, channelCount, OPUS_APPLICATION_AUDIO, &error);

    if (error != OPUS_OK || encoder_ == nullptr) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Encoder creation failed: %s", opus_strerror(error));
        encoder_ = nullptr;
        frameSize_ = 0;
        return false;
    }

    bitsPerChannel_ = bitsPerChannel;
    channelCount_ = channelCount;
    LOG_I(TAG, "Encoder initialized: %d Hz, %d channel(s), %d-bit PCM, frame size %d",
          sampleRate, channelCount, bitsPerChannel_, frameSize_);

    int complexity = 3;
    int err = opus_encoder_ctl(encoder_, OPUS_SET_COMPLEXITY(complexity));
    if (err != OPUS_OK) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "opus_encoder_ctl failed: %s", opus_strerror(err));
    }

    return true;
}

std::vector<unsigned char> Opus::encode(const void* pcmData) {
    if (encoder_ == nullptr || pcmData == nullptr) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Encoder is uninitialized or PCM input is null");
        return {};
    }

    // Reuse the scratch packet buffer: no per-frame allocation.
    packet_scratch_.assign(MAX_PACKET_SIZE, 0);

    int bytesWritten = 0;

    if (bitsPerChannel_ == 16) {
        bytesWritten = opus_encode(
            encoder_,
            static_cast<const opus_int16*>(pcmData),
            frameSize_,
            packet_scratch_.data(),
            static_cast<opus_int32>(packet_scratch_.size()));
    } else if (bitsPerChannel_ == 32) {
        // Interpret input as signed 32-bit PCM. memcpy avoids strict-aliasing
        // UB on the caller's buffer.
        const auto* raw = static_cast<const uint8_t*>(pcmData);
        const std::size_t sampleCount =
            static_cast<std::size_t>(frameSize_) *
            static_cast<std::size_t>(channelCount_);

        float_scratch_.resize(sampleCount);
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < sampleCount; ++i) {
            int32_t sample = 0;
            std::memcpy(&sample, raw + i * sizeof(int32_t), sizeof(int32_t));
            float_scratch_[i] = static_cast<float>(sample) / 2147483648.0f;
        }

        bytesWritten = opus_encode_float(
            encoder_,
            float_scratch_.data(),
            frameSize_,
            packet_scratch_.data(),
            static_cast<opus_int32>(packet_scratch_.size()));
    } else {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Unsupported PCM bit depth: %d", bitsPerChannel_);
        return {};
    }

    if (bytesWritten < 0) {
        LOG_E(TAG, "Encoding failed: %s", opus_strerror(bytesWritten));
        return {};
    }

    packet_scratch_.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(bytesWritten));
    return packet_scratch_;
}

void Opus::release() {
    if (encoder_ != nullptr) {
        opus_encoder_destroy(encoder_);
        encoder_ = nullptr;
    }

    channelCount_ = 0;
    bitsPerChannel_ = 0;
    frameSize_ = 0;
    float_scratch_.clear();
    packet_scratch_.clear();
}

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/general/opus/Opus.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------

#pragma once // Prevents multiple inclusions of this header.

#include <opus.h> // Provides the Opus encoder API.
#include <cstdint> // Provides fixed-width integer types.
#include <vector> 
class Opus { // Defines a minimal Opus encoder wrapper.
public: // Exposes the encoder interface.

    Opus(); // Initializes the encoder pointer and configuration.
    ~Opus(); // Releases the encoder automatically.

    Opus(const Opus&) = delete; // Prevents copying the encoder pointer.
    Opus& operator=(const Opus&) = delete; // Prevents copy assignment.

    // Initializes the encoder using the four core configuration arguments.
    bool init(int sampleRate, int channelCount, int bitsPerChannel,
              int targetFrameDurationMs);

    // Encodes one PCM frame and returns the compressed packet size in bytes.
    // Returns a negative Opus error code if encoding fails.
    std::vector<unsigned char> encode(const void* pcmData);

    // Releases the encoder when it is no longer needed.
    void release();

private: // Keeps encoder implementation details private.

    OpusEncoder* encoder_; // Owns the Opus encoder instance.
    int channelCount_; // Stores the configured channel count.
    int bitsPerChannel_; // Stores the PCM input bit depth.
    int frameSize_; // Stores samples per channel for each frame.

    // Reusable scratch buffers (avoid per-frame heap churn).
    std::vector<float> float_scratch_;
    std::vector<unsigned char> packet_scratch_;
};

#pragma once

// Portable identity/text formatting. No ESP-IDF / FreeRTOS dependencies.

#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <string>

namespace general::util {

inline std::string formatMac(const uint8_t mac[6]) {
    char buf[18];
    std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%02X:%02X:%02X:%02X:%02X:%02X",
                  mac[0], mac[1], mac[2], mac[3], mac[4], mac[5]);
    return std::string(buf);
}

inline std::string formatDeviceId(const std::string& chip, const uint8_t mac[6]) {
    char buf[48];
    std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%s-%02X%02X%02X",
                  chip.c_str(), mac[3], mac[4], mac[5]);
    return std::string(buf);
}

} // namespace general::util

FILE: main/interface.hpp

#pragma once

// Platform-agnostic ports (interfaces) for the application core.
// IMPORTANT: no ESP-IDF / FreeRTOS / KCP / WiFi headers may be included here.
// Implementations live in main/esp_hardware/* and are wired in the composition
// root (main/esp_hardware/app_context.hpp).

#include <cstdarg>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <memory>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace core {

using Bytes = std::vector<uint8_t>;

enum class LogLevel { Info, Warn, Error };

// Logging sink (ESP_LOGx today, stdout/other tomorrow).
struct ILogger {
    virtual ~ILogger() = default;
    virtual void log(LogLevel level, const char* msg) = 0;

    // Convenience printf-style helper (non-virtual). Adapters implement log().
    void logf(LogLevel level, const char* fmt, ...) {
        char buf[256];
        va_list ap;
        va_start(ap, fmt);
        std::vsnprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), fmt, ap);
        va_end(ap);
        log(level, buf);
    }
};

// Monotonic millisecond clock.
struct IClock {
    virtual ~IClock() = default;
    virtual uint32_t nowMs() const = 0;
    virtual int64_t  nowUnixMs() const = 0;
};

// Randomness source (used for the KCP conversation id).
struct IRandom {
    virtual ~IRandom() = default;
    virtual uint32_t u32() = 0;
};

// Connectivity: "is there a usable path to the server?".
// WiFi today; 4G / Ethernet tomorrow without touching the core.
struct ILink {
    virtual ~ILink() = default;
    virtual bool start() = 0;       // begin provisioning/connect (WiFi portal, PPP dial)
    virtual bool isUp() const = 0;  // usable path (associated + IP)
};

// Hardware identity, sealed away from the application: the app never calls
// esp_read_mac() / esp_chip_info() directly.
struct IDeviceInfo {
    virtual ~IDeviceInfo() = default;
    virtual std::string deviceId() const = 0;
    virtual std::string mac() const = 0;
    virtual std::string chip() const = 0;
};

// Audio capture (microphone / I2S / ...). Fills one PCM frame per call.
struct IAudioSource {
    virtual ~IAudioSource() = default;
    virtual bool open() = 0;
    virtual void close() = 0;
    virtual bool readFrame(std::vector<int16_t>& pcm) = 0;
};

// Audio codec (Opus / LC3 / ...).
struct IAudioEncoder {
    virtual ~IAudioEncoder() = default;
    virtual bool init() = 0;
    virtual bool encode(const std::vector<int16_t>& pcm, Bytes& out) = 0;
    virtual uint32_t frameDurationMs() const = 0;
    virtual uint32_t sampleRate() const = 0;
    virtual uint32_t channelCount() const = 0;
};

// Persistence: small blobs (keys, credentials, counters). NVS today, any
// other key-value store tomorrow.
struct IStorage {
    virtual ~IStorage() = default;
    virtual bool set(const std::string& key, const Bytes& value) = 0;
    virtual bool get(const std::string& key, Bytes& out) = 0;
    virtual bool remove(const std::string& key) = 0;
    virtual bool exists(const std::string& key) = 0;
};

// File persistence (recordings, offline queues, ...). LittleFS today.
struct IFileStore {
    virtual ~IFileStore() = default;
    virtual bool init() = 0;   // mount / prepare the backing store
    virtual bool exists(const std::string& path) = 0;
    virtual bool remove(const std::string& path) = 0;
    virtual bool readAll(const std::string& path, Bytes& out) = 0;
    virtual bool writeAll(const std::string& path, const Bytes& data) = 0;
};

// Byte transport to the server. Owns the conversation id (used by the
// handshake packet) and hides whether it is UDP+KCP, PPP, TCP, ...
struct ITransport {
    virtual ~ITransport() = default;

    virtual bool open() = 0;
    virtual void close() = 0;
    virtual bool isOpen() const = 0;

    // Rebuild the transport protocol state with a FRESH conversation id so the
    // peer restarts from a clean sequence (reusing the conv would desync KCP).
    virtual void reset(uint32_t new_conv_id) = 0;

    // KCP dead_link: retransmission limit exhausted. The session may reconnect
    // early instead of waiting for the full server-timeout window.
    virtual bool isDead() const { return false; }

    // Drive internal timers / retransmissions (called every worker tick).
    virtual void tick(uint32_t now_ms) = 0;

    // Reliable, ordered delivery (application data).
    virtual int sendReliable(const Bytes& data) = 0;
    // Unreliable delivery used for the handshake (must bypass reliability).
    virtual int sendRaw(const Bytes& data) = 0;

    // Return one complete inbound message, or nullopt when nothing is ready.
    virtual std::optional<Bytes> receive(uint32_t now_ms) = 0;

    // Timestamp of the last received datagram (including transport-level acks),
    // used for server liveness detection. 0 = nothing received yet.
    virtual uint32_t lastRxMs() const = 0;

    // Conversation id shared with the peer.
    virtual uint32_t conversationId() const = 0;
};

// ECDH + authenticated encryption.
struct ICrypto {
    virtual ~ICrypto() = default;
    virtual std::optional<Bytes> generatePublicKey() = 0;
    virtual std::optional<Bytes> computeSharedSecret(const Bytes& peer_public_key) = 0;
    virtual std::optional<Bytes> encrypt(const Bytes& plaintext, const Bytes& key) = 0;
    virtual std::optional<Bytes> decrypt(const Bytes& packet, const Bytes& key) = 0;
};

// Task/thread creation (FreeRTOS today; std::thread or another RTOS elsewhere).
struct ITaskRunner {
    using TaskId = void*;
    virtual ~ITaskRunner() = default;
    virtual TaskId start(const char* name, uint32_t stack_bytes, int priority, int core,
                         void (*fn)(void*), void* arg) = 0;   // core < 0 = no affinity
    virtual void stop(TaskId task) = 0;    // force-terminate
    virtual void delayMs(uint32_t ms) = 0;
    virtual void exitCurrentTask() = 0;    // must be called by fn() before it returns
};

// Firmware update (OTA / SD card / serial, ...).
struct IFirmwareUpdater {
    virtual ~IFirmwareUpdater() = default;
    virtual bool init() = 0;
    virtual bool applyFromUrl(const std::string& url) = 0;
    virtual bool markAppValid() = 0;   // cancel OTA rollback once healthy
};

// Media playback (music player / announcements).
enum class MediaState { None, Running, Paused, Stopped, Finished, Error };

struct IMediaPlayer {
    virtual ~IMediaPlayer() = default;
    virtual bool init() = 0;
    virtual bool play(const std::string& url) = 0;
    virtual void stop() = 0;
    virtual void pause() = 0;
    virtual void resume() = 0;
    virtual MediaState state() const = 0;
    virtual uint8_t volume() const = 0;
    virtual void setVolume(uint8_t volume) = 0;
};

// Status indication (RGB LED, display, ...).
enum class IndicatorMode { Idle, Playing, Recording, VoiceCommand };
enum class IndicatorColor { Red, Green, Blue, White };

struct IIndicator {
    virtual ~IIndicator() = default;
    virtual void setMode(IndicatorMode mode) = 0;
    virtual void setColor(IndicatorColor color) = 0;
};

// Physical buttons.
enum class ButtonId { Previous, PlayPause, Next };
enum class ButtonEvent { ShortPress, LongPress };

struct IButtonInput {
    using Handler = void (*)(ButtonId id, ButtonEvent event, void* ctx);
    virtual ~IButtonInput() = default;
    virtual bool start(Handler handler, void* ctx) = 0;
};

// Voice activity / commands.
enum class SpeechEvent { Awake, Command, CommandTimeout };

struct ISpeechInput {
    using Handler = void (*)(SpeechEvent event, int command, void* ctx);
    virtual ~ISpeechInput() = default;
    virtual bool start(Handler handler, void* ctx) = 0;
};

// File listing for playback.
struct IPlaylist {
    virtual ~IPlaylist() = default;
    virtual std::vector<std::string> scan(const std::string& directory,
                                          const std::string& extension) = 0;
};

// Audio configuration shared by the application and the hardware layer.
// Defaults match the current board (see the old macroPhone::argument values).
struct AudioConfig {
    uint32_t sample_rate = 16000;
    uint32_t channel_count = 2;
    uint32_t bits_per_chan = 32;              // 32-bit I2S slots
    uint32_t target_frame_duration_ms = 20;   // Opus requires 20 ms
};

// Creates the transport for a session. Implemented by the composition root
// because the choice of transport is hardware-specific.
struct ITransportFactory {
    virtual ~ITransportFactory() = default;
    virtual std::unique_ptr<ITransport> create(const char* host, const char* port,
                                               uint32_t conv_id) = 0;
};

// Bundle of every port the application needs. core/app receives this by
// reference and never sees the composition root or the hardware layer.
struct IAppServices {
    ILogger&           log;
    IClock&            clock;
    IRandom&           random;
    ICrypto&           crypto;
    IDeviceInfo&       device;
    IStorage&          storage;
    IFileStore&        files;
    ILink&             link;
    ITaskRunner&       tasks;
    IFirmwareUpdater&  firmware;
    IAudioSource&      audio_in;
    IAudioEncoder&     audio_encoder;
    IMediaPlayer&      media_player;
    IIndicator&        indicator;
    IButtonInput&      buttons;
    ISpeechInput&      speech;
    IPlaylist&         playlist;
    ITransportFactory& transport_factory;
};

} // namespace core

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: main/main.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
// Application entry point: board bring-up + application task.
// Everything hardware-specific lives behind HARDWARE_HEADER; swap the macro to
// build the same core/ on another MCU.

#ifndef HARDWARE_HEADER
#define HARDWARE_HEADER "esp_hardware/hardware.hpp"
#endif
#include HARDWARE_HEADER

#include "app_context.hpp"
#include "core/app/prog.hpp"

extern "C" void app_main() {
    // Program-lifetime composition root: a plain object, no singleton accessor.
    static app::AppContext context;

    hardware::boot(context.audio_config);
    context.startMedia();

    if (context.tasks.start("app_run_loop_task", 9152, 5, -1,
                            app_run_loop, &context.services()) == nullptr) {
        context.logger.logf(core::LogLevel::Error,
                            "Failed to start the application task");
    }
}

 if(NOT TARGET Protocol)
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.12)



# ==============================================================================
# ESP-IDF COMPONENT BRANCH (When compiled inside ESP32 Firmware)
# ==============================================================================
if(DEFINED ESP_PLATFORM)
    message(STATUS "[Protocol] Detected ESP-IDF Environment. Registering as component...")

    # Automatically find all source and header files recursively (No manual listing!)
    file(GLOB_RECURSE PROTO_SRCS "${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/*.cpp" "${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/*.c")
    file(GLOB_RECURSE PROTO_HDRS "${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/*.h" "${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/*.hpp")

    # lib/main.cpp is a standalone host runner (defines int main()); never
    # compile it into the firmware component.
    list(FILTER PROTO_SRCS EXCLUDE REGEX "/lib/main\.cpp$")

    # Find all unique directories containing headers for include paths
    set(INCLUDE_DIRS "")
    foreach(FILE_PATH ${PROTO_HDRS})
        get_filename_component(DIR_PATH ${FILE_PATH} DIRECTORY)
        list(APPEND INCLUDE_DIRS ${DIR_PATH})
    endforeach()
    list(REMOVE_DUPLICATES INCLUDE_DIRS)

    # Register with ESP-IDF build system (No add_library allowed here)
    idf_component_register(
        SRCS ${PROTO_SRCS}
        INCLUDE_DIRS ${INCLUDE_DIRS}
    )

    return() # Stop processing the rest for ESP32
endif()

FILE: components/proto/lib/IoT/audio_protocol.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <utility>

#include "../tool/helper.hpp"
#include "../tool/magic_number.hpp"

namespace protocol::IoT {

using tool::Bytes;

struct AudioPacket {
    int64_t timestampMs = 0;   // Stream-relative ms timestamp (0 = starts a new recording)
    uint32_t durationMs = 0;   // Frame duration in ms (e.g., 20 for 20ms Opus frame)
    Bytes payload;
    uint32_t codec = 0;        // 0 = raw PCM16, 1 = Opus, 255 = unknown (recorded into ASEG header)
    uint32_t sampleRate = 0;   // e.g. 8000; 0 = unknown
    uint32_t channels = 0;     // 1 = mono; 0 = unknown
};

namespace client {

inline Bytes encodeAudio(const AudioPacket& packet) {
    Bytes b;
    tool::putU32(b, BUIS_MAGIC(AudioPacket));
    tool::putI64(b, packet.timestampMs);
    tool::putU32(b, packet.durationMs);
    tool::putBytes(b, packet.payload);
    tool::putU32(b, packet.codec);
    tool::putU32(b, packet.sampleRate);
    tool::putU32(b, packet.channels);
    return b;
}

inline Bytes encodeAudio(const Bytes& payload, int64_t timestampMs, uint32_t durationMs = 20) {
    return encodeAudio(AudioPacket{timestampMs, durationMs, payload, 0, 0, 0});
}

}  // namespace client

namespace server {

inline bool isAudio(const Bytes& b) {
    return tool::hasMagic(b, BUIS_MAGIC(AudioPacket));
}

inline bool decodeAudio(const Bytes& b, AudioPacket& out) {
    tool::Reader r{b};
    AudioPacket tmp;

    if (!tool::open(b, BUIS_MAGIC(AudioPacket), r) ||
        !r.i64(tmp.timestampMs) ||
        tmp.timestampMs < 0 ||
        !r.u32(tmp.durationMs) ||
        !r.bytes(tmp.payload)) {
        return false;
    }

    // Codec fields are optional: older devices omit them (defaults stay 0).
    r.u32(tmp.codec);
    r.u32(tmp.sampleRate);
    r.u32(tmp.channels);
    if (!r.done()) {
        return false;
    }

    out = std::move(tmp);
    return true;
}

}  // namespace server

}  // namespace protocol::IoT
--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: components/proto/lib/IoT/IoT_identity_token.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <utility>

#include "../tool/helper.hpp"          // Bytes; tool::putU32, tool::putStr, tool::Reader, tool::hasMagic, tool::open
#include "../tool/magic_number.hpp"    // BUIS_MAGIC

namespace protocol::IoT {

using tool::Bytes;

enum class Status_DeviceQuery : uint8_t {
    SUCCESS          = 0,
    DEVICE_NOT_FOUND = 1,
    INVALID_PARAM    = 2,
    SERVER_ERROR     = 3,
    AUTH_REQUIRED    = 4,   // server sent a challenge nonce; device must prove it has the secret
    AUTH_FAILED      = 5
};

inline bool isValidStatus(uint8_t raw) {
    return raw <= static_cast<uint8_t>(Status_DeviceQuery::AUTH_FAILED);
}

// Request sent by the ESP32 device containing its deviceId
struct BuisDeviceQueryRequest {
    std::string deviceId;

    bool valid() const noexcept { return !deviceId.empty(); }
};

// Response returned to the ESP32 device.
// For authenticated devices this is the final result; for provisioned devices
// the server first replies AUTH_REQUIRED with a 16-byte nonce and expects a
// BuisDeviceAuthRequest next.
struct BuisDeviceQueryResponse {
    Status_DeviceQuery status = Status_DeviceQuery::SERVER_ERROR;
    Bytes nonce;       // 16 raw bytes; only set when status == AUTH_REQUIRED
    std::string message;   // Payload info or error description
};

// Step 2 of device authentication: HMAC-SHA256(key = SHA256(device_secret),
// message = nonce || deviceId), sent as 32 raw bytes.
struct BuisDeviceAuthRequest {
    std::string deviceId;
    Bytes hmac;

    bool valid() const noexcept {
        return !deviceId.empty() && hmac.size() == 32;
    }
};

// ---- client side (ESP32): encode request, decode response ----
namespace client {

inline bool isDeviceQueryResponse(const Bytes& b) {
    return tool::hasMagic(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisDeviceQueryResponse));
}

inline Bytes encodeDeviceQuery(const BuisDeviceQueryRequest& req) {
    Bytes b;
    tool::putU32(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisDeviceQueryRequest));
    tool::putStr(b, req.deviceId);
    return b;
}

// Convenient overload
inline Bytes encodeDeviceQuery(const std::string& deviceId) {
    return encodeDeviceQuery(BuisDeviceQueryRequest{deviceId});
}

inline Bytes encodeDeviceAuth(const BuisDeviceAuthRequest& req) {
    Bytes b;
    tool::putU32(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisDeviceAuthRequest));
    tool::putStr(b, req.deviceId);
    tool::putBytes(b, req.hmac);
    return b;
}

inline bool decodeDeviceQueryResponse(const Bytes& b, BuisDeviceQueryResponse& out) {
    tool::Reader r{b};
    uint8_t rawStatus;
    if (!tool::open(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisDeviceQueryResponse), r) ||
        !r.u8(rawStatus) ||
        !isValidStatus(rawStatus)) {
        return false;
    }

    BuisDeviceQueryResponse tmp;
    tmp.status = static_cast<Status_DeviceQuery>(rawStatus);
    if (!r.bytes(tmp.nonce) ||
        !r.str(tmp.message) ||
        !r.done()) {
        return false;
    }

    out = std::move(tmp);
    return true;
}

}  // namespace client

// ---- server side (Backend handling ESP32 query): decode request, encode response ----
namespace server {

inline bool isDeviceQueryRequest(const Bytes& b) {
    return tool::hasMagic(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisDeviceQueryRequest));
}

inline bool isDeviceAuthRequest(const Bytes& b) {
    return tool::hasMagic(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisDeviceAuthRequest));
}

inline bool decodeDeviceQuery(const Bytes& b, BuisDeviceQueryRequest& out) {
    tool::Reader r{b};
    BuisDeviceQueryRequest tmp;
    if (!tool::open(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisDeviceQueryRequest), r) ||
        !r.str(tmp.deviceId) ||
        !r.done()) {
        return false;
    }

    out = std::move(tmp);
    return true;
}

inline bool decodeDeviceAuth(const Bytes& b, BuisDeviceAuthRequest& out) {
    tool::Reader r{b};
    BuisDeviceAuthRequest tmp;
    if (!tool::open(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisDeviceAuthRequest), r) ||
        !r.str(tmp.deviceId) ||
        !r.bytes(tmp.hmac) ||
        !r.done()) {
        return false;
    }

    out = std::move(tmp);
    return true;
}

inline Bytes encodeDeviceQueryResponse(const BuisDeviceQueryResponse& res) {
    Bytes b;
    tool::putU32(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisDeviceQueryResponse));
    b.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(res.status));
    tool::putBytes(b, res.nonce);
    tool::putStr(b, res.message);
    return b;
}

// Convenient overloads
inline Bytes encodeDeviceQueryResponse(Status_DeviceQuery status, const std::string& message = "") {
    return encodeDeviceQueryResponse(BuisDeviceQueryResponse{status, {}, message});
}

inline Bytes encodeDeviceChallenge(Status_DeviceQuery status, const Bytes& nonce,
                                   const std::string& message = "") {
    return encodeDeviceQueryResponse(BuisDeviceQueryResponse{status, nonce, message});
}

}  // namespace server

}  // namespace protocol::IoT

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: components/proto/lib/IoT/turn_on_audio.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <utility>

#include "../tool/helper.hpp"
#include "../tool/magic_number.hpp"

namespace protocol::IoT {

using tool::Bytes;

enum class RecordCommand : uint8_t {
    STOP    = 0,
    START   = 1,
    MAX_CMD = START
};

inline const char* toString(RecordCommand cmd) {
    switch (cmd) {
        case RecordCommand::STOP:  return "STOP";
        case RecordCommand::START: return "START";
        default:                   return "UNKNOWN";
    }
}

struct RecordControlInfo {
    RecordCommand command = RecordCommand::STOP;
    int64_t timestampMs = 0;
    uint32_t requestId = 0;   // echoes the app's Begin/EndStream requestId

    bool valid() const noexcept {
        return timestampMs >= 0 &&
               static_cast<uint8_t>(command) <= static_cast<uint8_t>(RecordCommand::MAX_CMD);
    }
};

struct RecordAckInfo {
    RecordCommand command = RecordCommand::STOP;
    int64_t timestampMs = 0;
    uint32_t requestId = 0;   // matches the RecordControl that triggered this ack
    bool ok = true;
    std::string errorMessage;

    bool valid() const noexcept {
        return timestampMs >= 0 &&
               static_cast<uint8_t>(command) <= static_cast<uint8_t>(RecordCommand::MAX_CMD);
    }
};

namespace client {

inline bool isRecordControl(const Bytes& b) {
    return tool::hasMagic(b, BUIS_MAGIC(RecordControlInfo));
}

inline bool decodeRecordControl(const Bytes& b, RecordControlInfo& out) {
    tool::Reader r{b};
    uint8_t rawCmd = 0;
    RecordControlInfo tmp;

    if (!tool::open(b, BUIS_MAGIC(RecordControlInfo), r) ||
        !r.u8(rawCmd) ||
        rawCmd > static_cast<uint8_t>(RecordCommand::MAX_CMD) ||
        !r.i64(tmp.timestampMs) ||
        tmp.timestampMs < 0) {
        return false;
    }
    r.u32(tmp.requestId);   // optional: older servers omit it
    if (!r.done()) {
        return false;
    }

    tmp.command = static_cast<RecordCommand>(rawCmd);
    out = std::move(tmp);
    return true;
}

inline Bytes encodeRecordAck(const RecordAckInfo& info) {
    Bytes b;
    tool::putU32(b, BUIS_MAGIC(RecordAckInfo));
    b.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(info.command));
    tool::putI64(b, info.timestampMs);
    b.push_back(info.ok ? 1 : 0);
    tool::putStr(b, info.errorMessage);
    // New optional field MUST be appended last so legacy peers decode cleanly.
    tool::putU32(b, info.requestId);
    return b;
}

inline Bytes encodeRecordAck(RecordCommand cmd,
                             int64_t timestampMs = 0,
                             uint32_t requestId = 0,
                             bool ok = true,
                             std::string errorMessage = "") {
    return encodeRecordAck(RecordAckInfo{cmd, timestampMs, requestId, ok, std::move(errorMessage)});
}

}  // namespace client

namespace server {

inline Bytes encodeRecordControl(const RecordControlInfo& info) {
    Bytes b;
    tool::putU32(b, BUIS_MAGIC(RecordControlInfo));
    b.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(info.command));
    tool::putI64(b, info.timestampMs);
    // Optional field appended last AND omitted for requestId == 0, so legacy
    // devices (strict decoders) see the exact old wire format.
    if (info.requestId != 0) {
        tool::putU32(b, info.requestId);
    }
    return b;
}

inline Bytes encodeRecordControl(RecordCommand cmd, int64_t timestampMs = 0,
                                 uint32_t requestId = 0) {
    return encodeRecordControl(RecordControlInfo{cmd, timestampMs, requestId});
}

inline bool isRecordAck(const Bytes& b) {
    return tool::hasMagic(b, BUIS_MAGIC(RecordAckInfo));
}

inline bool decodeRecordAck(const Bytes& b, RecordAckInfo& out) {
    tool::Reader r{b};
    uint8_t rawCmd = 0;
    uint8_t rawOk = 0;
    RecordAckInfo tmp;

    if (!tool::open(b, BUIS_MAGIC(RecordAckInfo), r) ||
        !r.u8(rawCmd) ||
        rawCmd > static_cast<uint8_t>(RecordCommand::MAX_CMD) ||
        !r.i64(tmp.timestampMs) ||
        tmp.timestampMs < 0 ||
        !r.u8(rawOk) ||
        !r.str(tmp.errorMessage)) {
        return false;
    }

    // Optional trailing requestId: legacy devices omit it (stays 0).
    r.u32(tmp.requestId);
    if (!r.done()) {
        return false;
    }

    tmp.command = static_cast<RecordCommand>(rawCmd);
    tmp.ok = (rawOk != 0);
    out = std::move(tmp);
    return true;
}

}  // namespace server

}  // namespace protocol::IoT

FILE: components/proto/lib/phone/audio_command.hpp

#pragma once
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <utility>

#include "../tool/helper.hpp"        // Bytes, putU32, putStr, Reader, hasMagic, open
#include "../tool/magic_number.hpp"  // BUIS_MAGIC

// Audio streaming commands spoken by the user's app.
namespace protocol::user {

using tool::Bytes;  // resolves to protocol::tool::Bytes without polluting namespace

// ======================== BeginStream ========================

struct BuisBeginStreamRequest {
    std::string userId;
    std::string deviceId;
    uint32_t requestId = 0;   // echoed in the response; correlates with RecordAck

    bool valid() const noexcept {
        return !userId.empty() && !deviceId.empty();
    }
};

// Empty message -> success; non-empty -> error text to display to the user
struct BuisBeginStreamResponse {
    uint32_t requestId = 0;
    std::string message;

    bool ok() const noexcept { return message.empty(); }

    static BuisBeginStreamResponse success(uint32_t requestId = 0) { return {requestId, {}}; }
    static BuisBeginStreamResponse error(std::string msg, uint32_t requestId = 0) {
        return {requestId, std::move(msg)};
    }
};

// ========================= EndStream =========================

struct BuisEndStreamRequest {
    std::string userId;
    std::string deviceId;
    uint32_t requestId = 0;   // echoed in the response; correlates with RecordAck

    bool valid() const noexcept {
        return !userId.empty() && !deviceId.empty();
    }
};

// Empty message -> success; non-empty -> error text to display to the user
struct BuisEndStreamResponse {
    uint32_t requestId = 0;
    std::string message;

    bool ok() const noexcept { return message.empty(); }

    static BuisEndStreamResponse success(uint32_t requestId = 0) { return {requestId, {}}; }
    static BuisEndStreamResponse error(std::string msg, uint32_t requestId = 0) {
        return {requestId, std::move(msg)};
    }
};

// ---- internal serialization helpers ----
namespace detail {

template <typename Req>
inline Bytes encodeStreamRequest(std::uint32_t magic, const Req& req) {
    Bytes b;
    tool::putU32(b, magic);
    tool::putStr(b, req.userId);
    tool::putStr(b, req.deviceId);
    tool::putU32(b, req.requestId);
    return b;
}

template <typename Req>
inline bool decodeStreamRequest(const Bytes& b, std::uint32_t magic, Req& out) {
    tool::Reader r{b};
    Req tmp;
    if (!tool::open(b, magic, r) ||
        !r.str(tmp.userId) ||
        !r.str(tmp.deviceId)) {
        return false;
    }
    r.u32(tmp.requestId);   // optional: older clients omit it (stays 0)
    if (!r.done()) {
        return false;
    }
    out = std::move(tmp);
    return true;
}

template <typename Res>
inline Bytes encodeStreamResponse(std::uint32_t magic, const Res& res) {
    Bytes b;
    tool::putU32(b, magic);
    tool::putStr(b, res.message);
    // Optional field appended last AND omitted for requestId == 0, so legacy
    // clients (strict decoders) see the exact old wire format.
    if (res.requestId != 0) {
        tool::putU32(b, res.requestId);
    }
    return b;
}

template <typename Res>
inline bool decodeStreamResponse(const Bytes& b, std::uint32_t magic, Res& out) {
    tool::Reader r{b};
    Res tmp;
    if (!tool::open(b, magic, r)) {
        return false;
    }
    if (!r.str(tmp.message)) {
        return false;
    }
    // Optional trailing requestId: older servers omit it (stays 0).
    r.u32(tmp.requestId);
    if (!r.done()) {
        return false;
    }
    out = std::move(tmp);
    return true;
}

}  // namespace detail

// ---- client side: encode request, decode response ----
namespace client {

inline bool isBeginStreamResponse(const Bytes& b) {
    return tool::hasMagic(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisBeginStreamResponse));
}

inline Bytes encodeBeginStream(const BuisBeginStreamRequest& req) {
    return detail::encodeStreamRequest(BUIS_MAGIC(BuisBeginStreamRequest), req);
}

inline bool decodeBeginStreamResponse(const Bytes& b, BuisBeginStreamResponse& out) {
    return detail::decodeStreamResponse(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisBeginStreamResponse), out);
}

inline bool isEndStreamResponse(const Bytes& b) {
    return tool::hasMagic(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisEndStreamResponse));
}

inline Bytes encodeEndStream(const BuisEndStreamRequest& req) {
    return detail::encodeStreamRequest(BUIS_MAGIC(BuisEndStreamRequest), req);
}

inline bool decodeEndStreamResponse(const Bytes& b, BuisEndStreamResponse& out) {
    return detail::decodeStreamResponse(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisEndStreamResponse), out);
}

}  // namespace client

// ---- server side: decode request, encode response ----
namespace server {

inline bool isBeginStreamRequest(const Bytes& b) {
    return tool::hasMagic(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisBeginStreamRequest));
}

inline bool decodeBeginStream(const Bytes& b, BuisBeginStreamRequest& out) {
    return detail::decodeStreamRequest(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisBeginStreamRequest), out);
}

inline Bytes encodeBeginStreamResponse(const BuisBeginStreamResponse& res) {
    return detail::encodeStreamResponse(BUIS_MAGIC(BuisBeginStreamResponse), res);
}

inline bool isEndStreamRequest(const Bytes& b) {
    return tool::hasMagic(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisEndStreamRequest));
}

inline bool decodeEndStream(const Bytes& b, BuisEndStreamRequest& out) {
    return detail::decodeStreamRequest(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisEndStreamRequest), out);
}

inline Bytes encodeEndStreamResponse(const BuisEndStreamResponse& res) {
    return detail::encodeStreamResponse(BUIS_MAGIC(BuisEndStreamResponse), res);
}

}  // namespace server

}  // namespace protocol::user

namespace protocol::user {

using tool::Bytes;  // resolves to protocol::tool::Bytes without polluting namespace

struct BuisBindRequest {
    std::string userId;
    std::string deviceId;
    std::string pin;    // optional 6-digit pairing PIN printed inside the box

    bool valid() const noexcept {
        return !userId.empty() && !deviceId.empty();
    }
};

// Empty message -> success; non-empty -> error text to display to the user
struct BuisBindResponse {
    std::string message;

    bool ok() const noexcept { return message.empty(); }

    static BuisBindResponse success() { return {}; }
    static BuisBindResponse error(std::string msg) { return {std::move(msg)}; }
};

    return tool::hasMagic(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisBindResponse));
}

inline Bytes encodeBindDevice(const BuisBindRequest& req) {
    Bytes b;
    tool::putU32(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisBindRequest));
    tool::putStr(b, req.userId);
    tool::putStr(b, req.deviceId);
    tool::putStr(b, req.pin);
    return b;
}

    tool::Reader r{b};
    BuisBindResponse tmp;

        !r.str(tmp.message) ||
        !r.done()) {
        return false;
    }
    out = std::move(tmp);
    return true;

FILE: components/proto/lib/phone/matadata_query.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

#include "../tool/helper.hpp"
#include "../tool/magic_number.hpp"

namespace protocol::user {

using tool::Bytes;

struct FileInfoEntry {
    std::string deviceId;
    std::string fileId;
    std::string fileName;
    std::int64_t createdTimeMs = 0;
    std::int64_t durationMs = 0;
    std::int64_t sizeBytes = 0;
    bool isOpen = false;
    std::string description;
    std::uint32_t codec = 0;       // 0 = raw PCM16, 1 = Opus, 255 = unknown
    std::uint32_t sampleRate = 0;  // 0 = unknown (legacy v1 files)
    std::uint32_t channels = 0;    // 0 = unknown
};

struct BuisFileListRequest {
    std::string userId;
    std::string deviceId; // Empty -> all devices owned by userId
    std::uint32_t limit = 50;
    std::uint32_t offset = 0;

    bool valid() const noexcept {
        return !userId.empty() && limit > 0;
    }
};

struct BuisFileListResponse {
    std::string message;
    std::vector<FileInfoEntry> files;
    bool hasMore = false;

    bool ok() const noexcept { return message.empty(); }

    static BuisFileListResponse success(std::vector<FileInfoEntry> files = {},
                                        bool hasMore = false) {
        return {{}, std::move(files), hasMore};
    }

    static BuisFileListResponse error(std::string msg) {
        return {std::move(msg), {}, false};
    }
};

namespace detail::file_info {

inline void encodeFileEntries(Bytes& b, const std::vector<FileInfoEntry>& files) {
    tool::putU32(b, static_cast<std::uint32_t>(files.size()));
    for (const auto& f : files) {
        tool::putStr(b, f.deviceId);
        tool::putStr(b, f.fileId);
        tool::putStr(b, f.fileName);

        tool::putI64(b, f.createdTimeMs);
        tool::putI64(b, f.durationMs);
        tool::putI64(b, f.sizeBytes);

        b.push_back(f.isOpen ? 1 : 0);
        tool::putStr(b, f.description);
        // Optional codec metadata appended last (older clients ignore the
        // trailing bytes only if their decoder is lenient - mirror the
        // lenient decoder below).
        tool::putU32(b, f.codec);
        tool::putU32(b, f.sampleRate);
        tool::putU32(b, f.channels);
    }
}

// Minimal entry size: 4x u32 string lengths + 3x i64 + 1x u8 = 41 bytes.
constexpr std::size_t kMinEntryBytes = 41;

inline bool decodeEntry(tool::Reader& r, FileInfoEntry& entry, bool withCodec) {
    std::uint8_t openFlag = 0;
    if (!r.str(entry.deviceId) ||
        !r.str(entry.fileId) ||
        !r.str(entry.fileName) ||
        !r.i64(entry.createdTimeMs) ||
        !r.i64(entry.durationMs) ||
        !r.i64(entry.sizeBytes) ||
        !r.u8(openFlag) ||
        !r.str(entry.description)) {
        return false;
    }

    if (withCodec) {
        // Optional trailing codec fields: they must be complete for EVERY
        // entry. Partially present fields would consume the next entry's data.
        if (!r.u32(entry.codec) || !r.u32(entry.sampleRate) || !r.u32(entry.channels)) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    entry.isOpen = (openFlag != 0);
    return true;
}

inline bool decodeFileEntriesLayout(tool::Reader& r, std::size_t totalBytes,
                                    std::uint32_t vectorSize,
                                    std::vector<FileInfoEntry>& out,
                                    bool withCodec) {
    if (static_cast<std::size_t>(vectorSize) > (totalBytes - r.pos) / kMinEntryBytes) {
        return false;
    }

    std::vector<FileInfoEntry> entries;
    entries.reserve(vectorSize);

    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < vectorSize; ++i) {
        FileInfoEntry entry;
        if (!decodeEntry(r, entry, withCodec)) {
            return false;
        }
        entries.push_back(std::move(entry));
    }

    out = std::move(entries);
    return true;
}

// Accepts both wire layouts: new servers append 12 codec bytes per entry,
// older servers do not. Each layout is only accepted when it consumes the
// packet exactly, so variable-length filenames cannot be misinterpreted.
inline bool decodeFileEntries(tool::Reader& r,
                              std::size_t totalBytes,
                              std::vector<FileInfoEntry>& out) {
    std::uint32_t vectorSize = 0;
    if (!r.u32(vectorSize)) {
        return false;
    }

    const std::size_t bodyStart = r.pos;

    if (decodeFileEntriesLayout(r, totalBytes, vectorSize, out, true) &&
        r.pos == totalBytes) {
        return true;
    }

    r.pos = bodyStart;
    out.clear();
    return decodeFileEntriesLayout(r, totalBytes, vectorSize, out, false) &&
           r.pos == totalBytes;
}

}  // namespace detail::file_info

namespace client {

inline bool isFileListResponse(const Bytes& b) {
    return tool::hasMagic(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisFileListResponse));
}

inline Bytes encodeFileList(const BuisFileListRequest& req) {
    Bytes b;
    tool::putU32(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisFileListRequest));
    tool::putStr(b, req.userId);
    tool::putStr(b, req.deviceId);
    tool::putU32(b, req.limit);
    tool::putU32(b, req.offset);
    return b;
}

inline bool decodeFileListResponse(const Bytes& b, BuisFileListResponse& out) {
    tool::Reader r{b};
    BuisFileListResponse tmp;
    std::uint8_t moreFlag = 0;

    if (!tool::open(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisFileListResponse), r) ||
        !r.str(tmp.message) ||
        !r.u8(moreFlag) ||
        !detail::file_info::decodeFileEntries(r, b.size(), tmp.files) ||
        !r.done()) {
        return false;
    }

    tmp.hasMore = (moreFlag != 0);
    out = std::move(tmp);
    return true;
}

}  // namespace client

namespace server {

inline bool isFileListRequest(const Bytes& b) {
    return tool::hasMagic(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisFileListRequest));
}

inline bool decodeFileList(const Bytes& b, BuisFileListRequest& out) {
    tool::Reader r{b};
    BuisFileListRequest tmp;
    if (!tool::open(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisFileListRequest), r) ||
        !r.str(tmp.userId) ||
        !r.str(tmp.deviceId) ||
        !r.u32(tmp.limit) ||
        !r.u32(tmp.offset) ||
        !r.done()) {
        return false;
    }
    out = std::move(tmp);
    return true;
}

inline Bytes encodeFileListResponse(const BuisFileListResponse& res) {
    Bytes b;
    tool::putU32(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisFileListResponse));
    tool::putStr(b, res.message);
    b.push_back(res.hasMore ? 1 : 0);
    detail::file_info::encodeFileEntries(b, res.files);
    return b;
}

}  // namespace server

}  // namespace protocol::user
