FILE: components/proto/lib/phone/pull_audio_data.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

#include "../tool/helper.hpp"
#include "../tool/magic_number.hpp"

namespace protocol::user {

using tool::Bytes;

struct AudioChunkEntry {
    std::int64_t timestampMs = 0;
    std::int64_t durationMs = 0;
    Bytes payload;
};

struct BuisPullAudioRequest {
    std::string userId;
    std::string deviceId;
    std::string fileId;
    std::int64_t timestampMs = 0;
    std::int64_t durationMs = 0;
    // Opaque O(1) resume cursor (byte offset in the .seg file) from a previous
    // response. When != 0 it takes precedence over timestampMs. New optional
    // field appended last; older servers decode without it.
    std::uint64_t cursor = 0;

    bool valid() const noexcept {
        return !userId.empty() && !deviceId.empty() && !fileId.empty() &&
               timestampMs >= 0 && durationMs > 0;
    }
};

struct BuisPullAudioResponse {
    std::string fileId;
    std::string message;
    std::vector<AudioChunkEntry> chunks;
    bool hasMore = false;
    std::int64_t nextTimestampMs = 0;
    // Opaque resume cursor (byte offset); 0 when the range is fully drained.
    std::uint64_t cursor = 0;
    // Codec metadata of the .seg file (ASEG v2 header; 0 = legacy/unknown).
    std::uint32_t codec = 0;
    std::uint32_t sampleRate = 0;
    std::uint32_t channels = 0;

    bool ok() const noexcept { return message.empty(); }

    static BuisPullAudioResponse success(std::string fileId,
                                         std::vector<AudioChunkEntry> chunks = {},
                                         bool hasMore = false,
                                         std::int64_t nextTimestampMs = 0,
                                         std::uint64_t cursor = 0) {
        return {std::move(fileId), {}, std::move(chunks), hasMore, nextTimestampMs, cursor,
                0, 0, 0};
    }

    static BuisPullAudioResponse error(std::string msg, std::string fileId = {}) {
        return {std::move(fileId), std::move(msg), {}, false, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
    }
};

namespace client {

inline bool isPullAudioResponse(const Bytes& b) {
    return tool::hasMagic(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisPullAudioResponse));
}

inline Bytes encodePullAudio(const BuisPullAudioRequest& req) {
    Bytes b;
    tool::putU32(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisPullAudioRequest));
    tool::putStr(b, req.userId);
    tool::putStr(b, req.deviceId);
    tool::putStr(b, req.fileId);
    tool::putI64(b, req.timestampMs);
    tool::putI64(b, req.durationMs);
    // Optional cursor appended last (older servers ignore extra bytes only if
    // lenient; keep it last per the wire-compat rule).
    if (req.cursor != 0) {
        tool::putU64(b, req.cursor);
    }
    return b;
}

inline bool decodePullAudioResponse(const Bytes& b, BuisPullAudioResponse& out) {
    tool::Reader r{b};
    if (!tool::open(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisPullAudioResponse), r)) {
        return false;
    }

    BuisPullAudioResponse tmp;
    std::uint8_t moreFlag = 0;
    std::uint32_t count = 0;

    if (!r.str(tmp.fileId) ||
        !r.str(tmp.message) ||
        !r.u8(moreFlag) ||
        !r.i64(tmp.nextTimestampMs) ||
        !r.u32(count)) {
        return false;
    }

    // Each chunk encodes at least 20 bytes (2x i64 + u32 payload len).
    if (static_cast<std::size_t>(count) > (b.size() - r.pos) / 20) {
        return false;
    }

    tmp.hasMore = (moreFlag != 0);
    tmp.chunks.reserve(count);
    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
        AudioChunkEntry entry;
        if (!r.i64(entry.timestampMs) ||
            !r.i64(entry.durationMs) ||
            !r.bytes(entry.payload)) {
            return false;
        }
        tmp.chunks.push_back(std::move(entry));
    }

    // Optional trailing fields: cursor + codec metadata.
    r.u64(tmp.cursor);
    r.u32(tmp.codec);
    r.u32(tmp.sampleRate);
    r.u32(tmp.channels);

    if (!r.done()) {
        return false;
    }

    out = std::move(tmp);
    return true;
}

}  // namespace client

namespace server {

inline bool isPullAudioRequest(const Bytes& b) {
    return tool::hasMagic(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisPullAudioRequest));
}

inline bool decodePullAudio(const Bytes& b, BuisPullAudioRequest& out) {
    tool::Reader r{b};
    BuisPullAudioRequest tmp;

    if (!tool::open(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisPullAudioRequest), r) ||
        !r.str(tmp.userId) ||
        !r.str(tmp.deviceId) ||
        !r.str(tmp.fileId) ||
        !r.i64(tmp.timestampMs) ||
        !r.i64(tmp.durationMs)) {
        return false;
    }

    // Optional trailing cursor (older clients omit it; stays 0).
    r.u64(tmp.cursor);
    if (!r.done()) {
        return false;
    }

    out = std::move(tmp);
    return true;
}

inline Bytes encodePullAudioResponse(const BuisPullAudioResponse& res) {
    Bytes b;
    tool::putU32(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisPullAudioResponse));
    tool::putStr(b, res.fileId);
    tool::putStr(b, res.message);
    b.push_back(res.hasMore ? 1 : 0);
    tool::putI64(b, res.nextTimestampMs);

    tool::putU32(b, static_cast<std::uint32_t>(res.chunks.size()));
    for (const auto& chunk : res.chunks) {
        tool::putI64(b, chunk.timestampMs);
        tool::putI64(b, chunk.durationMs);
        tool::putBytes(b, chunk.payload);
    }

    // Optional trailing fields: cursor + codec metadata.
    tool::putU64(b, res.cursor);
    tool::putU32(b, res.codec);
    tool::putU32(b, res.sampleRate);
    tool::putU32(b, res.channels);
    return b;
}

}  // namespace server

}  // namespace protocol::user

// ======================== File Rename ========================

struct BuisFileRenameRequest {
    std::string userId;
    std::string deviceId;
    std::string fileId;
    std::string newName;

    bool valid() const noexcept {
        return !userId.empty() && !deviceId.empty() && !fileId.empty() && !newName.empty();
    }
};

struct BuisFileRenameResponse {
    std::string message;
    std::string fileId;
    std::string newName;

    bool ok() const noexcept { return message.empty(); }

    static BuisFileRenameResponse success(std::string fileId, std::string newName) {
        return {{}, std::move(fileId), std::move(newName)};
    }

    static BuisFileRenameResponse error(std::string msg,
                                        std::string fileId = {},
                                        std::string newName = {}) {
        return {std::move(msg), std::move(fileId), std::move(newName)};
    }
};

    std::string userId;
    std::string deviceId;
    std::string fileId;

    bool valid() const noexcept {
        return !userId.empty() && !deviceId.empty() && !fileId.empty();
    }
};

struct BuisFileDeleteResponse {
    std::string message;
    std::string fileId;

    bool ok() const noexcept { return message.empty(); }

    static BuisFileDeleteResponse success(std::string fileId) {
        return {{}, std::move(fileId)};
    }

    static BuisFileDeleteResponse error(std::string msg, std::string fileId = {}) {
        return {std::move(msg), std::move(fileId)};
    }
};

#pragma once
#include <string>
#include <utility>

#include "../tool/helper.hpp"        // Bytes, putU32, putStr, Reader, hasMagic, open
#include "../tool/magic_number.hpp"  // BUIS_MAGIC

// Messages spoken by the user's app (as opposed to IoT device protocols).
namespace protocol::user {

using tool::Bytes;  // resolves to protocol::tool::Bytes without polluting namespace

struct BuisSignUpRequest {
    std::string email;
    std::string password;
    std::string username;

    bool valid() const noexcept {
        return !email.empty() && !password.empty() && !username.empty();
    }
};

struct BuisSignInRequest {
    std::string email;
    std::string password;

    bool valid() const noexcept {
        return !email.empty() && !password.empty();
    }
};

// Empty message -> success (token is filled); non-empty -> error text to display to the user
struct BuisAuthResponse {
    std::string token;      // opaque 256-bit session token (hex) issued by the server
    std::string userId;     // the authenticated user's id (needed for later requests)
    std::string message;

    bool ok() const noexcept { return message.empty(); }

    static BuisAuthResponse success(std::string token, std::string userId) {
        return {std::move(token), std::move(userId), {}};
    }

    static BuisAuthResponse error(std::string msg) {
        return {{}, {}, std::move(msg)};
    }
};

// ---- client side: encode requests, decode response ----
namespace client {

inline bool isAuthResponse(const Bytes& b) {
    return tool::hasMagic(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisAuthResponse));
}

inline Bytes encodeSignUp(const BuisSignUpRequest& req) {
    Bytes b;
    tool::putU32(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisSignUpRequest));
    tool::putStr(b, req.email);
    tool::putStr(b, req.password);
    tool::putStr(b, req.username);
    return b;
}

inline Bytes encodeSignIn(const BuisSignInRequest& req) {
    Bytes b;
    tool::putU32(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisSignInRequest));
    tool::putStr(b, req.email);
    tool::putStr(b, req.password);
    return b;
}

inline bool decodeAuthResponse(const Bytes& b, BuisAuthResponse& out) {
    tool::Reader r{b};
    BuisAuthResponse tmp;
    if (!tool::open(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisAuthResponse), r) ||
        !r.str(tmp.token) ||
        !r.str(tmp.userId) ||
        !r.str(tmp.message) ||
        !r.done()) {
        return false;
    }
    out = std::move(tmp);
    return true;
}

}  // namespace client

// ---- server side: decode requests, encode response ----
namespace server {

inline bool isSignUpRequest(const Bytes& b) {
    return tool::hasMagic(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisSignUpRequest));
}

inline bool isSignInRequest(const Bytes& b) {
    return tool::hasMagic(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisSignInRequest));
}

inline bool decodeSignUp(const Bytes& b, BuisSignUpRequest& out) {
    tool::Reader r{b};
    BuisSignUpRequest tmp;
    if (!tool::open(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisSignUpRequest), r) ||
        !r.str(tmp.email) ||
        !r.str(tmp.password) ||
        !r.str(tmp.username) ||
        !r.done()) {
        return false;
    }
    out = std::move(tmp);
    return true;
}

inline bool decodeSignIn(const Bytes& b, BuisSignInRequest& out) {
    tool::Reader r{b};
    BuisSignInRequest tmp;
    if (!tool::open(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisSignInRequest), r) ||
        !r.str(tmp.email) ||
        !r.str(tmp.password) ||
        !r.done()) {
        return false;
    }
    out = std::move(tmp);
    return true;
}

inline Bytes encodeAuthResponse(const BuisAuthResponse& res) {
    Bytes b;
    tool::putU32(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisAuthResponse));
    tool::putStr(b, res.token);
    tool::putStr(b, res.userId);
    tool::putStr(b, res.message);
    return b;
}

}  // namespace server

}  // namespace protocol::user

#pragma once
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

#include "../tool/helper.hpp"
#include "../tool/magic_number.hpp"
#include "./matadata_query.hpp"

namespace protocol::user {

using tool::Bytes;

struct BuisStreamStateRequest {
    std::string userId;

    bool valid() const noexcept {
        return !userId.empty();
    }
};

struct BuisStreamStateResponse {
    std::string message;
    std::vector<FileInfoEntry> files;

    bool ok() const noexcept { return message.empty(); }

    static BuisStreamStateResponse success(std::vector<FileInfoEntry> files = {}) {
        return {{}, std::move(files)};
    }

    static BuisStreamStateResponse error(std::string msg) {
        return {std::move(msg), {}};
    }
};

struct BuisStreamStateNotify {
    std::string message;
    std::vector<FileInfoEntry> files;
};

namespace detail::stream_state {

template <typename Msg>
inline Bytes encodeStreamStateMsg(std::uint32_t magic, const Msg& msg) {
    Bytes b;
    tool::putU32(b, magic);
    tool::putStr(b, msg.message);
    detail::file_info::encodeFileEntries(b, msg.files);
    return b;
}

template <typename Msg>
inline bool decodeStreamStateMsg(const Bytes& b, std::uint32_t magic, Msg& out) {
    tool::Reader r{b};
    Msg tmp;
    if (!tool::open(b, magic, r) ||
        !r.str(tmp.message) ||
        !detail::file_info::decodeFileEntries(r, b.size(), tmp.files) ||
        !r.done()) {
        return false;
    }
    out = std::move(tmp);
    return true;
}

}  // namespace detail::stream_state

namespace client {

inline bool isStreamStateResponse(const Bytes& b) {
    return tool::hasMagic(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisStreamStateResponse));
}

inline Bytes encodeStreamState(const BuisStreamStateRequest& req) {
    Bytes b;
    tool::putU32(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisStreamStateRequest));
    tool::putStr(b, req.userId);
    return b;
}

inline bool decodeStreamStateResponse(const Bytes& b, BuisStreamStateResponse& out) {
    return detail::stream_state::decodeStreamStateMsg(
        b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisStreamStateResponse), out);
}

inline bool isStreamStateNotify(const Bytes& b) {
    return tool::hasMagic(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisStreamStateNotify));
}

inline bool decodeStreamStateNotify(const Bytes& b, BuisStreamStateNotify& out) {
    return detail::stream_state::decodeStreamStateMsg(
        b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisStreamStateNotify), out);
}

}  // namespace client

namespace server {

inline bool isStreamStateRequest(const Bytes& b) {
    return tool::hasMagic(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisStreamStateRequest));
}

inline bool decodeStreamState(const Bytes& b, BuisStreamStateRequest& out) {
    tool::Reader r{b};
    BuisStreamStateRequest tmp;
    if (!tool::open(b, BUIS_MAGIC(BuisStreamStateRequest), r) ||
        !r.str(tmp.userId) ||
        !r.done()) {
        return false;
    }
    out = std::move(tmp);
    return true;
}

inline Bytes encodeStreamStateResponse(const BuisStreamStateResponse& res) {
    return detail::stream_state::encodeStreamStateMsg(
        BUIS_MAGIC(BuisStreamStateResponse), res);
}

inline Bytes encodeStreamStateNotify(const BuisStreamStateNotify& notify) {
    return detail::stream_state::encodeStreamStateMsg(
        BUIS_MAGIC(BuisStreamStateNotify), notify);
}

}  // namespace server

#if defined (TestConflictNumber)
#include <iostream>

int main(){

     auto r = BUIS_MAGIC(sl);
     
     auto s = BUIS_MAGIC(sll);

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: components/proto/lib/tool/helper.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------



 #pragma once

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib> 
#include <string> 
#include <vector>


namespace protocol::tool {

using Bytes = std::vector<uint8_t>;
 
 

inline void putU32(Bytes& b, uint32_t v) {
    for (int s = 24; s >= 0; s -= 8) b.push_back(uint8_t(v >> s));
}
inline void putStr(Bytes& b, const std::string& s) {
    putU32(b, uint32_t(s.size()));
    b.insert(b.end(), s.begin(), s.end());
}
inline void putBytes(Bytes& b, const Bytes& data) {
    putU32(b, uint32_t(data.size()));
    b.insert(b.end(), data.begin(), data.end());
}
inline void putU64(Bytes& b, uint64_t v) {
    for (int s = 56; s >= 0; s -= 8) b.push_back(uint8_t(v >> s));
}
 
inline void putI64(Bytes& b, int64_t v) {
    putU64(b, static_cast<uint64_t>(v));
}
struct Reader {
    const Bytes& b;
    size_t pos = 0;

    bool u8(uint8_t& v) {
        if (pos + 1 > b.size()) return false;
        v = b[pos++];
        return true;
    }
    bool u32(uint32_t& v) {
        if (b.size() - pos < 4) return false;
        v = (uint32_t(b[pos]) << 24) | (uint32_t(b[pos + 1]) << 16) |
            (uint32_t(b[pos + 2]) << 8) | uint32_t(b[pos + 3]);
        pos += 4;
        return true;
    }
    // Inside struct Reader:
    bool u64(uint64_t& v) {
        if (b.size() - pos < 8) return false;
        v = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
            v = (v << 8) | uint64_t(b[pos++]);
        }
        return true;
    }
       bool i64(int64_t& v) {
        uint64_t raw = 0;
        if (!u64(raw)) return false;
        v = static_cast<int64_t>(raw);
        return true;
    }
    bool str(std::string& s) {
        uint32_t len;
        if (!u32(len) || len > b.size() - pos) return false;
        s.assign(b.begin() + pos, b.begin() + pos + len);
        pos += len;
        return true;
    }
        bool bytes(Bytes& out) {
        uint32_t len;
        if (!u32(len) || len > b.size() - pos) return false;
        out.assign(b.begin() + pos, b.begin() + pos + len);
        pos += len;
        return true;
    }
    bool done() const { return pos == b.size(); }
};

inline bool hasMagic(const Bytes& b, uint32_t magic) {
    if (b.size() < 4) return false;
    Reader r{b};
    uint32_t m;
    return r.u32(m) && m == magic;
}

inline bool open(const Bytes& b, uint32_t magic, Reader& r) {
    uint32_t m;
    return r.u32(m) && m == magic;
}
 

} 


--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: components/proto/lib/tool/magic_number.hpp
--------------------------------------------------------------

#pragma once
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <mutex>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string> 
namespace protocol::tool {
namespace detail {

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 1. The hash: FNV-1a over the name, evaluated at compile time.
//    The same text gives the same number on every machine, compiler and build.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
constexpr uint32_t magic(std::string_view name) {
    uint32_t h = 2166136261u;                    // FNV offset basis
    for (char c : name) {
        h ^= static_cast<uint8_t>(c);
        h *= 16777619u;                          // FNV prime
    }
    return h;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 2. The registry: remembers every magic number in use and aborts on a duplicate.
//    Function-local static, so it exists whenever the first registrar needs it,
//    whatever order the static initializers of different files run in.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class MagicRegistry {
public:
    static MagicRegistry& instance() {
        static MagicRegistry r;
        return r;
    }

    void add(uint32_t value, const char* name) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> l(m_);
        auto [it, inserted] = names_.emplace(value, name);
        if (!inserted) {
            std::fprintf(stderr,
                "[protocol] magic number collision 0x%08X: \"%s\" and \"%s\"\n",
                static_cast<unsigned int>(value), it->second.c_str(), name);
            std::abort();
        }
    }

    // Handy for logs: the message name for a magic number, or "unknown".
    std::string name_of(uint32_t value) {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> l(m_);
        auto it = names_.find(value);
        return it == names_.end() ? "unknown" : it->second;
    }

private:
    std::mutex m_;
    std::unordered_map<uint32_t, std::string> names_;
};

// Constructing one registers a number. Runs during static initialization.
struct MagicRegistrar {
    MagicRegistrar(uint32_t value, const char* name) {
        MagicRegistry::instance().add(value, name);
    }
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 3. A string literal that can be used as a template argument (C++20).
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
template <std::size_t N>
struct FixedString {
    char data[N]{};
    constexpr FixedString(const char (&s)[N]) {
        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) data[i] = s[i];
    }
    constexpr std::string_view view() const { return {data, N - 1}; }   // without the trailing '\0'
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 4. One class per distinct name in the whole program.
//    `value` is the compile-time number. Calling id() makes the compiler
//    instantiate `reg`, whose constructor registers the number before main.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
template <FixedString Name>
struct Registered {
    static constexpr uint32_t value = magic(Name.view());

    static uint32_t id() {
        (void)&reg;                              // odr-use: forces `reg` to exist and be initialized
        return value;
    }

private:
    [[maybe_unused]] static inline const MagicRegistrar reg{value, Name.data};
};

}  // namespace tool
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 5. The macros.
//    sizeof(T) makes a misspelled type name a compile error. Don't put spaces
//    inside the argument (`Foo::Bar` and `Foo :: Bar` stringify differently).
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

// Yields the uint32_t and registers it. Use it anywhere a number is needed.
#define BUIS_MAGIC(T) (static_cast<void>(sizeof(T)),  protocol::tool::detail::Registered<#T>::id())

// Same number as a compile-time constant (for `case` labels and constexpr).
// It does not register by itself; the BUIS_MAGIC uses of the same name do.
#define BUIS_MAGIC_CONST(T) (static_cast<void>(sizeof(T)), protocol::tool::detail::Registered<#T>::value)
--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: docs/architecture.md
--------------------------------------------------------------
# architecture

Ports & Adapters (hexagonal) with four folders. Dependencies point **inward**:
the application core knows nothing about ESP-IDF, FreeRTOS, KCP, WiFi or the board.

```
main/
  interface.hpp        ports + AudioConfig + IAppServices + ITransportFactory
  main.cpp             thin entry: hardware::boot(config) + start core/app task
  app_context.*        composition root (plain class) -> core::IAppServices
  core/                application + domain (pure C++)
    app/               prog, audio_service, net worker, media_controller,
                       session_factory
    session/           SessionManager (handshake, liveness, reconnect)
    audio/             AudioPipeline
  general/             portable libraries (no core/ or esp_hardware/ deps)
    kcp/               ikcp + KcpSession + DatagramIo
    crypto/            PSA AES-256-GCM + ECDH P-256 (backend injected)
    opus/              Opus wrapper
    audio/             frame math
    io/                path join, stdio file store, random-access file
    fs/                directory lister
    input/             button debounce / long-press state machine
    net/               POSIX UDP socket helpers (getaddrinfo/sendto/recvfrom)
    util/              MAC / device-id formatting
  esp_hardware/        the ONLY folder allowed to touch ESP-IDF
    hardware.hpp/.cpp  boot(const core::AudioConfig&) + board bring-up
    adapters/          port implementations (incl. media/UI inputs)
    drivers/           bsp, i2s, buttons, led, speech, io expander
    wifi/              WiFi manager / provisioning
    net/               UDP DatagramIo adapter (POSIX socket code is in general/net)
    storage/           LittleFS mount + OTA backend
```

`main/CMakeLists.txt` adds `main/`, `main/general` and `main/esp_hardware` as
include roots, so includes use stable prefixes (`interface.hpp`, `core/...`,
`general/...`, `esp_hardware/...`) regardless of where a file lives.

## Ports (`main/interface.hpp`)

| Port | Responsibility | Today | Tomorrow |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ILink` | start the path + report usable link | `EspWifiLink` | 4G / Ethernet |
| `ITransport` | move bytes to/from server; own conv id | `KcpUdpTransport` | PPP / TCP |
| `ICrypto` | ECDH + AES-GCM | `EspCrypto` | other backend |
| `IClock` | monotonic ms + unix ms | `esp_timer` / `gettimeofday` | any OS |
| `IRandom` | session id | `esp_random` | any OS |
| `ILogger` | logging (`log` / `logf`) | `ESP_LOGx` | any OS |
| `IDeviceInfo` | MAC / chip / device id | `EspDeviceInfo` | any board |
| `IStorage` | small blob key-value | `EspNvsStorage` | any KV store |
| `IFileStore` | mount + file persistence | `EspFileStore` | any FS |
| `IAudioSource` | open/close/read PCM | `EspI2sAudioSource` | any mic |
| `IAudioEncoder` | audio codec | `OpusEncoder` | LC3 / other |
| `ITaskRunner` | create/stop/delay/exit tasks | `EspTaskRunner` (FreeRTOS) | `std::thread` / RTOS |
| `IFirmwareUpdater` | OTA init / apply | `EspFirmwareUpdater` | SD / serial |
| `IMediaPlayer` | play/pause/stop/volume | `EspMediaPlayer` | any player |
| `IIndicator` | RGB / display status | `EspIndicator` | any UI |
| `IButtonInput` | physical buttons | `EspButtonInput` | any input |
| `ISpeechInput` | voice commands | `EspSpeechInput` | any ASR |
| `IPlaylist` | track listing | `EspSdPlaylist` | any FS |
| `ITransportFactory` | create the session transport | `AppContext` | any transport |

`IAppServices` (also in `interface.hpp`) bundles references to every port above
plus `ITransportFactory`; `core/app` receives it by reference. `AudioConfig`
carries the sample rate / channels / bit depth / frame duration.

## General logic rescued from `esp_hardware`

Deleting `main/esp_hardware/` now only removes board glue; these behaviours are
compiled from `core/` and `general/`:

| Logic | Lives in | Hardware counterpart |
|---|---|---|
| KCP session, tuning, reassembly, liveness | `general/kcp/KcpSession` | `net/EspUdpDatagramIo` (over `general/net` POSIX sockets) |
| playlist, volume clamp, play/pause, voice feedback | `core/app/MediaController` | `hardware.cpp` + drivers |
| button debounce / short / long press | `general/input/ButtonStateMachine` | `drivers/button` |
| directory scan + extension filter | `general/fs/DirectoryLister` | `drivers/bsp` |
| path join, stdio read/write | `general/io/Path`, `general/io/StdioFileStore` | `adapters/storage/EspFileStore` |
| random-access file ops (offset/append/shift/truncate) | `general/io/RandomAccessFile` | `storage/file_manager` |
| MAC / device-id formatting | `general/util/format.hpp` | `adapters/device/EspDeviceInfo` |
| session wiring / conv id | `core/app/session_factory` + `Network::init` | `AppContext::create` |
| audio frame sizing | `general/audio/frame_math.hpp` | `drivers/audio_capture` |

## Layer rules (enforced)

`tools/check_layers.sh` (grep + include gate):

* `core/` never mentions ESP-IDF, FreeRTOS, NVS, WiFi or PSA.
* `core/` never includes `app_context.hpp`, `esp_hardware/` or `adapters/`.
* `general/` never mentions ESP-IDF, FreeRTOS, NVS or WiFi. PSA/Opus/KCP are
  allowed: they are portable libraries, not ESP-IDF headers.
* `general/` never includes `interface.hpp`, `core/` or `esp_hardware/`.
* `esp_hardware/` never includes `core/`; it may use `interface.hpp`, `general/`
  and ESP-IDF.
* `main/` (`app_context`, `main.cpp`) is the composition root and may include
  everything.

`tools/host_check.sh` (host compiler):

* `core/app` (`media_controller`, `net_manager`, `session_factory`),
  `core/audio`, `core/session` compile with `-fsyntax-only` on the host.
* `general/` pure sources + `general/kcp/ikcp.c` (C99) compile on the host.
* `general/crypto` and `general/opus` need PSA/libopus headers, so they are
  validated by the IDF build.

Run both after every change. Run `idf.py reconfigure` after adding a source
directory (the CMake glob has no `CONFIGURE_DEPENDS`).

## Domain core (`main/core`)

* `session/SessionManager` - handshake / liveness / reconnect / encryption:
  ```
  IDLE --link up--> HANDSHAKING(WAIT_ACK) --peer key--> ESTABLISHED
  ESTABLISHED --no RX for 8s--> IDLE (reset transport => fresh KCP sequence numbers)
  any --link down--> IDLE (reset transport)
  ```
  * handshake retries forever with backoff 600ms -> 3s (server may boot late)
  * every reset reopens the transport with a FRESH conversation id (random.u32)
    so a restarted/alive server and the client never desync KCP sequences
  * KCP dead_link (retransmission limit) triggers an early reset instead of
    waiting for the full 8 s server timeout
  * transport.open() is attempted only while the link is up (no getaddrinfo
    stalls when offline)
  * inbound messages are drained in a loop per tick; KCP datagrams drained
    per poll with a 32-datagram bound
  * outbound data queued (max 128) and dropped when not established
    (real-time audio: dropping is better than buffering)
* `audio/AudioPipeline` - capture -> encode -> emit `(compressed, timestampMs)`.

## Application (`main/core/app`)

* `prog.cpp` - device registration + server message dispatch. Receives
  `core::IAppServices&` from the platform layer; no ESP-IDF/root includes.
  Registration is sent on (re)connect transitions detected via the session
  epoch, then as a 3 s keepalive; the RX queue is drained per loop iteration;
  `IFirmwareUpdater::markAppValid()` runs once on the first successful query.
* `audio_service.cpp` - start/stop the capture task through `ITaskRunner`;
  record acks use `IClock::nowUnixMs()`.
* `net_manager.cpp` - builds the transport/session via `ITransportFactory` +
  `SessionManager`, then runs the worker task through `ITaskRunner`.

## Hardware (`main/esp_hardware`)

* `hardware.cpp` - board bring-up: BSP/I2S, IO expander and SD card.
  `main.cpp` includes it through the `HARDWARE_HEADER` macro.
* `adapters/` - `KcpUdpTransport`, `EspWifiLink`, `EspCrypto`, `EspClock`,
  `EspRandom`, `EspLogger`, `EspTaskRunner`, `EspDeviceInfo`,
  `EspNvsStorage`, `EspFileStore`, `EspFirmwareUpdater`,
  `EspI2sAudioSource`, `OpusEncoder`, `EspMediaPlayer`, `EspIndicator`,
  `EspButtonInput`, `EspSpeechInput`, `EspSdPlaylist`.
* `drivers/`, `wifi/`, `net/`, `storage/` - the ESP-IDF implementation
  details behind those adapters.

## Composition root

`main/app_context.{hpp,cpp}` is a **plain class** owned by `main.cpp`. It
constructs every concrete adapter and exposes `core::IAppServices& services()`.
There is no `app::context()` singleton: `main.cpp` passes
`&context.services()` to `app_run_loop`, so `core/app` receives ports by
reference and never sees the composition root.

**Swapping hardware** means replacing `main/esp_hardware/` (or pointing
`HARDWARE_HEADER` at another folder). `main/core/` and `main/general/` stay
untouched; `main/main.cpp` only calls `hardware::boot(context.audio_config)`,
starts the media controller and the application task.

## Crypto backend

`general/crypto` holds the AES-256-GCM / ECDH protocol logic on top of PSA
(mbedTLS is a portable library, so that code stays general).
`encryption::CryptoBackend` is the only platform seam:
`esp_hardware/adapters/crypto/EspCrypto.hpp` implements `EspCryptoBackend`
(`psa_crypto_init()` + `psa_generate_random()`), so a different MCU only
replaces that thin wrapper.

## Migration status

- [x] Phase 0/1: ports, SessionManager, KcpUdpTransport, liveness + KCP reset.
- [x] Phase 2: `IDeviceInfo` + handshake gated on got-IP.
- [x] Phase 3: `IAudioSource` / `IAudioEncoder` + `AudioPipeline`.
- [x] Phase 4: `IStorage` / `IFileStore` + adapters.
- [x] Phase 5: `AppContext` composition root; `ITaskRunner`; `IFirmwareUpdater`.
- [x] Four-folder reorg: `interface.hpp` / `core/` / `general/` / `esp_hardware/`.
- [x] Gates: `tools/check_layers.sh` + `tools/host_check.sh`.
- [x] General logic rescued from `esp_hardware/` (KCP session, media controller,
      button state machine, directory lister, file I/O, formatting,
      session factory, audio frame math).
- [x] Dead code removed: duplicate protocols, `core/ports.hpp` shim, test blocks.
- [x] Deleted now-unused hardware leftovers (`storage/nvs`, `KcpClient`,
      duplicate `FileManager` paths, `Folder_retrieval`, commented duplicates).
- [x] Dependency directions enforced: `core/` → interface/general only;
      `esp_hardware/` → interface/general only; `general/` is a leaf.
- [x] `AppContext` is a plain class in `main/`; `app::context()` singleton removed.
- [x] Audio config + frame math moved to the contract / `general/audio/`.
- [x] Crypto backend injected (`CryptoBackend` / `EspCryptoBackend`).
- [x] Robustness pass: RGB boot crash, UDP FD leak, keepalive instead of
      registration spam, fresh conv per reset, dead-link early reset,
      NetState/MediaController mutexes, graceful task shutdown, ECDH key
      ownership (`EcdhHandshake`), OTA `markAppValid()`, Opus scratch buffers,
      atomic `shiftBytes()` replace.
- [x] Second robustness pass: tick-clock `receive(now)` + underflow guard,
      WaitAck timeout with key retention, task-handle publication races,
      requestId-echoing acks after real init, 0-based frame timestamps,
      correct AudioPacket codec/rate/channels, SNTP, connected UDP,
      legacy/new file-list decoding, proto CMake excludes lib/main.cpp.
- [ ] Next: 4G adapter implementing `ILink`/`ITransport`;
      `IFirmwareUpdater::applyFromUrl()` streaming.

## Portability snapshot

| Layer | survives an MCU swap |
|---|---|
| `components/proto` | 100% |
| `core/` (session, pipeline, app) | 100% |
| `general/` (ikcp, crypto, opus) | ~95% (PSA/libopus backends) |
| `esp_hardware/` (WiFi, socket, I2S, NVS, clock) | 0% (isolated, mechanical) |

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: tools/check_layers.sh
--------------------------------------------------------------
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Layer guard for the Ports & Adapters layout.
#
#   interface.hpp  the contract: core/ + esp_hardware/ + main/ may include it
#   core/          pure C++ application (no ESP-IDF / FreeRTOS / WiFi / NVS)
#   general/       portable helpers (KCP, crypto, codecs) - leaf; no core/hardware deps
#   esp_hardware/  the only place allowed to touch ESP-IDF; never includes core/
#   main/          composition root (app_context + main.cpp); may include everything
#
# Run from anywhere; exits non-zero on violations.

set -u
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."

fail=0
# core/app must be fully portable (PSA included); general may use portable
# libraries such as PSA/Opus/KCP, but never ESP-IDF or FreeRTOS.
PATTERN_APP='esp_[a-z]|freertos/|driver/|nvs_|nvs\.h|wifi::|psa/|esp_ota'
PATTERN_GENERAL='esp_[a-z]|freertos/|driver/|nvs_|nvs\.h|wifi::|esp_ota'

check_pure() {
    local dir="$1" label="$2" pattern="$3"
    [ -d "$dir" ] || return 0

    local hits
    hits=$(grep -rnE "$pattern" "$dir" \
               --include='*.cpp' --include='*.hpp' --include='*.h' 2>/dev/null \
           | grep -vE '^[^:]+:[0-9]+:[[:space:]]*(//|\*|/\*)' || true)

    if [ -n "$hits" ]; then
        echo "[FAIL] $label has platform leaks:"
        echo "$hits" | sed 's/^/       /'
        fail=1
    else
        echo "[ok]   $label"
    fi
}

check_pure main/core    "core     (pure C++)"  "$PATTERN_APP"
check_pure main/general "general  (portable)"  "$PATTERN_GENERAL"

# general/ is a leaf: it must never depend on the app core or the hardware layer.
if [ -d main/general ]; then
    hits=$(grep -rnE '#include[[:space:]]*"(interface\.hpp|core/|esp_hardware/)' main/general \
               --include='*.cpp' --include='*.hpp' --include='*.h' 2>/dev/null || true)
    if [ -n "$hits" ]; then
        echo "[FAIL] general depends on interface/core/esp_hardware:"
        echo "$hits" | sed 's/^/       /'
        fail=1
    fi
fi

# core/ must never include the composition root or the hardware layer.
hits=$(grep -rnE '#include[[:space:]]*"(app_context\.hpp|esp_hardware/|adapters/)' main/core \
           --include='*.cpp' --include='*.hpp' --include='*.h' 2>/dev/null || true)
if [ -n "$hits" ]; then
    echo "[FAIL] core depends on the composition root / hardware layer:"
    echo "$hits" | sed 's/^/       /'
    fail=1
else
    echo "[ok]   core     (no composition/hardware includes)"
fi

# esp_hardware/ may use interface.hpp + general/ + ESP-IDF only; never core/.
hits=$(grep -rnE '#include[[:space:]]*"core/' main/esp_hardware \
           --include='*.cpp' --include='*.hpp' --include='*.h' 2>/dev/null || true)
if [ -n "$hits" ]; then
    echo "[FAIL] esp_hardware depends on core/ (use interface.hpp or general/):"
    echo "$hits" | sed 's/^/       /'
    fail=1
else
    echo "[ok]   esp_hardware (no core/ includes)"
fi

if [ "$fail" -eq 0 ]; then
    echo "[ok]   layer rules satisfied"
fi
exit "$fail"

--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: tools/host_check.sh
--------------------------------------------------------------
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Proves that core/ compiles without ESP-IDF, and that the portable KCP core
# is valid C.
#
# general/crypto and general/opus need external headers (PSA / libopus), so they
# are validated by the IDF build; check_layers.sh keeps them free of ESP-IDF.

set -u
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."

CXX=${CXX:-g++}
CC=${CC:-gcc}
fail=0

PURE_SOURCES="main/core/app/media_controller.cpp main/core/app/net_manager.cpp main/core/app/session_factory.cpp main/esp_hardware/net/esp_udp_datagram_io.cpp"

for f in $PURE_SOURCES $(find main/core/audio main/core/session main/general/io main/general/fs main/general/input main/general/kcp -name '*.cpp' | sort); do
    if "$CXX" -std=c++17 -Imain -fsyntax-only "$f" 2>/tmp/host_check.err; then
        echo "[ok]   host compile: $f"
    else
        echo "[FAIL] host compile: $f"
        sed 's/^/       /' /tmp/host_check.err
        fail=1
    fi
done

if [ -f main/general/kcp/ikcp.c ]; then
    if "$CC" -std=c99 -Imain/general/kcp -fsyntax-only main/general/kcp/ikcp.c 2>/tmp/host_check.err; then
        echo "[ok]   host compile: main/general/kcp/ikcp.c"
    else
        echo "[FAIL] host compile: main/general/kcp/ikcp.c"
        sed 's/^/       /' /tmp/host_check.err
        fail=1
    fi
fi

[ "$fail" -eq 0 ] && echo "[ok]   core/ is portable"
exit "$fail"


--------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: python/output_folder.py
--------------------------------------------------------------

#!/usr/bin/env python3

import os
from pathlib import Path

SOURCE_EXTENSIONS = {
    ".cpp", ".hpp", ".h", ".cc", ".c",
    ".cxx", ".hxx", ".hh"
}

SPECIAL_FILES = {
    "CMakeLists.txt",
    "README.md",
    "Makefile"
}

EXCLUDED_DIRS = {
    ".git",
    ".vscode",
    ".idea",
    "__pycache__",
    "build",
    "dist",
    "venv",
    ".venv",
    "node_modules",
}


def should_include(path):
    return (
        path.suffix.lower() in SOURCE_EXTENSIONS
        or path.name in SPECIAL_FILES
    )


def get_files(root):
    files = []

    for current, dirs, filenames in os.walk(root):
        # Prevent entering excluded directories
        dirs[:] = sorted(
            d for d in dirs if d not in EXCLUDED_DIRS
        )

        for filename in sorted(filenames):
            path = Path(current) / filename

            if should_include(path):
                files.append(path)

    return sorted(files)


def get_tree(root, files):
    lines = [root.name + "/"]

    included_dirs = set()
    included_files = set(files)

    for file in files:
        parent = file.parent
        while parent != root:
            included_dirs.add(parent)
            parent = parent.parent

    all_paths = sorted(
        included_dirs | included_files,
        key=lambda p: (str(p.relative_to(root)).lower(),
                       p.is_file())
    )

    for path in all_paths:
        relative = path.relative_to(root)
        depth = len(relative.parts)
        name = relative.name

        suffix = "/" if path.is_dir() else ""
        lines.append("    " * depth + name + suffix)

    return "\n".join(lines)


def get_includes(files, root):
    result = []

    for file in files:
        try:
            content = file.read_text(
                encoding="utf-8",
                errors="replace"
            )
        except OSError:
            continue

        for line_number, line in enumerate(
            content.splitlines(), 1
        ):
            stripped = line.strip()

            if stripped.startswith("#include"):
                relative = file.relative_to(root)
                result.append(
                    f"{relative}:{line_number}: {stripped}"
                )

    return result


def export_project(root):
    files = get_files(root)

    output = root / "project_context.txt"

    with output.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as out:

        out.write("=" * 80 + "\n")
        out.write("C++ PROJECT CONTEXT\n")
        out.write("=" * 80 + "\n\n")

        # 1. Project structure
        out.write("PROJECT STRUCTURE\n")
        out.write("-" * 80 + "\n")
        out.write(get_tree(root, files))
        out.write("\n\n")

        # 2. Include relationships
        out.write("INCLUDE RELATIONSHIPS\n")
        out.write("-" * 80 + "\n")

        includes = get_includes(files, root)

        if includes:
            out.write("\n".join(includes))
        else:
            out.write("No include directives found.")

        out.write("\n\n")

        # 3. File contents
        out.write("SOURCE FILE CONTENTS\n")
        out.write("=" * 80 + "\n")

        for file in files:
            relative = file.relative_to(root)

            out.write("\n\n")
            out.write("=" * 80 + "\n")
            out.write(f"FILE: {relative}\n")
            out.write("=" * 80 + "\n\n")

            try:
                content = file.read_text(
                    encoding="utf-8",
                    errors="replace"
                )
                out.write(content)
            except OSError as error:
                out.write(f"[ERROR READING FILE: {error}]")

            out.write("\n")

    print("\nExport completed!")
    print(f"Files collected: {len(files)}")
    print(f"Output: {output}")


def main():
    print("================================")
    print("       C++ PROJECT EXPORTER")
    print("================================")

    folder = input("\nEnter project folder path: ").strip()
    folder = folder.strip('"').strip("'")

    root = Path(folder).expanduser().resolve()

    if not root.exists():
        print("Error: Folder does not exist.")
        return

    if not root.is_dir():
        print("Error: Path is not a directory.")
        return

    export_project(root)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
